## Part 71 — The Young Astronomer's Dream

Dr. Wang's research **assistant**（助手） Shen Yu was more than just a helper — he was a brilliant young scientist. They **associate**（使联合） with leading researchers from the national astronomy **association**（协会）. Most people **assume**（假定） that teenage interns cannot contribute much, but that **assumption**（假定） was wrong. Shen Yu's ideas would **astonish**（使惊讶） even senior professors. Ever since he was a child, he had wanted to become an **astronaut**（宇航员）. His hero was a famous **astronomer**（天文学家） who discovered three comets, and he dreamed of studying **astronomy**（天文学） at university. Shen Yu could spend hours gazing **at**（在） the stars through the observatory telescope. He was also a talented **athlete**（运动员） — his physical fitness would serve him well in space. He could explain the structure of an **atom**（原子） as easily as he could run a marathon. One day, he would **attach**（附上） his name to a major discovery. The idea of an asteroid **attack**（攻击） on Earth fascinated him — not out of fear, but scientific curiosity. He was determined to **attain**（达到） his goals, no matter how difficult. Every **attempt**（尝试） brought him closer. He chose to **attend**（参加） a prestigious summer program, paying close **attention**（注意） to every lecture. His positive **attitude**（态度） inspired those around him. The night sky seemed to **attract**（吸引） him with an irresistible force.

王博士的研究助手沈宇不仅仅是一个帮手——他是一位才华横溢的年轻科学家。他们与国家天文学协会的顶尖研究人员交往。大多数人假定青少年实习生不能有多大贡献，但这个假定是错误的。沈宇的想法会让资深教授都感到惊讶。从他孩提时代起，他就想成为一名宇航员。他的偶像是一位发现了三颗彗星的著名天文学家，他梦想在大学里学习天文学。沈宇可以在天文望远镜旁对着星空凝视数小时。他也是一名有天赋的运动员——他的体能将为太空任务做好准备。他能像跑马拉松一样轻松地解释原子结构。有一天，他会将自己的名字附在一项重大发现上。小行星撞击地球的想法令他着迷——不是出于恐惧，而是出于科学的好奇心。他决心达到目标，无论多么困难。每一次尝试都让他离目标更近。他选择参加一个享有盛誉的暑期项目，对每一堂课都聚精会神。他积极的态度感染了身边的人。夜空似乎以一股不可抗拒的力量吸引着他。

---

## Part 72 — Autumn on Maple Avenue

The ancient gardens of Suzhou held an irresistible **attraction**（吸引） for Fang Ning. There was something deeply **attractive**（吸引人的） about the way autumn light fell on the old pavilions. As a writer, she was her own **audience**（听众） — at least until she found a publisher. Her **aunt**（阿姨） had left her a small house in the old town, filled with **authentic**（可靠的） Qing dynasty furniture. Fang Ning wanted to become a serious **author**（作家） whose words carried **authority**（权力）. The house had an old **automatic**（自动的） clock that still chimed every hour. This place felt **autonomous**（自治的） — a world apart from the modern city. **autumn**（秋天） was when she felt most creative. A tea shop — the only quiet place **available**（可获得的） near the busy **avenue**（大街） — became her daily writing spot. On **average**（平均）, she wrote two thousand words a day. She had learned to **avoid**（避免） distractions — no phone, no internet. When inspiration struck, she would lie **awake**（醒着的） until dawn, scribbling in her notebook. Last month, she won an **award**（奖品） for her short story. She was keenly **aware**（知道的） that discipline mattered more than talent. Walking **away**（离去） from her desk at sunset, she watched the sky turn colors. The view was truly **awesome**（引起敬畏的） — and tomorrow, she would capture it in words.

苏州古老的园林对方宁有着不可抗拒的吸引力。秋日的阳光洒在古老的亭台上，有着某种深邃而吸引人的美。作为一名作家，她是自己的听众——至少在找到出版社之前是这样。她的阿姨在老城区给她留下了一座小房子，里面摆满了可靠的清代家具。方宁想成为一名严肃的作家，让文字带有权威。房子里有一个古老的自动钟，每小时仍会报时。这个地方感觉是自治的——一个独立于现代城市之外的世界。秋天是她最有创造力的时候。附近繁忙大街上唯一安静的可获得去处——一家茶馆——成了她每日写作的地方。平均来说，她每天写两千字。她学会了避免干扰——没有手机，没有网络。当灵感来临时，她会醒着直到破晓，在本子上潦草地写。上个月，她的短篇小说获得了一个奖项。她深知纪律比天赋更重要。日落时从书桌旁离去，她看着天空变换色彩。景象真正令人敬畏——而明天，她会用文字将它捕捉下来。

---

## Part 73 — A Weekend at the Family Bakery

The weather was **awful**（可怕的） — rain poured down in sheets. But inside the cozy house, Little Dou was safe in his mother's arms, a happy **baby**（婴孩）. His **bachelor**（单身汉） uncle had come to visit, sitting at the **back**（后面的） of the room telling stories. With a **background**（背景） in food science, Uncle Chen knew all about the family **bakery**（面包店）. He never looked **backward**（向后地） — only forward, always planning the next recipe. The smell of frying **bacon**（熏猪肉） filled the kitchen. Uncle Chen explained how **bacterium**（细菌） could spoil food if not handled properly — his food safety lectures were never **bad**（坏的）. Later, the family played **badminton**（羽毛球运动） in the yard once the rain stopped. Little Dou crawled toward a **bag**（袋子） of flour and giggled. The family's **baggage**（行李） from the trip was still unpacked. At the bakery, a new batch of bread was cooling on the rack. Little Dou bounced a colorful **ball**（球） on the floor. His sister practiced **ballet**（芭蕾舞） in the living room, twirling like a spinning top. A red **balloon**（气球） floated near the ceiling. Outside, the **bamboo**（竹子） in the garden swayed in the wind. The government had recently imposed a **ban**（禁令） on plastic packaging, so the bakery used paper bags instead. Little Dou reached for a **banana**（香蕉） and mashed it in his tiny hands, making everyone laugh.

天气很糟糕——瓢泼大雨倾盆而下。但在温暖的屋子里，小豆子在母亲怀中很安全，他是一个快乐的婴孩。他的单身汉叔叔来看望他们，坐在房间后面讲着故事。有着食品科学背景的陈叔叔对家里的面包店了如指掌。他从不向后看——只向前看，总是在规划下一个配方。煎熏猪肉的香味充满了厨房。陈叔叔解释如果不恰当处理细菌会如何让食物变质——他的食品安全讲座从不枯燥。后来，雨停了，一家人在院子里打羽毛球运动。小豆子爬向一袋面粉，咯咯笑着。一家人旅途中的行李还没有拆包。在面包店里，一批新面包正在架子上冷却。小豆子在地上拍着一个彩色的球。他的姐姐在客厅里练芭蕾舞，像陀螺一样旋转。一只红色气球飘在天花板附近。屋外，园子里的竹子在风中摇曳。政府最近颁布了塑料包装禁令，所以面包店改用纸袋。小豆子伸手去抓香蕉，用小手把它捏得稀烂，逗得大家哈哈大笑。

---

## Part 74 — A Sunny Day in the Old Neighborhood

The morning began with the sound of a **band**（乐队） practicing in the park. Grandpa Li went to the **bank**（银行） first, then stopped at his favorite breakfast **bar**（吧台）. The whole neighborhood smelled of **barbecue**（烤肉） — someone was already firing up the grill. He visited the **barber**（理发师） at the old **barbershop**（理发店）, where conversations were always a **bargain**（交易） of stories and laughter. A neighbor's dog let out a cheerful **bark**（吠声） as children ran past. The language **barrier**（障碍） between the old residents and the young newcomers was slowly melting away. At the park's **base**（底部）, near the fountain, a group of kids played **baseball**（棒球）. Grandpa Li walked past the **basement**（地下室） entrance of the community center and remembered the **basic**（基本原理） joy of simple living. A woman washed vegetables in a ceramic **basin**（盆） outside her door. Another carried a woven **basket**（篮子） full of freshly picked greens. As dusk fell, a **bat**（蝙蝠） swooped through the evening sky. Grandpa Li returned home, ready for a warm **bath**（沐浴）. He liked to **bathe**（沐浴） slowly, letting the hot water ease his old bones, thinking about how much he loved this small corner of the world.

早晨在公园里一支乐队练习的声音中开始。李爷爷先去了银行，然后在他最喜欢的早餐吧台停留。整个街区弥漫着烤肉的香气——有人已经在点燃烤架了。他去找老理发店里的理发师，那里的对话总是故事和笑声的一笔美好交易。邻居的狗在孩子们跑过时发出欢快的吠声。老住户和年轻新来者之间的语言障碍正在慢慢融化。在公园底部靠近喷泉的地方，一群孩子在打棒球。李爷爷走过社区中心的地下室入口，想起了简单生活的基本原理带来的快乐。一个女人在门外的陶瓷盆里洗菜。另一个人提着一只装满新鲜蔬菜的编织篮子。暮色降临，一只蝙蝠掠过夜空。李爷爷回到家，准备好好沐浴一番。他喜欢慢慢沐浴，让热水舒缓他年迈的骨头，想着他多么热爱这个世界的小角落。

---

## Part 75 — Chapter 1: Morning at the Seaside Inn

Tom woke up in his cozy **bedroom**（卧室） and hurried to the **bathroom**（浴室）. The **bathtub**（浴缸） filled slowly, and he was glad the water heater's **battery**（电池） still worked. After his bath, he looked in the mirror at his messy **beard**（胡须） and decided to shave later. He cooked **bean**（豆子） soup for breakfast and fried some fresh **beef**（牛肉）. The morning was **beautiful**（美丽的）, and Tom smiled **because**（因为） he felt truly alive. He wanted to **become**（变成） a great chef someday, just like his grandmother. Back upstairs, he tidied his **bed**（床） and folded the soft **beddings**（寝具） neatly. Through the window, a single **bee**（蜜蜂） buzzed past, and far away he heard the steady **beat**（心跳） of waves against the shore. He gazed at the sparkling **bay**（海湾） and the golden **beach**（海滩） stretching for miles. His grandfather had once fought a fierce **battle**（战役） on this very coast. Nature could be a cruel **beast**（畜生）, but today all was peaceful. That evening, he planned to enjoy a cold glass of **beer**（啤酒） while watching the sunset over the water.

汤姆在他舒适的卧室里醒来，匆匆走进浴室。浴缸里的水慢慢注满，他很庆幸热水器的电池还能正常工作。洗过澡后，他看着镜子里凌乱的胡须，决定晚点再刮。他煮了豆子汤当早餐，还煎了一些新鲜的牛肉。早晨美极了，汤姆笑了，因为他感到自己真正地活着。他想要变成一名伟大的厨师，就像他的祖母那样。回到楼上，他整理了床铺，把柔软的寝具叠得整整齐齐。窗外，一只蜜蜂嗡嗡飞过，远处传来海浪有节奏的拍打声。他凝视着波光粼粼的海湾和绵延数英里的金色海滩。他的祖父曾在这片海岸上打过一场激烈的战役。大自然可以是一头残忍的畜生，但今天一切都平静安详。那天傍晚，他打算一边享用一杯冰凉的啤酒，一边看着落日沉入海面。

---

## Part 76 — Chapter 2: The Park Encounter

**before**（在...之前） the sun rose, Lisa had already dressed and gone to the park. She stopped to **beg**（乞求） a stranger to help her find her lost dog. Her search would **begin**（开始） near the old fountain. She was acting on **behalf**（利益） of her elderly neighbour, whose little dog had run off. Lisa understood that the dog's **behaviour**（行为） was often unpredictable. She looked **behind**（在后面） every bush and under every bench, her heart pounding. Every **being**（存在） in the park seemed to watch her search. She held a firm **belief**（信念） that the dog would be found. Suddenly, a distant **bell**（铃） rang from the church tower. Her **belly**（腹部） rumbled with hunger, but she pushed on. A stream flowed **below**（在下面） a small wooden bridge. She tightened her **belt**（带子） and sat on a cold **bench**（长椅子） to rest. She could see a path **bend**（变弯曲） around the old oak tree. **beneath**（在...下方） the tree, she spotted something small and white. Exercise was **beneficial**（有益的） for the dog, but not when it ran away. The **benefit**（利益） of an early start was finally paying off. With **bent**（弯曲的） knees, she knelt down and found the dog sleeping peacefully. She sat **beside**（在旁边） the little creature and gently stroked its head. **besides**（除此之外）, she had also made a new friend in the park that morning.

在太阳升起之前，丽莎已经穿好衣服去了公园。她停下来乞求一个陌生人帮助她寻找走失的狗。她的搜索将从那口古老的喷泉附近开始。她是在代表年迈邻居的利益——邻居的小狗跑丢了。丽莎明白，这只狗的行为常常不可预测。她查看了每一丛灌木的后面，每一张长椅底下，心怦怦直跳。公园里的每一个存在似乎都在注视着她的搜寻。她抱着坚定的信念，一定会找到狗。突然，远处教堂的钟声响了起来。她的腹部因为饥饿咕咕作响，但她继续坚持。一条小溪在一座小木桥下面流过。她勒紧了腰带，坐在一张冰冷的长椅子上休息。她可以看到小路在老橡树那里变弯曲。在橡树下方，她发现了一小团白色的东西。锻炼对狗是有益的，但跑丢的时候可就不是了。早起的利益终于得到了回报。她屈下弯曲的膝盖，跪下来，发现狗正睡得安详。她坐在小家伙的旁边，轻轻地抚摸它的头。除此之外，那天早晨她在公园还交到了一位新朋友。

## Part 77 — Chapter 3: The Science Fair

David could never **betray**（出卖） his teammates, so he worked hard on their project. **between**（在...之间） chemistry and physics, he preferred biology by far. His ambition went **beyond**（超过） simply winning a prize — he wanted to change the world. Every morning, he rode his **bicycle**（自行车） to the university library. The team made a **bid**（出价） to secure funding for their research. It was a **big**（大的） opportunity, and they could not afford to fail. The lab received a **bill**（帐单） for the equipment, but their professor covered it. They celebrated with a game of **bingo**（宾戈游戏） after a long week of experiments. David was fascinated by **biochemistry**（生物化学） and dreamed of curing diseases. He had just finished reading a **biography**（传记） of a Nobel Prize winner. **biology**（生物学） was not just a subject to him — it was a calling. A small **bird**（鸟） perched on the window ledge, as if watching them work. The **birth**（出生） of a new idea excited him more than anything. His **birthplace**（诞生地） was a tiny village, yet here he was in a top lab. He nibbled a **biscuit**（饼干） while reviewing data. The lab director was as stern as a **bishop**（主教） but secretly kind. Every **bit**（少量） of data was carefully recorded. A mosquito tried to **bite**（咬） his arm, but he swatted it away. The **bitter**（苦的） taste of failure once taught him resilience. Outside, the sky turned **black**（黑色） as a storm approached.

大卫永远不会背叛他的队友，所以他努力推进他们的项目。在化学和物理之间，他远远更喜欢生物学。他的志向超过仅仅赢得一个奖项——他想要改变世界。每天早晨，他都骑着自行车去大学图书馆。团队发出了一个出价，为他们的研究争取资金。这是一个大的机会，他们不能失败。实验室收到了一张设备的帐单，但教授帮他们支付了。在漫长的一周实验后，他们玩了一把宾戈游戏来庆祝。大卫对生物化学深深着迷，梦想着治愈疾病。他刚读完一本诺贝尔奖得主的传记。生物学对他来说不只是一门学科——而是一种使命。一只小鸟停在窗台上，仿佛在看他们工作。一个新想法的诞生比任何事情都更让他兴奋。他的诞生地是一个小村庄，然而此刻他却在顶尖的实验室里。他一边吃着饼干，一边审阅数据。实验室主任像主教一样严肃，但私下里很和蔼。每一点数据都被仔细记录下来。一只蚊子试图咬他的手臂，但他把它拍走了。失败的苦涩滋味曾教会他坚韧。窗外，天空变成了黑色，暴风雨即将来临。

## Part 78 — Chapter 4: The Classroom Mystery

Mr. Zhang walked to the **blackboard**（黑板） and began writing. He refused to **blame**（责备） anyone for the broken window. A **blank**（空白的） sheet of paper lay on each student's desk. Lucy saw a small cut on her finger and tried not to let it **bleed**（流血）. The teacher would always **bless**（祝福） his students before every exam. Jack was not **blind**（瞎的） to the problem — he simply needed time to think. Outside the classroom window, the entire **block**（街区） was covered in snow. The story they were reading was full of **blood**（血） and drama. Sarah wore a white **blouse**（宽松的上衣） that made her look very professional. The wind began to **blow**（吹） fiercely, rattling the windows. She took out her **blue**（蓝色的） notebook and started writing. The teacher drew a diagram on the **board**（木板） to explain the concept. He compared a cell to a tiny **boat**（船） floating in the ocean of the **body**（身体）. Water began to **boil**（煮沸） in the science experiment at the back of the room. A sudden loud noise, like a **bomb**（炸弹）, came from the chemistry lab next door. The strong **bond**（结合） between the students was forged through years of shared struggle. The biology teacher held up a **bone**（骨头） model to illustrate the skeleton. As a **bonus**（奖金）, the headmaster rewarded the class with extra break time. At the end of the lesson, everyone closed their **book**（书） and breathed a sigh of relief.

张老师走到黑板前开始写字。他拒绝为打破的窗户责备任何人。每个学生的课桌上都放着一张空白的纸。露西看到手指上有个小伤口，尽量不让它流血。老师总是在每次考试前祝福他的学生。杰克对问题并不瞎，他只是需要时间来思考。教室窗外，整个街区都被大雪覆盖了。他们正在读的故事充满了血与戏剧性。莎拉穿着一件白色的宽松上衣，看起来非常专业。风开始猛烈地吹，窗户格格作响。她拿出蓝色的笔记本，开始写字。老师在一块木板上画了图解来解释概念。他把细胞比作一艘小船，漂浮在身体的海洋中。教室后面，科学实验中的水开始沸腾。突然一声巨响，像炸弹一样，从隔壁化学实验室传来。学生们之间牢固的结合，是在多年共同奋斗中铸造的。生物老师举起一根骨头模型来说明骨骼结构。作为奖金，校长奖励了这个班级额外的休息时间。下课时，每个人都合上了书，松了一口气。

## Part 79 — Chapter 5: The Startup Dream

A new economic **boom**（繁荣） was sweeping through the coastal city. Mark pulled on his leather **boot**（长靴） and headed to the trade fair. His company had a small **booth**（摊位） near the entrance. He sold handmade leather goods and lived near the **border**（边缘） of the city. He felt **bored**（无聊的） with his old job, so he started his own business. The paperwork was **boring**（烦人的）, but he pushed through it. He was **born**（天生的） to be an entrepreneur — it ran in his family. He had to **borrow**（借） money from the bank to fund his workshop. His former **boss**（老板） had laughed at his dream, but Mark did not give up. Near his shop was a **botanical**（植物学的） garden that inspired his designs. His love for **botany**（植物学） influenced the patterns he carved into leather. **both**（两者的） creativity and hard work were needed to succeed. He did not let small setbacks **bother**（烦扰） him anymore. He carried a **bottle**（瓶子） of water everywhere to stay hydrated. From the **bottom**（底部） of his heart, he believed his products would sell. Business began to **bounce**（反弹） back after a slow first month. He felt **bound**（受约束的） by tradition at first, but then he broke free. The **boundary**（边界） of what he could achieve kept expanding. At the gala, he wore a **bow**（蝴蝶结） tie and received an award. He celebrated by eating from a wooden **bowl**（碗） of noodles at his favourite stall.

一场新的经济繁荣正席卷这座沿海城市。马克穿上皮长靴，走向贸易博览会。他的公司在入口附近有一个小摊位。他销售手工皮具，住在城市的边缘。他对旧工作感到无聊，所以创办了自己的生意。文书工作很烦人，但他坚持了过来。他天生就是企业家——这在他的家族中代代相传。他不得不从银行借钱来资助他的作坊。他以前的老板嘲笑过他的梦想，但马克没有放弃。他店铺附近有一个植物学花园，给了他很多设计灵感。他对植物学的热爱影响了他雕刻在皮革上的纹样。创造力和勤奋两者都是成功所必需的。他不再让小小的挫折来烦扰自己。他到哪都带着一瓶水，保持水分。他从内心深处相信自己的产品会畅销。在第一个月的冷淡之后，生意开始反弹。起初他感到受传统的约束，但后来他挣脱了。他所能达到的边界在不断扩大。在晚会上，他戴着蝴蝶结领结领了一个奖。他在最喜欢的小摊用一只木碗吃面来庆祝。

---

## Part 80 — Chapter 6: The Brave Boy

Tommy loved to go **bowling**（打保龄球） with his father every Saturday. One day, he found a mysterious wooden **box**（盒子） hidden in the attic. His older brother was training in **boxing**（拳击） at the local gym. Tommy was a quiet **boy**（男孩） who loved to read adventure stories. The school planned to **boycott**（联合抵制） the unfair cafeteria prices. He rode his bike so fast that the **brake**（刹车） nearly gave out. His father built a famous **brand**（商标） of organic honey. Tommy tried to be **brave**（勇敢的） when he heard strange noises at night. His **bravery**（勇敢） was tested when a stray cat got stuck in a tree. He ate a slice of **bread**（面包） before heading out to help. He needed a **break**（休息） after climbing halfway up the tall tree. That morning, he had eaten a huge **breakfast**（早餐） of eggs and milk. The rescue felt like a real **breakthrough**（突破） in overcoming his fear of heights. He held the cat tightly against his **breast**（胸部） as he climbed down. By the time his feet touched the ground, he was completely **breathless**（喘不过气的）. His uncle ran a small **brewery**（啤酒厂） on the edge of town. They celebrated the rescue by helping his uncle stack **brick**（砖块） for a new wall. The rescued cat belonged to a lovely **bride**（新娘） who was getting married. She introduced Tommy to her **bridegroom**（新郎）, who shook his hand warmly. It was a **bright**（明亮的） and unforgettable day in Tommy's young life.

汤米喜欢每个星期六和父亲去打保龄球。有一天，他在阁楼上发现了一个神秘的木盒子。他的哥哥在当地的健身房训练拳击。汤米是一个安静的男孩，喜欢读冒险故事。学校计划联合抵制食堂不合理的价格。他骑车太快，刹车差点失灵。他的父亲创了一个有名的有机蜂蜜品牌。夜里听到奇怪的声音时，汤米努力保持勇敢。当一只流浪猫被困在树上时，他的勇敢受到了考验。他吃了一片面包，然后出发去帮忙。爬到树的一半时，他需要休息一下。那天早上，他吃了一顿丰盛的早餐，有鸡蛋和牛奶。这次救援就像一次突破，帮他克服了对高度的恐惧。他下树时把猫紧紧搂在胸前。当他的脚落地时，他已经完全上气不接下气了。他的叔叔在镇子边上开了一家小啤酒厂。他们通过帮叔叔为新墙搬砖块来庆祝这次救援。那只被救的猫属于一位即将结婚的可爱新娘。她把汤米介绍给她的新郎，新郎热情地握了握他的手。那是汤米幼小的生命中一个明亮而难忘的日子。

---

## Part 81 — Chapter 7: The Family Restaurant

Lily wanted to **bring**（带来） her family closer together through food. The old kitchen window was **broken**（坏掉的）, so they replaced it first. She grabbed a **broom**（扫帚） and swept the dusty floor clean. Her younger **brother**（兄弟） helped paint the walls a warm **brown**（褐色）. They decided to serve **brunch**（早午餐） every Sunday from ten to two. They set a tight **budget**（预算） to keep costs under control. A **buffet**（自助餐） style would let customers choose their favourite dishes. Together, they would **build**（建立） not just a restaurant, but a community. The **building**（建筑物） was old, but it had character and charm. A **bunch**（束） of fresh flowers brightened every table. They lived in a small bungalow behind the restaurant. Navigating the bureaucratic paperwork for permits was exhausting. One night, a **burglar**（窃贼） tried to break in, but the alarm scared him off. They decided to **bury**（埋葬） their doubts and give the restaurant everything they had. The local **bus**（公共汽车） stopped right in front, bringing customers from downtown. A wild rose **bush**（矮树丛） bloomed beautifully by the entrance. Their **business**（生意） grew steadily, and they hired more staff. Lily was always **busy**（忙碌的）, **but**（但是） she never complained. They bought meat from a local **butcher**（屠夫） known for his quality. Every dish was finished with a touch of fresh **butter**（黄油）.

莉莉想通过食物把家人凝聚在一起。旧厨房的窗户是坏掉的，所以他们先把它换了。她抓起一把扫帚，把满是灰尘的地面扫干净。她的弟弟帮忙把墙刷成了温暖的褐色。他们决定每个星期天从十点到两点供应早午餐。他们设定了严格的预算来控制成本。自助餐的形式能让顾客选择他们最喜欢的菜。他们将一起建立的不仅仅是一家餐厅，而是一个社区。这栋建筑物很旧，但它有独特的个性和魅力。每张桌子上都有一束鲜花，增添了不少光彩。他们住在餐厅后面的一栋小平房里。应付官僚的许可证文书工作让人精疲力尽。一天夜里，一个窃贼试图闯进来，但警报把他吓跑了。他们决定埋葬自己的疑虑，把一切投入到餐厅中。本地的公共汽车就停在门口，从市中心拉来客人。入口旁的一丛野玫瑰花开得很美。他们的生意稳步增长，还多雇了员工。莉莉总是很忙碌，但是她从不抱怨。他们从一个以品质著称的本地屠夫那里买肉。每道菜最后都点缀上一点新鲜的黄油。

---

## Part 82 — Chapter 8: A Day at the Market

A beautiful **butterfly**（蝴蝶） landed on the flower stall at the morning market. Grandma Wang fastened the last **button**（纽扣） on her coat and headed out. She planned to **buy**（买） fresh vegetables for the week. She passed **by**（经过） the bakery and waved **bye**（再见） to the baker. A yellow **cab**（出租车） honked as it squeezed through the narrow street. She picked up a fresh **cabbage**（卷心菜） and put it in her basket. Then she stopped at a cozy **cafe**（咖啡馆） for a cup of tea. Nearby, a bird in a **cage**（笼） sang a cheerful melody. She bought a small **cake**（蛋糕） for her grandson's birthday. She tried to **calculate**（计算） the total cost in her head. She made a phone **call**（呼叫） to her daughter to ask about dinner plans. A merchant from the desert was selling rides on his camel. Some children had set up a little **camp**（露营） tent in the park. A local politician was running a **campaign**（竞选运动） for mayor. A narrow **canal**（运河） ran through the centre of the old market district. She had to **cancel**（取消） her appointment because the market took too long. She donated money to a **cancer**（癌症） research charity box. Each **candidate**（候选人） for the cooking contest prepared a signature dish. Grandma bought some homemade **candy**（糖果） for the children. She put on her favourite **cap**（帽子） as the sun grew stronger.

一只美丽的蝴蝶停在了早市的花摊上。王奶奶扣好外套上最后一颗纽扣，出了门。她打算买够一周用的新鲜蔬菜。她经过面包店，和面包师道了再见。一辆黄色的出租车鸣着喇叭挤过狭窄的街道。她挑了一棵新鲜的卷心菜放进篮子。然后她在一家舒适的咖啡馆停下来喝了杯茶。旁边，笼子里的一只鸟唱着欢快的曲子。她为孙子买了一个小蛋糕过生日。她试图在脑中计算总花费。她打了个电话给女儿，询问晚饭的安排。一个来自沙漠的商人在出租他的骆驼供人骑乘。几个孩子在公园里搭了一个小小的露营帐篷。一位当地的政治人物正在进行市长竞选活动。一条狭窄的运河流经老市场区的中心。她不得不取消她的预约，因为逛市场花了太长时间。她为癌症研究慈善箱捐了款。烹饪比赛的每一个候选人都准备了拿手菜。奶奶给孩子们买了些自家做的糖果。太阳越来越猛，她戴上了她最喜欢的帽子。

---

## Part 83 — Chapter 9: The Carpenter's Workshop

Old Li lived in the **capital**（首都） and ran a tiny woodworking shop. He could craft a tiny **capsule**（胶囊） box no bigger than a fingernail. He had once been a **captain**（船长） on a fishing vessel before retirement. Under each piece, he carved a small **caption**（说明） describing its origin. His old **car**（汽车） was parked out back, hardly ever used anymore. He would use a piece of **carbon**（碳） paper to trace designs onto wood. A handwritten **card**（卡片） hung on the door: 'Closed on Sundays.' He worked with great **care**（小心）, knowing one mistake could ruin days of work. Every student he taught learned to be **careful**（小心的） with sharp tools. A **careless**（粗心的） apprentice once nearly cut off his finger. The old **carpenter**（木匠） had trained dozens of young craftsmen over the years. A rich woman ordered a custom **carpet**（地毯） rack made of rosewood. In the corner stood an old **carriage**（马车） he was restoring for a museum. He was known as a **carrier**（运送者） of a dying traditional art. At lunch, he munched on a **carrot**（胡萝卜） and a piece of bread. He could **carve**（雕刻） the most delicate patterns into any piece of wood. In any **case**（情形）, he never turned away a student who truly wanted to learn. He kept little **cash**（现金） at hand, trusting most customers to pay later. An old **cassette**（盒式磁带） player in the corner played traditional folk music. The entire **cast**（演员阵容） of a local theatre came to him for prop repairs.

老李住在首都，经营着一家小小的木工坊。他能做出比指甲还小的胶囊盒。他退休前曾是一艘渔船的船长。每件作品下面他都刻上简短的说明，描述它的来历。他的旧汽车停在后院，几乎不再开了。他会用复写纸把图案描到木头上。门上挂着一张手写的卡片：周日休息。他工作时极尽小心，知道一个错误可能毁掉数日的成果。他教导的每一个学生都学会了小心使用锋利的工具。一个粗心的学徒曾经差点切断了自己的手指。这些年来，这位老木匠培训了数十名年轻的工匠。一位富太太定制了一个红木的地毯架。角落里放着一辆他正在为博物馆修复的旧马车。他被视为一门濒死传统技艺的运送者。午餐时，他嚼着一根胡萝卜和一块面包。他能在任何一块木头上雕刻出最精美的图案。在任何情形下，他从不拒绝一个真心想学的学生。他手边不存太多现金，相信大多数顾客会随后付款。角落里一台旧的盒式磁带播放器放着传统民乐。本地剧团的全套演员阵容都来找他修理道具。

---

## Part 84 — Chapter 10: The Castle Tour

The ancient **castle**（城堡） stood on a hill overlooking the valley. On a **casual**（偶然的） visit, Amy discovered a hidden passage. A black **cat**（猫） seemed to guide her through the dark corridor. She found a dusty **catalogue**（目录） listing every treasure in the fortress. An earthquake had nearly caused a **catastrophe**（大灾难） here centuries ago. The museum had organised every artifact by **category**（种类）. A small restaurant inside **cater**（提供饮食） to hungry tourists. In the courtyard, a herd of **cattle**（牛） grazed peacefully. She entered a cold **cave**（洞） beneath the eastern tower. The townspeople would **celebrate**（庆祝） the harvest festival here every autumn. The **celebration**（庆典） was the highlight of the entire year. Each prison **cell**（牢房） told a story of former captives. Admission was only one **cent**（分） when the castle first opened to the public. The thermometer showed thirty-eight degrees **centigrade**（摄氏的）. The **central**（中央的） hall featured a magnificent chandelier. The **centre**（中心） of the room was occupied by a giant round table. This castle had stood for over a **century**（世纪）. She was **certain**（确定的） she could hear whispers from the stone walls. At the gift shop, she bought a **certificate**（证书） of her visit. A heavy iron **chain**（锁链） once hung across the drawbridge.

古老的城堡矗立在小山上，俯瞰着山谷。在一次偶然的游览中，艾米发现了一条秘密通道。一只黑猫似乎在引导她穿过黑暗的走廊。她找到了一本布满灰尘的目录，列出了堡垒里的每一件珍宝。几个世纪前，一场地震差点在这里造成大灾难。博物馆按种类把每件文物都整理好了。里面的一家小餐厅为饥饿的游客提供饮食。庭院里，一群牛在安详地吃草。她走进了东塔下面一个阴冷的洞穴。每年秋天，镇上的人都会在这里庆祝丰收节。这个庆典是一年中最重要的盛事。每一间牢房都讲述着昔日囚犯的故事。城堡刚向公众开放时，门票只要一分钱。温度计显示三十八摄氏度。中央大厅里悬挂着一盏壮丽的吊灯。房间的中心是一张巨大的圆桌。这座城堡已经矗立了超过一个世纪。她确定自己听到了石墙中传来的低语。在礼品店里，她买了一张参观证书。一条沉重的铁锁链曾经横挂在吊桥上。

---

## Part 85 — Chapter 11: The Championship Match

Coach Zhao drew a diagram on the **chair**（椅子） with **chalk**（粉笔） dust. The team faced their greatest **challenge**（挑战） yet — the national finals. It would be a **challenging**（挑战的） match, but they were ready. Their star player was a former world **champion**（冠军）. He could **change**（变化） the pace of the game in an instant. Spring weather was changeable— sunny one moment, rainy the next. They crossed the **channel**（海峡） by ferry to reach the stadium. From the stands, the crowd began to **chant**（吟唱） their team's anthem. The first minutes of the game were pure **chaos**（混乱）. This was the most important **chapter**（章节） in the club's history. Every player showed true **character**（品格） under pressure. The captain led a swift **charge**（冲锋） toward the goal. Their coach held up a **chart**（图表） showing the opponent's weaknesses. During halftime, the players **chat**（闲谈） quietly to calm their nerves. Tickets were not **cheap**（便宜的）, but the stadium was packed. A rival player tried to **cheat**（作弊） by faking a foul. The referee decided to **check**（检查） the video replay. Sweat dripped down every player's **cheek**（脸颊）. A roar of **cheer**（欢呼） erupted when their team scored. The coach remained **cheerful**（快活的） even when they fell behind.

赵教练用粉笔灰在一把椅子上画战术图。球队面临着迄今为止最大的挑战——全国总决赛。这将是一场极具挑战的比赛，但他们准备好了。他们的明星球员曾是世界冠军。他能在瞬间变化比赛的节奏。春天的天气变幻莫测——一会儿晴，一会儿雨。他们乘渡轮穿过海峡，到达了体育场。看台上，观众开始吟唱起他们的队歌。比赛的前几分钟完全是混乱。这是俱乐部历史上最重要的章节。每一个球员在压力下都展现出了真正的品格。队长带领球队向球门发起了一次迅猛的冲锋。他们的教练举着一张图表，上面标出了对手的弱点。中场休息时，球员们轻声闲谈，平复紧张的心情。票价并不便宜，但体育场还是座无虚席。一名对方球员试图通过假摔来作弊。裁判决定检查视频回放。汗水从每个球员的脸颊淌下来。当球队进球时，爆发出一阵欢呼声。即使落后时，教练也保持快活的态度。

---

## Part 86 — Chapter 12: The Chef's Kitchen

"**cheers**（干杯）!" the guests shouted, raising their glasses high. Famous **chef**（厨师） Antonio had prepared a feast of local **cheese**（乳酪） and wine. He had once trained as a **chemist**（化学家） before discovering his passion for cooking. His understanding of **chemistry**（化学） helped him create innovative dishes. He took out his **cheque**（支票） book to pay the vegetable supplier. A heavy **chest**（箱子） in the pantry stored rare spices from around the world. He taught his students to **chew**（咀嚼） slowly and savour every flavour. The **chicken**（鸡肉） dish was the most popular item on the menu. The **chief**（主要的） ingredient in his signature sauce was a family secret. As a **child**（孩子）, Antonio had watched his grandmother cook for hours. His **childhood**（孩童时期） memories were filled with the aroma of baking bread. He faced a difficult **choice**（选择） — stay in Italy or open a restaurant in Paris. The restaurant's **choir**（唱诗班） performed every Sunday evening. A diner began to **choke**（窒息） on a piece of food, but a quick waiter saved him. Customers could **choose**（选择） from over fifty different dishes. At every table, there was a pair of elegant **chopsticks**（筷子） for Asian dishes. The whole dining room joined in a beautiful **chorus**（合唱） of 'Happy Birthday'. The restaurant was located inside a converted **church**（教堂）. After dinner, guests enjoyed a fine **cigar**（雪茄） on the terrace.

干杯！客人们高举起酒杯喊道。著名厨师安东尼奥准备了一席本地乳酪与美酒的盛宴。在发现对烹饪的热爱之前，他曾受训成为一名化学家。他对化学的理解帮助他创造出了新颖的菜肴。他拿出支票本付给蔬菜供应商。储藏室里一只沉重的箱子里存放着来自世界各地的珍稀香料。他教导学生要慢慢咀嚼，细细品味每一种味道。那道鸡肉菜肴是菜单上最受欢迎的菜品。他的招牌酱汁中的主要配料是家族机密。小时候，安东尼奥曾看着祖母烹饪好几个小时。他的孩童时期的记忆充满了烤面包的香气。他面临着一个艰难的抉择——留在意大利还是去巴黎开店。餐厅的唱诗班每个周日晚上演出。一位食客被食物噎住了，但一个反应迅速的侍者救了他。顾客可以从五十多道菜中自由选择。每张桌上都备有一双精美的筷子，供亚洲菜肴使用。整个餐厅齐声合唱了生日快乐歌。餐厅坐落在由一座教堂改建的建筑内。晚餐后，客人们在露台上享用着上等的雪茄。

---

## Part 87 — Chapter 13: A Night at the Cinema

Mia lit a **cigarette**（香烟） outside the old **cinema**（电影院）, then quickly put it out. She walked through the revolving doors and entered a perfect **circle**（圆周） of light. The neon signs formed an intricate **circuit**（电路） around the marquee. Fans began to **circulate**（流通） through the lobby, buzzing with excitement. Under no **circumstance**（环境） would she miss the premiere of her favourite director. The film was set in a travelling circus visiting a forgotten **city**（城市）. A brave civilian became the unlikely hero of the story. The audience began to **clap**（鼓掌） as the opening credits rolled. The narrator's voice helped clarify the complex plot. Mia loved films that transcended **class**（阶级） and spoke to everyone. This movie was destined to become a **classic**（经典）. Film scholars would later **classify**（分类） it as a masterpiece of modern cinema. Mia had come with her **classmate**（同班同学） from film school. They had first met in a tiny **classroom**（教室） three years ago. The monster's **claw**（爪） appeared on screen, and the audience gasped. A **clean**（干净的） white light washed over the final scene. Outside, a street **cleaner**（清洁工） was sweeping the pavement. The ticket **clerk**（售票员） was a **clever**（聪明的） young man who loved discussing films. With a single **click**（咔哒声） of the projector, the film came to an end. As the lights came up, Mia felt ready to **climb**（攀登） any mountain to become a filmmaker.

米娅在老电影院外点了一支香烟，随即又把它掐灭了。她穿过旋转门，走进了一圈完美的光晕之中。霓虹灯在电影看板周围形成了精密的电路。影迷们开始在大厅里流动穿梭，兴奋不已。在任何环境下，她都不会错过她最爱的导演的首映式。电影讲述了一个巡回马戏团造访一座被遗忘的城市的故事。一个勇敢的平民成了故事中出人意料的英雄。当开场字幕滚动时，观众们开始鼓掌。叙述者的声音帮助阐明了复杂的情节。米娅喜欢那些跨越阶级、向所有人说话的电影。这部电影注定要成为一部经典之作。影评学者日后会将它分类为现代电影的杰作。米娅是和她的同班同学一起来的。三年前，她们在一间小小的教室里初次相识。怪物的爪子出现在银幕上，观众倒吸了一口气。一道干净的白色光芒冲刷过最后的场景。外面，一位清洁工正在清扫人行道。售票员是一个聪明的年轻人，热爱讨论电影。随着放映机咔哒一声，电影结束了。当灯光亮起时，米娅觉得自己准备好去攀登任何高山来成为电影人了。

---

## Part 88 — Chapter 14: The School Sports Day

Dr. Chen arrived at the **clinic**（诊所） before dawn to prepare for Sports Day. She had helped **clone**（克隆） a rare plant in biology class last semester. Now, the big event would **close**（结束） the school year with a celebration. She wiped her hands on a soft **cloth**（布） and stepped outside. All the students wore matching **clothes**（衣服） in the school colours. The new **clothing**（衣服） design had been chosen by student vote. A single grey **cloud**（云） drifted across the sky, but the forecast was not **cloudy**（多云的）. The sports **club**（俱乐部） had organised every event with military precision. Their **coach**（教练） blew the whistle sharply, and the races began. Beneath the track, old **coal**（煤） mines ran deep into the earth. The school stood on a hill near the **coast**（海岸）, but the **changeable**（可改变的） spring weather worried the organisers. Each student wore a light **coat**（外套） against the morning chill. At the refreshment stand, volunteers served hot cocoa and coffee. A student found an ancient coin buried beneath the running track. By pure coincidence, it matched one in the local museum. Someone offered a can of coke to the exhausted winner. The morning was **cold**（寒冷的）, but spirits were high. The principal straightened his collar before giving a speech. A **colleague**（同事） from a neighbouring school came to observe the event. Volunteers helped **collect**（收集） the sports equipment after the final race.

陈医生在黎明前就来到了诊所，为运动会日做准备。上个学期，她在生物课上帮助克隆了一株稀有植物。现在，这场盛大的活动将以庆典结束整个学年。她用一块软布擦了擦手，走了出去。所有学生都穿着校服颜色的统一服装。新的服装设计是由学生投票选出的。一片灰色的云飘过天空，但天气预报说不会阴天。体育俱乐部以军事化的精确度组织了每一项赛事。教练吹响了尖锐的哨声，比赛开始了。跑道下面，旧日的煤矿深埋在大地之中。学校坐落在海岸附近的一座小山上，海风清凉了空气。每个学生都穿着一件薄外套来抵御清晨的寒意。在茶点摊，志愿者供应着热可可和咖啡。一个学生在跑道下发现了一枚古老的硬币。纯属巧合，它和本地博物馆里的一枚一模一样。有人递给筋疲力尽的冠军一罐可口可乐。早晨很冷，但大家的兴致很高。校长在发表讲话前理了理衣领。一位来自邻校的同事前来观摩这场活动。最后一场比赛后，志愿者们帮忙收集体育器材。

---

## Part 89 — Chapter 15: The College Reunion

On an autumn morning, alumni gathered at their old **college**（学院）. A treasured **collection**（收藏品） of yearbooks lay on the table, autumn leaves adding brilliant **colour**（颜色）. Mrs. Wang, now seventy, still carried a wooden **comb**（梳子） in her handbag. Dr. Lin, from the chemistry department, wore a neatly pressed **collar**（衣领）. By pure **coincidence**（巧合）, a merchant arrived with a gentle **camel**（骆驼） — a gift for the biology lab. The **bureaucratic**（官僚的） approval to accept the animal had taken many months. Professor Zhang lived in a modest **bungalow**（平房） near the old **circus**（马戏团） grounds outside town. A brave **civilian**（平民） who once helped a student after a car **collision**（碰撞） was honoured at the ceremony. Volunteers served hot **cocoa**（可可粉）, fresh **coffee**（咖啡）, and ice-cold **coke**（可口可乐）. A student found an ancient **coin**（硬币） while digging in the campus garden. The dean asked everyone to **clarify**（阐明） their career paths for the alumni records. They promised to treasure more shared moments and never lose touch.

秋日清晨，校友们齐聚在昔日的学院。一套珍贵的年鉴收藏摊在桌上，秋叶增添了绚丽的颜色。已经七十岁的王女士仍在手提包里带着一把木梳子。林博士，来自化学系，穿着笔挺的衣领。纯属巧合，一位商人带着一头温驯的骆驼来了——这是送给生物实验室的礼物。官僚的审批手续花了整整好几个月。张教授住在城郊老马戏团场地附近的一栋简朴的平房里。一位曾在一场车祸碰撞后救助过学生的平民在典礼上受到了表彰。志愿者供应着热可可、新鲜咖啡和冰凉的可口可乐。一个学生在校园花园里挖到了一枚古老的硬币。院长请大家阐明各自的职业道路，留作校友档案。他们约定珍惜更多的共同回忆，永不失联。
