## Part 90 — Chapter 1: The Startup Pitch

Three college friends decided to **combine**（联合）their skills and **come**（来到）up with a business idea. After watching a **comedy**（喜剧）show for **comfort**（安慰）one stressful night, they had an epiphany. Their team leader took **command**（指挥）and wrote a brilliant **comment**（评论）on the **commercial**（商业的）potential of a mobile app. “We must **commit**（投入）fully,” he said, “and our **commitment**（承诺）will impress the **committee**（委员会）of investors.” They shared a **common**（共同的）dream: to **communicate**（传达）their vision clearly. Good **communication**（交流）was key. Though they joked about **communism**（共产主义）and one friend’s grandfather being a **communist**（共产党员）, their real **companion**（同伴）was ambition. They formed a **company**（公司）and used every **compass**（指南针）of market research to find direction. To **compensate**（补偿）for their lack of experience, they worked twice as hard. Now they would **compete**（竞争）in the national startup contest.

三个大学朋友决定联合他们的技能，想出一个商业创意。在一个压力重重的夜晚看了一部喜剧节目寻求安慰后，他们顿悟了。他们的队长指挥并写了一篇关于某个手机应用商业潜力的精彩评论。他说：“我们必须全心投入，我们的承诺会给投资者委员会留下深刻印象。”他们共享一个共同的梦想：清晰地传达他们的愿景。良好的交流是关键。虽然他们开玩笑说共产主义和其中一位朋友的祖父曾是共产党员，但他们真正的同伴是雄心壮志。他们成立了一家公司，用市场研究的每一个指南针寻找方向。为了补偿缺乏经验的短板，他们加倍努力。现在他们将在全国创业大赛中竞争。

---

## Part 91 — Chapter 2: The Office Project

Lisa proved her **competence**（能力）by delivering a **complete**（完整的）project plan. Every **component**（组件）was detailed, and her **composition**（作文）of the executive summary showed deep **comprehension**（理解）. She refused to **compromise**（妥协）on quality. Though overtime was not **compulsory**（义务的）, she stayed late at her **computer**（电脑）to **concentrate**（集中）. Her main **concern**（关心）was the launch event — she had even booked a **concert**（音乐会）venue. She needed to **conclude**（得出结论）the budget report, and her **conclusion**（结论）would determine the project’s fate. She would **condemn**（谴责）any careless work. Under one **condition**（条件）— a strict code of **conduct**（行为）— the team would succeed. The **conductor**（指挥）of this orchestra of talent was Mr. Zhang, back from an international **conference**（会议）with **confidential**（机密的）partnership documents. He needed Lisa to **confirm**（确认）every detail before the big day.

丽莎通过提交一份完整的项目计划证明了自己的能力。每一个组件都很详细，她对执行摘要的作文展示出深刻的理解。她拒绝在质量上妥协。虽然加班不是义务的，但她还是在电脑前待到很晚以集中精力。她主要的关心是发布活动——她甚至预订了一个音乐会场地。她需要为预算报告得出结论，而她的结论将决定项目的命运。她会谴责任何粗心的工作。在一个条件下——遵守严格的行为准则——团队将获得成功。这支人才乐团的指挥是张先生，他刚从一场国际会议上带回机密的合作文件。他需要丽莎在大日子之前确认每一个细节。

---

## Part 92 — Chapter 3: The Debate Team Triumph

The complex topic could easily **confuse**（使困惑）any beginner. I want to **congratulate**（祝贺）you all — your **congratulation**（恭喜）is well-deserved! To **connect**（连接）ideas and find the **connection**（联系）between arguments requires a sharp mind. One must follow their **conscience**（良心）. The team reached **consensus**（共识）on supporting **conservation**（保护）policies. Even the **conservative**（保守的）members agreed. Now **consider**（考虑）our strategy carefully, and be **considerate**（体贴的）of each other’s views. After much **consideration**（考虑）, we agreed our argument must **consist**（由……组成）of solid evidence and be **consistent**（一致的）throughout. We need **constant**（不断的）practice. The **constitution**（宪法）protects free speech — we should **construct**（构建）our case on that foundation. The **construction**（构建）of a winning argument takes time. Let’s **consult**（咨询）our **consultant**（顾问）for final advice.

这个复杂的话题很容易使初学者困惑。我想祝贺你们所有人——你们的恭喜是当之无愧的！要连接各种想法并找到论据之间的联系需要敏锐的头脑。人必须遵循自己的良心。团队就支持保护政策达成了共识，即使保守的成员也同意了。现在仔细考虑我们的策略，要体贴彼此的观点。经过深思熟虑后，我们一致认为我们的论点必须由确凿的证据组成，并且始终一致。我们需要不断的练习。宪法保护言论自由——我们应该在这个基础上构建我们的案例。构建一个获胜的论点需要时间。让我们咨询一下顾问以获得最后的建议。

---

## Part 93 — Chapter 4: The Cooking Show Challenge

Don’t **consume**（消耗）all your energy chopping vegetables! The pot must **contain**（容纳）enough water, and the **container**（容器）should be heatproof. Our **contemporary**（当代的）cooking style blends flavors from every **continent**（大陆）. Stir gently and **continue**（继续）cooking. Never **contradict**（反驳）the head chef — a **contradictory**（矛盾的）attitude ruins teamwork. On the **contrary**（相反）, harmony helps everyone **contribute**（贡献）their best. Every **contribution**（贡献）matters. You must **control**（控制）the flame precisely. This dish is **controversial**（有争议的）among traditionalists but offers great **convenience**（便利）for busy families. A **convenient**（方便的）recipe is what modern cooks need. Break free from **conventional**（传统的）thinking. Our **conversation**（交谈）with the judge will **convey**（传达）our passion. I must **convince**（说服）him that a simple **cook**（厨师）can create magic!

不要把精力全部消耗在切菜上！锅里必须容纳足够的水，容器要耐热。我们当代的烹饪风格融合了各大洲的风味。继续轻轻搅拌。永远不要反驳主厨——矛盾的态度会破坏团队合作。相反，和谐相处有助于每个人贡献出最好的自己。每一份贡献都很重要。你必须精准地控制火候。这道菜在传统主义者中是有争议的，但给忙碌的家庭带来了极大的便利。一个方便的食谱正是现代厨师所需要的。打破传统的思维模式。我们与评委的交谈将传达我们的热情。我必须说服他，一个简单的厨师也能创造魔法！

---

## Part 94 — Chapter 5: A Trip to the Countryside

Grandma’s old **cooker**（炉灶）still worked perfectly. She baked a tray of **cookie**（饼干）and let them **cool**（冷却）on the windowsill. I made a **copy**（副本）of her handwritten recipe. The fields of **corn**（玉米）stretched to the horizon. The local **corporation**（公司）had offered to buy the land, but the farmers insisted on the **correct**（正确的）price. Every **correction**（改正）to the contract took weeks. I would **correspond**（通信）with my city friends about this — their **corrupt**（腐败的）land deals were shocking. The **cost**（代价）of losing farmland was too high. Inside the **cosy**（温暖舒适的）**cottage**（小屋）, we sat on **cotton**（棉花）cushions. A sudden **cough**（咳嗽）reminded grandpa he **could**（可以）catch a cold. I helped him **count**（计算）the eggs at the **counter**（柜台）. This **country**（乡村）life was a sharp contrast to the busy **countryside**（乡下）markets we passed earlier.

奶奶的旧炉灶依然完美运作。她烤了一盘饼干，让它们在窗台上冷却。我把她手写的食谱抄了一份副本。玉米田一直延伸到地平线。当地的公司提出要购买这块土地，但农民们坚持要一个正确的价格。合同的每一次改正都花了好几周。我会和城里的朋友通信聊聊这件事——他们那里腐败的土地交易令人震惊。失去农田的代价太高了。在温暖舒适的小屋里，我们坐在棉花垫子上。一声突然的咳嗽提醒爷爷他可能会感冒。我帮他在柜台数鸡蛋。这种乡村生活与我们之前经过的热闹的乡下市场形成了鲜明对比。

---

## Part 95 — Chapter 6: The Family Gathering

A young **couple**（夫妇）arrived at the farm. Over the **course**（过程中）of the day, they played tennis on the grass **court**（球场）beside the quiet **courtyard**（庭院）. Their **cousin**（表兄弟）used leaves to **cover**（覆盖）a surprise gift. A **cow**（母牛）wandered nearby, and the boy shouted, “Don’t **crash**（碰撞）into the fence!” He drew a picture with a **crayon**（蜡笔）— his **crazy**（疯狂的）imagination filled the page. Mom served ice **cream**（奶油）and said, “Let’s **create**（创造）happy memories.” Every **creature**（生物）on the farm deserved kindness. Give Dad **credit**（功劳）for organizing this! The **crew**（全体人员）of relatives worked together. A minor **crime**（罪行）had happened nearby — a **criminal**（罪犯）had stolen tools. The police used a strict **criterion**（标准）to investigate. The **crop**（农作物）harvest was approaching. As the sun set, the family stood at the **cross**（交叉）path leading home.

一对年轻夫妇来到了农场。在这一天的过程中，他们在安静的庭院旁草地上的球场打网球。他们的表兄弟用树叶覆盖了一个惊喜礼物。一头母牛在附近漫步，男孩喊道：“别撞到栅栏！”他用蜡笔画了一幅画——他那疯狂的想象力填满了整页纸。妈妈端上冰淇淋并说：“让我们一起创造快乐的回忆。”农场里的每一种生物都值得善待。把功劳归于爸爸组织了这次聚会！一群亲戚齐心协力。附近发生了一起小罪行——一个罪犯偷走了工具。警方用严格的标准展开调查。农作物收割的日子临近了。日落时分，一家人站在通向回家的交叉小径上。

---

## Part 96 — Chapter 7: The City Festival

At the zebra **crossing**（人行横道）, we stopped to watch the parade arrive at the **crossroads**（十字路口）. The excited **crowd**（人群）cheered. No one was **cruel**（残酷的）in this joyful moment. A baby’s happy **cry**（叫声）echoed through the square, where an artist displayed a giant **cube**（立方体）sculpture with **cubic**（立方体的）patterns. The **cuisine**（美食）stalls offered every **culture**（文化）’s specialty. I bought a handmade **cup**（杯子）from a craftsman who stored his wares in an old **cupboard**（碗柜）. “This tea will **cure**（治愈）your tiredness,” he smiled. Foreign **currency**（货币）was accepted at some booths. The school’s **curriculum**（课程）included this festival visit. Sitting on a soft **cushion**（垫子）, I observed local **custom**（风俗）— every **customer**（顾客）was treated like family. The **customs**（海关）officers even joined the fun. I decided to **cut**（切）a piece of cake to share. What a wonderful **cycle**（循环）of seasons to celebrate!

在人行横道处，我们停下脚步观看游行队伍抵达十字路口。兴奋的人群欢呼着。在这欢乐的时刻没有人是残酷的。一个婴儿的欢快叫声回荡在广场上，那里一位艺术家展示了一个带有立方体图案的巨型立方体雕塑。美食摊位提供了每种文化的特色菜。我从一位工匠那里买了一个手工杯子，他把商品存放在一个旧碗柜里。“这种茶能治愈你的疲劳，”他微笑着说。一些摊位接受外币。学校的课程包括了这次节日参观。坐在柔软的垫子上，我观察着当地的风俗——每位顾客都被当作家人对待。海关官员甚至也加入了欢乐之中。我决定切一块蛋糕来分享。多么美好的季节循环值得庆祝啊！

---

## Part 97 — Chapter 8: The Emergency Rescue

It was just an ordinary **daily**（日常的）shift. A **dam**（水坝）upstream had burst after severe **damage**（损害）. The rescue team gathered at the **dance**（跳舞）hall turned command center — lives were in **danger**（危险）and the situation was **dangerous**（危险的）. “I **dare**（敢）anyone to give up now!” the captain shouted into the **dark**（黑暗）. The **darkness**（黑暗）made the operation harder. Rescuers made a **dash**（猛冲）through the flood. The **data**（数据）from sensors was critical. The **date**（日期）would be remembered forever. A mother clutched her **daughter**（女儿）tightly. That **day**（日子）felt endless. One injured man looked nearly **dead**（死的）. We faced a strict **deadline**（截止期限）— evacuate before midnight. A **deaf**（聋的）elderly woman couldn’t hear warnings. We had to make a **deal**（交易）with the helicopter company. “Hold on, my **dear**（亲爱的）!” a medic whispered. We could not bear another **death**（死亡）.

这只是一个普通的日常班次。上游的一座水坝在遭受严重损害后决堤了。救援队在由舞厅改成的指挥中心集合——生命处于危险之中，情况非常危险。“我敢说你们谁也不要现在放弃！”队长在黑暗中喊道。黑暗让救援行动更加困难。救援人员猛冲过洪水。来自传感器的数据至关重要。这个日期将被永远铭记。一位母亲紧紧抱着她的女儿。那一天感觉无比漫长。一个受伤的男人看起来已经快死了。我们面临严格的截止期限——午夜前完成撤离。一位聋哑的老妇人听不到警报。我们必须和直升机公司达成交易。“坚持住，我亲爱的！”一位医护人员轻声说。我们再也承受不起又一次死亡了。

---

## Part 98 — Chapter 9: The Courtroom Verdict

Overwhelmed by **debt**（债务）, he had spent a **decade**（十年）building his business. Now he had to **decide**（决定）whether to **declare**（宣布）bankruptcy. His company’s **decline**（衰退）began three years ago. His wife had tried to **decorate**（装饰）their home beautifully, and each **decoration**（装饰品）told a story. The **decrease**（减少）in sales was devastating. Yet his **deed**（行为）was not criminal — his conviction ran **deep**（深的）. A stray **deer**（鹿）on the road had caused the crash. The insurance company tried to **defeat**（击败）his claim, but his lawyer mounted a strong **defence**（辩护）to **defend**（防护）his rights. To a high **degree**（程度）, the evidence favored him. The judge announced a **delay**（延迟）to **delete**（删除）flawed evidence. The opposition had **deliberately**（故意地）withheld documents. In this **delicate**（微妙的）legal battle, every word mattered.

他身陷沉重的债务，花了十年时间建立自己的企业。现在他必须决定是否宣布破产。他的公司三年前开始衰退。他的妻子曾努力把家装饰得漂漂亮亮，每一件装饰品都诉说着一个故事。销售额的减少是毁灭性的。然而他的行为并非犯罪——他的信念深得很。路上的一只迷路的鹿导致了车祸。保险公司试图击败他的索赔，但他的律师进行了有力的辩护来防护他的权利。在很大程度上，证据对他有利。法官宣布延迟审理以删除有瑕疵的证据。对方故意地隐瞒了文件。在这微妙的官司中，每一个字都至关重要。

---

## Part 99 — Chapter 10: The Restaurant Dream

The dish was **delicious**（美味的）! She felt pure **delight**（高兴）and looked **delighted**（高兴的）. A courier came to **deliver**（递送）fresh ingredients. The **demand**（需求）for her cooking grew daily. Her **dentist**（牙科医生）joked that her desserts were worth every cavity! The health **department**（部门）inspected the kitchen. “Everything must **depend**（依赖）on hygiene,” she told staff. A large **deposit**（定金）secured the lease. The **depth**（深度）of flavor in her soup amazed everyone. She would **describe**（描述）each recipe in a book, and the **description**（描述）alone made mouths water. Beyond the city lay a **desert**（沙漠）, but she didn’t **deserve**（值得）such isolation — her talent was her ticket. Her **desire**（渴望）to succeed was strong. In her most **desperate**（绝望的）moment, a food critic arrived and ordered **dessert**（甜点）. This was her **destination**（目的地）— success. She would not let doubt **destroy**（破坏）her dream.

这道菜太美味了！她感到纯粹的欢喜，脸上露出高兴的表情。快递员送来了新鲜食材。对她厨艺的需求与日俱增。她的牙科医生开玩笑说她的甜点值得每一颗蛀牙！卫生部门检查了厨房。“一切都必须依赖卫生，”她对员工说。一大笔定金确保了租约。她汤里风味的深度让所有人惊叹。她将在一本书中描述每一道食谱，光是描述就让人垂涎三尺。城市之外是一片沙漠，但她不应该承受这样的孤立——她的才华是她的通行证。她对成功的渴望非常强烈。在她最绝望的时刻，一位美食评论家到来并点了甜点。这就是她的目的地——成功。她不会让怀疑破坏她的梦想。

---

## Part 100 — Chapter 11: The Detective’s Pursuit

The **detective**（侦探）had spent years trying to **develop**（发展）a breakthrough. The **development**（进展）of the case was slow. He would **devote**（投入）all his energy, and his **devotion**（热爱）to justice never wavered. He picked up the phone to **dial**（拨号）a contact. The **dialogue**（对话）was cryptic — something about a **diamond**（钻石）shipment. He opened his **diary**（日记）to review old entries. The suspect’s **dictation**（口述）was recorded in a thick **dictionary**（词典）-sized transcript. “Don’t let this trail **die**（消失）,” his partner urged. The suspect maintained a strict **diet**（日常饮食）, but his story and the evidence would **differ**（不一致）. This was a **difficult**（困难的）case with many layers of **difficulty**（困难）. They needed to **dig**（挖掘）deeper into financial records and **digest**（理解）complex documents. The suspect’s loss of **dignity**（尊严）was complete when confronted with the truth.

这位侦探花了多年时间尝试发展出突破性的进展。案情的进展非常缓慢。他将投入全部精力，他对正义的热爱从未动摇。他拿起电话拨号联系一位线人。对话内容隐晦难懂——涉及一批钻石货物。他翻开日记回顾旧记录。嫌疑人的口述被记录在一份词典那么厚的笔录中。“别让这条线索消失，”他的搭档催促道。嫌疑人保持着严格的日常饮食，但他的说法与证据会不一致。这是一桩困难的案件，困难重重。他们需要更深地挖掘财务记录并消化理解复杂的文件。当面对真相时，嫌疑人彻底丧失了尊严。

---

## Part 101 — Chapter 12: The Expedition Gone Wrong

Trapped in a steep valley, the team faced a terrible **dilemma**（困境）. Each **dimension**（尺寸）of the gorge seemed impossible to climb. After a cold **dinner**（晚餐）of canned food, someone joked about a **dinosaur**（恐龙）fossil they had found. The carbon **dioxide**（二氧化碳）levels were rising in the narrow cave. They took a quick **dip**（浸入）in the stream. Their guide, holding a geology **diploma**（文凭）, gave **direct**（直接的）orders. “This **direction**（方向）leads to the old mining **directory**（名录）site.” Their clothes were **dirty**（肮脏的）and torn. One member had a **disability**（残疾）, but the **disabled**（残疾的）climber was the bravest. His **disadvantage**（劣势）became a strength. No one would **disagree**（不同意）with the plan to move forward. They refused to let hope **disappear**（消失）. They would not **disappoint**（使失望）their sponsors. Though **disappointed**（失望的）by the delay, they pressed on. Only a **disaster**（灾难）could stop them now.

被困在陡峭的山谷中，团队面临着一个可怕的困境。峡谷的每一个维度看起来都无法攀爬。吃了一顿冷冷的罐头晚餐后，有人开玩笑说起他们发现的一块恐龙化石。狭窄洞穴里的二氧化碳浓度正在上升。他们在小溪里快速地浸了一下。持有地质学文凭的向导发出了直接命令。“这个方向通向旧的矿业名录地点。”他们的衣服又脏又破。一位成员有残疾，但这名残疾的攀登者是最勇敢的。他的劣势变成了优势。没有人会不同意继续前进的计划。他们拒绝让希望消失。他们不会让赞助人失望。尽管对延误感到失望，他们仍继续前进。只有一场灾难才能阻止他们了。

---

## Part 102 — Chapter 13: The Diving Competition

The equipment store offered a student **discount**（折扣）. “Don’t let the price **discourage**（使气馁）you,” the coach said. The team would **discover**（发现）a hidden underwater cave — a remarkable **discovery**（发现）that made headlines. Underwater, there is no **discrimination**（歧视）— everyone is equal. They gathered to **discuss**（讨论）strategy, and the heated **discussion**（讨论）lasted hours. One diver caught a **disease**（疾病）from contaminated water — the wound looked **disgusting**（令人厌恶的）. The team chef prepared a special **dish**（菜肴）to boost morale. There was no room for **dislike**（讨厌）or negativity. The coach had to **dismiss**（解散）two members who broke rules. The **distance**（距离）to the finals felt enormous. Every diver earned **distinction**（荣誉）. You must **distinguish**（辨别）between safe and risky currents. We’ll **distribute**（分配）tasks fairly. Nothing should **disturb**（干扰）our focus — the **disturbing**（令人不安的）news about the weather was set aside. Time to **dive**（跳水）!

装备店提供了学生折扣。“别让价格使你气馁，”教练说。团队将发现一个隐藏的水下洞穴——这是一个登上头条的重大发现。在水下，没有歧视——每个人都是平等的。他们聚集在一起讨论策略，热烈的讨论持续了几个小时。一位潜水员因受污染的水而感染了疾病——伤口看起来令人厌恶。团队厨师准备了一道特别的菜肴来提振士气。这里没有讨厌或消极情绪的空间。教练不得不解散了两名违反规则的队员。距离决赛的距离感觉无比遥远。每位潜水员都赢得了荣誉。你必须辨别安全与危险的水流。我们将公平地分配任务。任何事都不应干扰我们的专注——关于天气的令人不安的消息被暂时搁置了。跳水时间到了！

---

## Part 103 — Chapter 14: The Dormitory Life

Our university dorm was home to **diverse**（多样的）characters. We learned to **divide**（分配）chores fairly — the **division**（分工）kept peace. One roommate’s parents were going through a **divorce**（离婚）, and he often felt **dizzy**（晕眩的）from stress. “We can **do**（做）something to help,” we decided. A **doctor**（医生）visited weekly. We kept every **document**（文件）organized. A stray **dog**（狗）became our mascot. My sister sent a handmade **doll**（洋娃娃）for good luck. Every **dollar**（美元）mattered, so we planned to **donate**（捐赠）to charity. The squeaky **door**（门）of our **dormitory**（宿舍）needed oil. On the map, a red **dot**（点）marked our campus. The workload was **double**（双倍的）compared to high school. We sat **down**（向下）to **download**（下载）lecture notes every evening, then raced **downstairs**（楼下）for a late-night snack.

我们的大学宿舍住着多样的成员。我们学会公平分配家务——这种分工维持了和平。一位室友的父母正在经历离婚，他经常因压力感到晕眩。“我们可以做点什么来帮忙，”我们决定。一位医生每周来访。我们保持每一份文件井井有条。一只流浪狗成了我们的吉祥物。我姐姐寄来了一个手工洋娃娃以祝好运。每一美元都很重要，所以我们计划向慈善机构捐赠。宿舍嘎嘎作响的门需要上油。地图上一个红点标出了我们的校园。作业量是高中时的双倍。每晚我们坐下来下载讲义，然后跑到楼下吃夜宵。

---

## Part 104 — Chapter 15: The City Hustle

In **downtown**（市中心）, Jack sold **dozen**（一打）of handmade crafts daily. Every morning he reviewed his **draft**（草稿）designs, then had to **drag**（拖）his heavy cart to the square. He would **draw**（画）portraits quickly — the only **drawback**（缺点）was the weather. His money went into a locked **drawer**（抽屉）. The construction **drill**（钻孔机）noise was annoying, but he took a **drink**（饮料）break at noon. A taxi would **drive**（驾驶）by, and the **driver**（驾驶员）often bought a sketch. One **drop**（滴）of rain could ruin his work. He avoided **drug**（药物）stores whose ads blared. A street performer beat a **drum**（鼓）nearby. A **drunk**（喝醉的）man once knocked over his easel, but Jack stayed **dry**（冷静的）-tempered. He would **duck**（躲避）trouble, not seek it. His rent was **due**（到期的）tomorrow, and the **dull**（无趣的）routine never broke his spirit.

在市中心，杰克每天卖出一打手工制品。每天早晨他审阅草稿设计，然后不得不拖着他沉重的推车到广场。他能快速地画出肖像——唯一的缺点是天气。他的收入锁在一个抽屉里。建筑工地的钻孔机噪音很烦人，但他中午会休息喝杯饮料。一辆出租车驶过，驾驶员经常买一幅速写。一滴雨水就能毁掉他的作品。他避开那些播放大声音乐的药物商店。附近有一个街头艺人在打鼓。一个喝醉的人曾撞倒过他的画架，但杰克保持着冷静的脾气。他会躲避麻烦，而不是去找麻烦。他的房租明天就到期了，而这无趣的日常从未击垮他的精神。

---

## Part 105 — The Sunset Kitchen

As **dusk**（黄昏）fell, Lily decided to make **dumpling**（饺子）for dinner. **During**（在...期间）her preparation, a cloud of **dust**（灰尘）rose from the flour bag, making the counter **dusty**（灰尘多的）. She wiped it clean and tossed the waste into the **dustbin**（垃圾箱）. Her **dynamic**（有活力的）little brother ran in, **eager**（渴望的）to help. 'Be careful with **each**（每个）step,' she warned. Outside the window, an **eagle**（鹰）soared high above the **earth**（地球）. Her grandmother whispered in her **ear**（耳朵）. Lily remembered how she used to **earn**（赚得）pocket money by helping neighbors cook. An **early**（早的）summer **earthquake**（地震）last year had taught her community how precious home-cooked meals can be. In the **east**（东方）, the first stars appeared. Her **eastern**（东方的）grandmother always said cooking was never **easy**（容易的）—it required patience. 'Let's **eat**（吃）!' she called. Her brother, studying **ecology**（生态学）at school, reminded everyone not to waste food.

当黄昏降临时，莉莉决定做饺子当晚餐。在她准备的过程中，面粉袋里扬起一阵灰尘，让台面变得灰蒙蒙的。她擦干净台面，把垃圾扔进垃圾箱。她那活力四射的弟弟跑进来，渴望帮忙。“每一步都要小心，”她提醒道。窗外，一只鹰在地球上空高高翱翔。祖母的声音在她耳朵里回响。莉莉记得她曾经靠帮邻居做饭赚零花钱。去年初夏的一场地震让她的社区懂得了家常菜饭有多么珍贵。东方出现了最初的星星。她那来自东方的祖母总是说，做饭从来不是容易的事——它需要耐心。“我们吃吧！”她喊道。她弟弟在学校学了生态学，提醒大家不要浪费食物。

---

## Part 106 — The School Newspaper

On the **edge**（边缘）of campus, the student **editor**（编辑）worked on the latest **edition**（版本）of the school paper. 'We need to **educate**（教育）our readers,' she said. The topic was **education**（教育）reform, and every **educator**（教育家）in town would read it. The **effect**（影响）of their **effort**（努力）could be significant. Her breakfast—a boiled **egg**（蛋）and **eggplant**（茄子）—sat untouched. '**Either**（两者之中任一）you eat or you work, not both,' joked an **elder**（年长者）teacher passing by. The student council would **elect**（选举）new members next week. Meanwhile, the **electrical**（电的）system in the lab needed fixing—without **electricity**（电）, the **electronic**（电子的）equipment was useless. The new science wing looked **elegant**（优雅的）, with a mural of an **elephant**（象）. 'What **else**（别的）should we cover?' she asked. The question **embarrass**（使困窘）her junior reporter, who had no ideas.

在校园边缘，学生编辑正在制作校报的最新一期。“我们需要教育我们的读者，”她说。主题是教育改革，镇上的每一位教育家都会读到。他们努力的影响可能很大。她的早餐——一个煮蛋和茄子——原封不动。“你要么吃饭要么工作，不能两样都做，”一位路过的年长老师开玩笑说。学生会下周将选举新成员。与此同时，实验室的电力系统需要维修——没有电，电子设备就毫无用处。新的科学楼看起来很优雅，墙上画着一头大象的壁画。“我们还应该报道什么别的？”她问道。这个问题使她的初级记者感到困窘，因为他毫无想法。

---

## Part 107 — The Embassy Intern

Working at the **embassy**（大使馆）was a dream come true. During an **emergency**（紧急状况）, the young intern had to assist the **emperor**（皇帝）'s delegation. His supervisor would **employ**（雇用）only the most reliable staff. 'I **encourage**（鼓励）you to stay calm,' she said, offering much-needed **encouragement**（鼓励）. The meeting seemed **endless**（无尽的）, with no clear **end**（结束）in sight. A perfect **ending**（收场）required compromise from every side. Across the table sat an old **enemy**（敌人）of the state—now a negotiation partner. The intern remained **energetic**（精力充沛的）despite the long hours. Outside, a powerful **engine**（引擎）hummed in the diplomatic vehicle. '**Enjoy**（享受）the challenge,' his mentor whispered. The experience was genuinely **enjoyable**（令人愉快的）. He watched the team **enlarge**（扩大）the agreement's scope. There was **enough**（足够的）trust to proceed. An **enquiry**（询问）from the press came through. As they prepared to **enter**（进入）the final session, he understood what true **enterprise**（事业心）meant—turning tension into triumph.

在大使馆工作是他梦想成真的事。在一次紧急状况中，这位年轻的实习生必须协助皇帝的代表团。他的主管只雇用最可靠的工作人员。“我鼓励你保持冷静，”她说，给了他急需的鼓励。会议似乎无尽的，看不到明确的结束。一个完美的收场需要各方的妥协。桌子对面坐着国家的一位旧敌人——现在是谈判伙伴。尽管时间漫长，实习生仍然精力充沛。外面，外交车辆里传来强劲的引擎轰鸣声。“享受这个挑战吧，”他的导师低声说。这段经历确实是令人愉快的。他目睹团队扩大了协议的范围。有足够的信任来继续推进。媒体发来了一则询问。当他们准备进入最后一场会议时，他明白了什么是真正的事业心——将紧张化为胜利。

---

## Part 108 — The Talent Show

The school **entertainment**（娱乐）night was about to begin. An **enthusiastic**（狂热的）crowd gathered at the main **entry**（入口）. The **environment**（环境）buzzed with excitement. Lily watched with **envy**（羡慕）as performers prepared backstage. 'We are all **equal**（平等的）tonight,' the host announced, celebrating **equality**（平等）. Each performer was **equip**（配备）with proper **equipment**（装备）—microphones, lights, and an **eraser**（擦子）board for last-minute changes. Suddenly, a volcano model began to **erupt**（爆发）unexpectedly on stage, and the science team had to **escape**（逃跑）amid laughter. '**Especially**（尤其）impressive!' the judges laughed. They would **evaluate**（评估）each act fairly. Not **even**（甚至）the smallest mistake would go unnoticed. By **evening**（傍晚）, the **event**（事件）became the talk of the town. 'We will **eventually**（最后）win next year,' Lily promised herself. Had she **ever**（曾经）felt so inspired? **Every**（每一个）performance touched her heart.

学校的娱乐之夜即将开始。一群狂热的观众聚集在入口处。环境中充满了兴奋的嗡嗡声。莉莉羡慕地看着表演者在后台准备。“今晚我们都是平等的，”主持人宣布，赞颂着平等精神。每位表演者都配备了适当的装备——麦克风、灯光，以及一块用于临时修改的擦子板。突然，一个火山模型在台上意外地开始爆发，科学小组在一阵笑声中不得不逃跑。“尤其令人印象深刻！”评委们笑着说。他们将公正地评估每一个节目。甚至最小的失误也不会被忽略。到了傍晚，这个事件成了全镇热议的话题。“我们最后一定会赢的，”莉莉对自己承诺。她可曾感到如此受鼓舞？每一个表演都触动了她的心。

---

## Part 109 — The Science Fair Mystery

**Everybody**（每个人）at school attended the annual science fair. **Everyone**（人人）had worked hard on their **everyday**（日常的）experiments, turning ordinary moments into discovery. **Everything**（每件事物）was on display, and visitors were **everywhere**（到处）. The judges examined the **evidence**（证据）carefully. It was **evident**（显然的）that this year's projects were exceptional. One project traced the **evolution**（进化）of local river species. Another provided the **exact**（精确的）chemical formula for clean energy. 'Let me **examine**（检查）this more closely,' said Professor Wang, using the project as an **example**（例子）of **excellent**（出色的）student research. **Except**（除了）for one messy display, every booth impressed the judges. The volcano model would **excite**（使兴奋）the younger students. '**Excuse**（原谅）me,' a parent said, squeezing through the crowd. The fire safety **exercise**（演习）team stood ready. The **exhibition**（展览会）proved that scientific curiosity does **exist**（存在）in every young mind. Their very **existence**（存在）as young scientists was validated. With a satisfied smile, Lily headed toward the **exit**（出口）.

学校的每个人都参加了年度科学展。人人都在他们的日常实验上付出了努力，将平凡时刻化为发现。每件事物都在展出，到处都挤满了参观者。评委们仔细研究着证据。显然，今年的项目非常出色。一个项目追踪了本地河流物种的进化。另一个提供了清洁能源的精确化学公式。“让我更仔细地检查一下，”王教授说，以这个项目作为出色学生研究的例子。除了一个凌乱的展位，每个展台都给评委留下了深刻印象。火山模型将会使年幼的学生兴奋。“请原谅，”一位家长说着挤过人群。消防安全演习小组已准备就绪。展览会证明了科学好奇心确实存在于每个年轻的心灵中。他们作为年轻科学家的存在得到了认可。带着满意的微笑，莉莉向出口走去。

---

## Part 110 — The Lab Experiment

Dr. Chen planned to **expand**（扩张）his research. He would **expect**（预期）results within weeks, though his **expectation**（期待）was cautious given the **expense**（费用）of **expensive**（贵的）materials. As an **expert**（专家）in molecular biology, he knew how to **explain**（解释）complex ideas simply. His **explanation**（解释）was always clear and **explicit**（清楚的）. Today's experiment might even **explode**（爆炸）old theories. 'We must **explore**（探索）every possibility,' he told his team. Their work could **export**（出口）knowledge to labs worldwide. The procedure would **expose**（使暴露）hidden cellular patterns. With an **express**（明确的）order from the institute, they proceeded. The **expression**（表达）on his face shifted from tension to joy. They needed an **extension**（延长）of the grant deadline. The **extra**（额外的）funding was **extraordinary**（非凡的）news. Under **extreme**（极度的）pressure, the team worked late into the night. Dr. Chen rubbed his tired **eye**（眼睛）and smiled.

陈博士计划扩张他的研究。他预期几周内会有结果，尽管考虑到昂贵材料的费用，他的期待相当谨慎。作为分子生物学领域的专家，他知道如何简单地解释复杂的想法。他的解释总是清晰而清楚的。今天的实验甚至可能炸毁旧理论。“我们必须探索每一种可能性，”他对团队说。他们的工作可以将知识出口到全世界的实验室。这个程序将暴露隐藏的细胞模式。在研究所的明确指令下，他们继续进行。他脸上的表情从紧张变成了喜悦。他们需要延长资助期限。额外的资金是非凡的好消息。在极度的压力下，团队工作到深夜。陈博士揉了揉他疲惫的眼睛，笑了。

---

## Part 111 — A Family Dinner

Grandpa's **eyesight**（视力）had weakened, but his **face**（脸）still lit up at family gatherings. Mom applied a **facial**（脸的）mask before the guests arrived—a **fact**（事实）that made Dad laugh. The **factory**（工厂）where Dad worked had given him the weekend off. 'Have **faith**（信心）in my cooking,' Grandma said, dismissing any **false**（错误的）modesty. The kitchen smelled **familiar**（熟悉的）—of soy sauce and ginger. The whole **family**（家庭）loved her recipes, which were **famous**（出名的）in the neighborhood. Uncle Li, a **fan**（迷）of traditional cuisine, brought a **fancy**（精美的）dessert that looked **fantastic**（奇妙的）—like something from a **fantasy**（幻想）novel. The dinner **fare**（食物）included vegetables from their own **farm**（农场）. Every **farmer**（农夫）in the area knew Grandpa's techniques were **fast**（快速的）and reliable. '**Fasten**（系紧）your napkin,' Mom reminded her son. Uncle, who was quite **fat**（胖的）, laughed heartily and asked for seconds.

爷爷的视力已经衰退，但他的脸在家庭聚会时仍然容光焕发。客人们到来之前，妈妈敷了一张脸部面膜——这个事实让爸爸笑了。爸爸工作的工厂给了他周末休息。“对我的厨艺要有信心，”奶奶说，摈弃了任何错误的谦虚。厨房里飘着熟悉的味道——酱油和姜。整个家庭都喜欢她的食谱，在邻里间是出名的。李叔叔是一位传统美食迷，他带来了一份精美的甜点，看起来奇妙的——就像幻想小说里的东西。晚餐的食物包括他们自己农场种植的蔬菜。当地的每一位农夫都知道爷爷的技术是快速而可靠的。“系紧你的餐巾，”妈妈提醒儿子。那位相当胖的叔叔开怀大笑，还要再添一碗。

---

## Part 112 — The Small Town Story

My **father**（父亲）always said it was never anyone's **fault**（过错）that our town stayed small. The mayor did us a great **favour**（恩惠）by preserving old buildings. Everyone's **favourite**（喜爱的）spot was the post office, where Mr. Zhang still sent documents by **fax**（传真）. During festivals, the whole street became a **feast**（宴会）of local specialties. A **feather**（羽毛）from the town's legendary swan was displayed in the **federal**（联邦的）heritage museum. The entrance **fee**（费用）was just one yuan. Farmers would **feed**（喂）their animals early, then **feel**（感觉）the crisp morning air. There was a special **feeling**（感觉）of belonging here. An old **fellow**（同伴）named Chen, the town's first **female**（女性的）doctor, still tended her garden. She had built a **fence**（栅栏）from recycled wood. Visitors crossed the river by **ferry**（渡船）. 'Go **fetch**（取来）some water,' she'd say to children. A **few**（少数）families still wove **fibre**（纤维）into cloth. Her life story read like **fiction**（小说）—but every word was true.

我的父亲总是说，小镇一直这么小，不是任何人的过错。市长为我们做了件大好事，保护了老建筑。每个人最喜爱的地方是邮局，张先生仍然在那里用传真发送文件。节日期间，整条街变成了本地特色美食的盛宴。小镇传奇天鹅的一根羽毛陈列在联邦遗产博物馆里。门票费用只需一元。农夫们早早喂完牲口，然后感受着清新的晨间空气。这里有一种特别的归属感。一位叫陈的老同伴，镇上第一位女医生，仍然打理着她的花园。她用回收木材建了一道栅栏。游客们乘渡船过河。“去取些水来，”她对孩子们说。少数几户人家仍然用纤维织布。她的人生故事读起来像小说——但每一个字都是真的。

---

## Part 113 — The Sports Competition

On the sports **field**（场地）, the final **fight**（对抗）was about to begin. The scoreboard showed a surprising **figure**（数字）—the underdogs were leading. Coach Wang pulled a **file**（文件）from his bag with last-minute tactics. '**Fill**（充满）your lungs with air,' he told the team. A **film**（电影）crew captured every moment for the **final**（最后的）highlight reel. The sports club's **finance**（财务）team had worked hard to **find**（找到）sponsors. Each athlete stretched every **finger**（手指）, and one anxiously bit a **fingernail**（手指甲）. '**Finish**（完成）strong!' the captain shouted. The starting **fire**（火）of determination burned in their eyes. Nearby, the swimming team competed like **fish**（鱼）in water. An old **fisherman**（渔夫）watched from the stands, clenching his **fist**（拳头）with excitement. The runners had to be **fit**（适宜的）enough for the marathon. A technician came to **fix**（修理）the timing system. The Olympic **flame**（火焰）symbol glowed on the giant screen. A camera **flash**（闪光）captured the winning moment forever.

在运动场上，最后的对抗即将开始。记分牌上显示着一个令人惊讶的数字——不被看好的队伍正领先。王教练从包里抽出一份文件，上面写着最后一刻的战术。“让你的肺充满空气，”他对队员们说。一个电影摄制组捕捉着每一个瞬间，为最后的精彩集锦做准备。体育俱乐部的财务团队努力找到了赞助商。每位运动员都伸展着每一根手指，有人紧张地咬着手指甲。“完美地完成比赛！”队长喊道。决心的起跑之火在他们的眼中燃烧。旁边的游泳池里，队员们像水中的鱼一样比赛。一位老渔夫在看台上观看，激动地握紧拳头。跑者们必须足够适宜才能参加马拉松。一名技术员赶来修理计时系统。奥运火焰标志在巨大的屏幕上闪耀。一道相机闪光永远捕捉了胜利的瞬间。

---

## Part 114 — Wilderness Survival

When the storm hit, they had to **flee**（逃跑）into the forest. Every muscle and **flesh**（肉）ached from the hike. Survival required **flexible**（灵活的）thinking. Their **flight**（飞行）had been canceled, stranding them in the wilderness. Leaves began to **float**（漂浮）on the rising water. A sudden **flood**（洪水）threatened their camp. They moved to higher ground and swept the cave **floor**（地板）clean. With only a bag of **flour**（面粉）, they had to get creative. A small stream continued to **flow**（流动）nearby. Wild **flower**（花）bloomed along the bank—a sign of hope. Despite the cold, nobody caught the **flu**（流感）. Their guide's **fluency**（流畅）in local dialects—and his **fluent**（流利的）communication skills—helped them navigate. Morning **fog**（雾）made everything **foggy**（模糊的）. They had to **fold**（折叠）their wet clothes. Local **folk**（人们）eventually found them. '**Follow**（跟随）us,' an elder said. The villagers were **fond**（喜欢的）of helping travelers. They offered warm **food**（食物）and shelter, proving that kindness survives even in the wildest places.

暴风雨来袭时，他们不得不逃进森林里。徒步跋涉让每一块肌肉和肉体都在疼痛。生存需要灵活的思维。他们的飞行航班被取消了，把他们困在了荒野中。树叶开始在上涨的水面上漂浮。一场突如其来的洪水威胁着他们的营地。他们转移到更高处，把洞穴的地板打扫干净。只有一袋面粉，他们必须发挥创造力。附近一条小溪继续流动着。岸边盛开着野花——那是希望的象征。尽管寒冷，没有人染上流感。向导在当地方言方面的流畅——以及他流利的沟通能力——帮助他们找到了方向。清晨的雾让一切变得模糊。他们不得不折叠湿透的衣服。当地的乡民最终找到了他们。“跟我们来，”一位长者说。村民们是乐于帮助旅行者的。他们提供了温暖的食物和庇护，证明了善意即使在最荒凉的地方也能存活。

---

## Part 115 — The Trip Abroad

Tom felt **foolish**（愚蠢的）for packing too much. On **foot**（脚）, he explored the old city while his friends played **football**（足球）at the park. '**For**（为了）the best experience, get lost on purpose,' his guidebook said. The local laws **forbid**（禁止）littering—a rule they respected. The historical site's cultural **force**（影响力）was undeniable. The weather **forecast**（预报）promised sunshine. Rubbing his **forehead**（额头）, Tom realized he looked like a typical **foreigner**（外国人）with his map. He could never **foresee**（预见）meeting an old friend in this distant city. They hiked together through a bamboo **forest**（森林）, talking as if they had known each other **forever**（永远）. 'Don't **forget**（忘记）to write,' she said. But Tom was **forgetful**（健忘的）by nature. At dinner, he struggled with his **fork**（叉子）while filling out a customs **form**（表格）. The restaurant menu's **format**（格式）was confusing. His **former**（从前的）teacher had taught him well, though. After a **fortnight**（两星期）abroad, he felt truly **fortunate**（幸运的）to have made this journey.

汤姆觉得自己带了太多行李，真是愚蠢的。他步行探索古城，而朋友们在公园里踢足球。“为了最好的体验，故意迷路吧，”他的旅行指南上写着。当地法律禁止乱扔垃圾——这是他们遵守的规则。历史遗迹的文化影响力是不可否认的。天气预报承诺会出太阳。汤姆揉着额头，意识到自己拿着地图的样子像一个典型的外国人。他从未预见到会在这遥远的城市遇见一位老朋友。他们一起徒步穿过一片竹林，聊着天，仿佛已经认识彼此永远。“别忘了写信，”她说。但汤姆天生是健忘的。晚餐时，他笨拙地使用叉子，同时填写着海关表格。餐厅菜单的格式令人困惑。不过，他从前的老师教得很好。在国外度过两星期后，他感到非常幸运能够完成这趟旅程。

---

## Part 116 — City Exploration

The orphanage aimed to **foster**（培养）every child's talent. It was **found**（建立）by a wealthy merchant decades ago. A clever **fox**（狐狸）statue guarded the garden, surrounded by **fragrant**（芳香的）jasmine. The building's **framework**（结构）was European, built with old **franc**（法郎）donations. Children enjoyed the **free**（自由的）afternoons, biking along the **freeway**（高速公路）service road. In winter, the pond would **freeze**（冻结）solid, and **freezing**（严寒的）winds made outdoor play brief. Their **frequent**（频繁的）visits to the city museum sparked curiosity. Physics class taught them about **friction**（摩擦）—how surfaces resist motion. The staff were **friendly**（友好的）, never wanting to **frighten**（使惊吓）the children. A pond **frog**（青蛙）became the unofficial pet. 'I come **from**（来自）a small village,' new children would say, standing at the **front**（前面）gate. They had crossed a mountain **frontier**（边境）to reach this home. Every autumn, they harvested **fruit**（水果）from the orchard. Cook would **fry**（油炸）apples for special treats.

这家孤儿院旨在培养每个孩子的天赋。它是几十年前由一位富商建立的。一尊聪明的狐狸雕像守护着花园，四周环绕着芳香的茉莉花。建筑的结构是欧式的，用古老的法国法郎捐款建成。孩子们享受自由的午后，沿着高速公路辅路骑自行车。冬天，池塘会冻结成冰，严寒的风让户外玩耍变得短暂。他们对城市博物馆频繁的造访激发了好奇心。物理课教他们关于摩擦的知识——表面如何抵抗运动。工作人员是友好的，从不想吓到孩子们。一只池塘青蛙成了非官方宠物。“我来自一个小村庄，”新来的孩子会站在前门这样说。他们曾跨越山区的边境才到达这个家。每年秋天，他们从果园采摘水果。厨师会油炸苹果作为特别款待。

---

## Part 117 — A Weekend at Home

After a **full**（满满的）week of exams, Lily wanted pure **fun**（乐趣）. Her brain's memory **function**（功能）needed rest. She remembered the **funeral**（葬礼）of her pet goldfish last month—sad but **funny**（好笑的）in hindsight, with its tiny shoebox coffin. Her cat, shedding **fur**（毛皮）everywhere, curled up on the **furnished**（有家具的）balcony. The rattan **furniture**（家具）creaked in the breeze. She hoped to **gain**（获得）new skills over the weekend. Scrolling through her phone's photo **gallery**（画廊）, she found pictures of her dad holding a **gallon**（加仑）of paint, renovating the **garage**（车库）. They played a board **game**（比赛）every Saturday—her mother always won. Taking out the **garbage**（垃圾）was her least favorite chore. The **garden**（花园）out back was her sanctuary, where **garlic**（大蒜）and tomatoes grew side by side.

满满一周的考试后，莉莉想要纯粹的乐趣。她大脑的记忆功能需要休息。她想起了上个月宠物金鱼的葬礼——回想起来既悲伤又好笑，那个小小的鞋盒棺材。她的猫掉得到处是毛皮，在有家具的阳台上蜷缩着。藤制家具在微风中吱嘎作响。她希望周末能获得新技能。翻看手机里的照片画廊，她找到了爸爸提着一加仑油漆翻新车库的照片。他们每个星期六都玩棋盘比赛——她妈妈总是赢。倒垃圾是她最不喜欢的家务。后花园是她的避难所，那里大蒜和西红柿并排生长。

---

## Part 118 — The Market Day

At the town market, a tailor displayed his finest **garment**（衣服）. The **gas**（汽油）lamp flickered above his stall near the main **gate**（大门）. People began to **gather**（聚集）as the morning sun grew warm. A **gay**（欢快的）melody from a street musician filled the air. In **general**（大体上）, the market was a cheerful place. A **generous**（慷慨的）vendor gave free samples of honey. A **gentle**（温和的）old **gentleman**（绅士）quietly sketched the scene. '**Geometry**（几何学）is everywhere,' he said, pointing at the patterns on a carpet. A street performer's dramatic **gesture**（手势）drew applause. 'You'll **get**（得到）a discount if you buy two,' a seller called out. A **gifted**（有天才的）young painter sold portraits by the fountain. Nearby, a **giraffe**（长颈鹿）statue from Africa attracted tourists. A little **girl**（女孩）begged her mother to **give**（给）her a coin for the fountain. Her **glad**（高兴的）laughter echoed through the square. At a **glance**（一瞥）, the market seemed chaotic, but it had its own rhythm. The sun's **glare**（刺眼的强光）reflected off the **glass**（玻璃）display cases. A **globe**（地球仪）in the antique shop spun slowly. The old town's **glory**（荣耀）lived on in moments like these.

在镇上的集市里，一位裁缝展示着他最精美的衣服。主大门附近他的摊位上方，汽油灯闪烁着微光。随着晨日变暖，人们开始聚集。街头艺人欢快的旋律在空气中飘荡。大体上，集市是个愉快的地方。一位慷慨的摊主赠送蜂蜜样品。一位温和的老绅士静静地素描着眼前的场景。“几何学无处不在，”他指着地毯上的图案说。一位街头表演者戏剧性的手势赢得了掌声。“买二送一，你会得到折扣，”一位卖家喊道。一个有天才的年轻画家在喷泉旁出售肖像画。不远处，来自非洲的长颈鹿雕像吸引了游客。一个小女孩恳求妈妈给她一枚硬币投进喷泉。她高兴的笑声在广场中回荡。乍看之下，集市似乎杂乱无章，但它有自己的节奏。玻璃展示柜反射着刺眼的阳光。古董店里的地球仪慢慢旋转着。老城的荣耀在这样的时刻得以延续。

---

## Part 119 — The Graduation Ceremony

Wearing white **glove**（手套）, the graduates marched in. The ceremony was like **glue**（胶水）, bonding years of memories together. 'Once you **go**（去）into the world, be bold,' the principal said. He joked about the school **goat**（山羊）that once escaped into the library. 'Thank **god**（上帝）that chapter is over,' a student whispered. Their **gold**（黄金）medalists received thunderous applause. The **golden**（金色的）evening light streamed through the windows. On the lawn, someone mentioned a **golf**（高尔夫球）scholarship award. 'Be a **good**（好的）person above all else,' the speaker concluded. Graduates collected their **goods**（货物）from the dormitory, feeling like a mother **goose**（鹅）herding goslings. 'Now you must **govern**（管理）your own lives,' the dean said. A representative from the local **government**（政府）offered congratulations. Each **grade**（年级）had its own stories to tell. As a new **graduate**（毕业生）, Lily felt ready. The **graduation**（毕业）caps flew into the air—a moment of pure freedom.

佩戴白手套的毕业生们列队行进。典礼就像胶水，把多年的记忆粘合在一起。“一旦你们走向世界，要勇敢，”校长说。他开玩笑地提起那只曾经跑进图书馆的学校山羊。“感谢上帝，那段篇章结束了，”一个学生低声说。金牌获得者收获了雷鸣般的掌声。金色的夕阳余晖透过窗户洒进来。草坪上，有人提到了高尔夫球奖学金的事。“首先做一个好的人，”演讲者总结道。毕业生们从宿舍领取自己的物品，像一只母鹅带着小鹅。“现在你们必须管理自己的生活了，”院长说。一位来自地方政府代表表示了祝贺。每个年级都有自己的故事要讲。作为一名新的毕业生，莉莉感到准备好了。学士帽飞向天空——那是纯粹自由的时刻。

---

## Part 120 — Chapter 1: The Grand Family Reunion 盛大的家庭团聚

It was a **grand**（庄重的） **holiday**（假日） at the old family **home**（家）. The **house**（房子） overflowed with warmth as the **grandparents**（祖父母） arrived from their **hometown**（故乡）. **Granny**（奶奶） brought homemade **honey**（蜂蜜）, and her **grandson**（孙子） Tom ran to **greet**（欢迎） her with a warm **hug**（拥抱）. His sister Lily, the **granddaughter**（孙女）, offered a shy **greeting**（问候） to every relative. Their father acted as **host**（主人） while their mother played **hostess**（女主人）, preparing a feast. Even the youngest **grandchild**（孙辈）, baby Amy, giggled with delight. Granny looked at her **husband**（丈夫） and said, 'I spent my life as a **housewife**（家庭主妇） doing endless **housework**（家务）, but seeing our **homeland**（祖国） traditions live on makes it all worthwhile.'

那是一个**庄重的**（grand）**假日**（holiday），在老家的**家**（home）里。**房子**（house）里洋溢着温暖，**祖父母**（grandparents）从**故乡**（hometown）赶来。**奶奶**（granny）带来了自制**蜂蜜**（honey），她的**孙子**（grandson）汤姆跑过去**欢迎**（greet）她，给了她一个温暖的**拥抱**（hug）。他姐姐莉莉作为**孙女**（granddaughter），腼腆地向每位亲戚致以**问候**（greeting）。父亲充当**主人**（host），母亲扮演**女主人**（hostess），准备着盛宴。连最小的**孙辈**（grandchild），小宝宝艾米，都咯咯地笑了。奶奶看着她的**丈夫**（husband），说：'我做了一辈子**家庭主妇**（housewife），做着无尽的**家务**（housework），但看到我们**祖国**（homeland）的传统得以延续，一切都值得了。'

---

## Part 121 — Chapter 2: The Green Grocer's Kitchen 绿色菜贩的厨房

Every morning, the local **greengrocer**（蔬菜水果商） opened his **grocery**（食品杂货店）. He sold fresh **grain**（谷物） by the **gram**（克） and sweet **grape**（葡萄） from his **green**（绿色的） garden. A **hungry**（饥饿的） boy walked in, his **hunger**（饥饿） visible on his face. The kind **grocer**（食品商） handed him a warm **hamburger**（汉堡包）. 'Try a **hot**（热的） **hotdog**（热狗） too, and some cured **ham**（火腿）,' he said. In the back **kitchen**（厨房）, his wife turned on the **grill**（烤架） and set the **kettle**（水壶） to boil. She used a sharp **knife**（刀） to open a **jar**（罐子） of strawberry **jam**（果酱）, then poured a glass of fresh orange **juice**（果汁）. The boy ate until he was full, a smile finally replacing his hunger.

每天早上，当地的**蔬菜水果商**（greengrocer）都会打开他的**食品杂货店**（grocery）。他卖以**克**（gram）计的新鲜**谷物**（grain），以及来自他**绿色的**（green）花园的甜**葡萄**（grape）。一个**饥饿的**（hungry）男孩走进来，脸上的**饥饿**（hunger）显而易见。善良的**食品商**（grocer）递给他一个热乎乎的**汉堡包**（hamburger）。'再尝尝**热的**（hot）**热狗**（hotdog），还有腌制的**火腿**（ham），'他说。在后面**厨房**（kitchen）里，他的妻子打开**烤架**（grill），把**水壶**（kettle）烧上。她用锋利的**刀**（knife）打开一**罐**（jar）草莓**果酱**（jam），又倒了一杯新鲜橙**果汁**（juice）。男孩吃饱了，笑容终于取代了饥饿。

---

## Part 122 — Chapter 3: The School History Project 学校历史项目

Tom walked through the **high**（高的） **hall**（走廊） of his school, heading to meet the **headmistress**（女校长）. She had assigned a special **history**（历史） project — the most important **homework**（家庭作业） of the term. Tom's favourite **hobby**（爱好） was drawing, so he created a colourful **graph**（图表） of ancient trade routes. A **junior**（年少的） student from the **kindergarten**（幼儿园） section asked for help, and Tom was happy to **guide**（指导） him. 'Good **guidance**（指导） makes **knowledge**（知识） grow,' he said. Miss Chen gave clear **instruction**（指令） on using the **keyboard**（键盘） for research. Tom dipped his pen in black **ink**（墨水） to label his map. During the **interval**（间隔） between classes, he searched for more **information**（信息） online. After school, he helped **instruct**（教导） younger students in **gymnastics**（体操）. 'I **know**（知道） you will all do well,' he encouraged them.

汤姆走过学校**高的**（high）**走廊**（hall），去见**女校长**（headmistress）。她布置了一个特殊的**历史**（history）项目——这是本学期最重要的**家庭作业**（homework）。汤姆最喜欢的**爱好**（hobby）是画画，于是他绘制了一张古代贸易路线的彩色**图表**（graph）。一个来自**幼儿园**（kindergarten）的**年少的**（junior）学生来求助，汤姆很乐意**指导**（guide）他。'好的**指导**（guidance）让**知识**（knowledge）成长，'他说。陈老师就如何使用**键盘**（keyboard）做研究给出了清晰的**指令**（instruction）。汤姆用黑**墨水**（ink）给地图标注。在课间**间隔**（interval），他在网上搜索更多**信息**（information）。放学后，他帮忙**教导**（instruct）更小的学生练习**体操**（gymnastics）。'我**知道**（know）你们都会做得很好，'他鼓励道。

---

## Part 123 — Chapter 4: The Hospital Recovery 医院康复记

Mr. Zhang lay in **hospital**（医院）, grateful that his **health**（健康） was slowly returning. His **heart**（心脏） still felt weak, but each day he grew stronger. 'A **healthy**（健康的） diet makes all the difference,' the nurse reminded him. He still suffered from a mild **headache**（头痛） and his **hearing**（听觉） was not perfect after the accident. His bruised **heel**（脚后跟） and swollen **knee**（膝盖） made walking difficult, though his **leg**（腿） was healing well. A patient of similar **height**（身高） limped past his bed. 'The **human**（人类的） body is fragile,' Mr. Zhang reflected. His daughter visited daily. 'Don't let this **hurt**（伤害） your spirit,' she said. 'No permanent **harm**（伤害） was done, but smoking is **harmful**（有害的） — you must stop.' He touched his aching **jaw**（颌）. 'Even minor **illness**（疾病） teaches gratitude,' he said. His wife had cut her **hair**（头发） — a fresh **haircut**（发型） to cheer him up. 'An **injury**（伤） to the body cannot **injure**（伤害） the soul,' she said. 'The **ill**（生病的） need hope more than medicine.'

张先生躺在**医院**（hospital），庆幸**健康**（health）在慢慢恢复。他的**心脏**（heart）仍然虚弱，但一天比一天强壮。'**健康的**（healthy）饮食至关重要，'护士提醒道。他仍然有轻微**头痛**（headache），事故后**听觉**（hearing）也不太好。青肿的**脚后跟**（heel）和肿胀的**膝盖**（knee）让走路困难，但**腿**（leg）恢复得不错。一个同等**身高**（height）的病友蹒跚走过他的床边。'**人类的**（human）身体真脆弱，'张先生沉思道。他女儿每天都来探望。'别让这**伤害**（hurt）你的精神，'她说。'没有永久的**伤害**（harm），但吸烟是**有害的**（harmful）——你必须戒掉。'他摸了摸疼痛的**颌**（jaw）。'即使小**疾病**（illness）也教人感恩，'他说。他妻子剪短了**头发**（hair）——新**发型**（haircut）让他振作起来。'身体的**伤**（injury）无法**伤害**（injure）灵魂，'她说。'**生病的**（ill）人比药更需要希望。'

---

## Part 124 — Chapter 5: The Two Brothers 兄弟俩的故事

Two brothers could not be more different. Jack was **hardworking**（苦干的） and **honest**（诚实的）, while Ben was **lazy**（懒惰的） and **greedy**（贪婪的）. Jack found **happiness**（快乐） in simple work; Ben was never **happy**（快乐的） with what he had. 'It is a **great**（重大的） pity,' their father said with dry **humour**（幽默）. 'One son works, the other dreams.' Their uncle, a **humorous**（幽默的） man, added, 'At least Ben is **interesting**（有趣的） at parties!' Ben felt **guilty**（有罪的） about wasting time. 'I am not **innocent**（无罪的） of laziness,' he admitted. 'But I am **hopeful**（有希望的）, not **hopeless**（绝望的）.' Jack took genuine **interest**（兴趣） in helping his brother change. 'It is my **honour**（荣誉） to support you,' he said. 'There is **joy**（欢喜） in honest work.' Their mother's **laughter**（笑声） filled the house. 'You two make me **laugh**（笑） every day,' she said. The **horrible**（可怕的） old quarrels were finally behind them. Ben no longer felt **hate**（憎恨） toward his own weakness.

两兄弟天差地别。杰克**苦干的**（hardworking）而**诚实的**（honest），本却**懒惰的**（lazy）而**贪婪的**（greedy）。杰克在简单劳动中找到**快乐**（happiness）；本从未对自己拥有的感到**快乐的**（happy）。'这真是**重大的**（great）遗憾，'父亲带着冷淡的**幽默**（humour）说。'一个儿子在干活，另一个在做梦。'他们的叔叔是个**幽默的**（humorous）人，补充道：'至少本在聚会时很**有趣的**（interesting）！'本对浪费光阴感到**有罪的**（guilty）。'我对懒惰不是**无罪的**（innocent），'他承认。'但我**有希望的**（hopeful），不是**绝望的**（hopeless）。'杰克真诚地**兴趣**（interest）帮助弟弟改变。'支持你是我的**荣誉**（honour），'他说。'诚实劳动中有**欢喜**（joy）。'母亲的**笑声**（laughter）充满房间。'你们俩每天都让我**笑**（laugh），'她说。那些**可怕的**（horrible）旧日争吵终于过去了。本不再**憎恨**（hate）自己的软弱。

---

## Part 125 — Chapter 6: The Storm on the Hill 山丘上的暴风雨

A **grey**（灰色的） sky hung over the **ground**（土地）. On the **hill**（小山）, a farmer watched his crops **grow**（生长）. 'The **growth**（成长） this season is remarkable,' he said. His **hen**（母鸡） clucked nervously as the **heat**（热度） rose. A strange **insect**（昆虫） crawled across a **leaf**（树叶）. In the distance, a **lamb**（羔羊） cried for its mother. 'A **hurricane**（飓风） is coming,' the farmer whispered. Dark clouds, **heavy**（沉重的） with rain, rolled over the **lake**（湖）. He checked a deep **hole**（洞） where crops were stored after **harvest**（收获）. Nearby, fragrant **herb**（香草） bent in the wind. Hours later, the storm passed. A rainbow painted the **heaven**（天空）. The farmer stood on his **land**（土地）. 'This **island**（岛） of peace in a wild world,' he murmured. 'Even the **jungle**（丛林） across the water looks calm now.'

**灰色的**（grey）天空低垂在**土地**（ground）上方。在**小山**（hill）上，农夫看着庄稼**生长**（grow）。'这季的**成长**（growth）真是不错，'他说。他的**母鸡**（hen）紧张地咯咯叫着，**热度**（heat）在上升。一只奇怪的**昆虫**（insect）在一片**树叶**（leaf）上爬过。远处，一只**羔羊**（lamb）在呼唤妈妈。'**飓风**（hurricane）要来了，'农夫低语道。**沉重的**（heavy）乌云翻滚着掠过**湖**（lake）面。他检查了一个深**洞**（hole），那里储藏着**收获**（harvest）后的庄稼。附近，芳香的**香草**（herb）在风中弯腰。几小时后，暴风雨过去了。彩虹装点了**天空**（heaven）。农夫站在自己的**土地**（land）上。'这个狂野世界中的宁静之**岛**（island），'他喃喃道。'连对岸的**丛林**（jungle）现在也显得平静了。'

---

## Part 126 — Chapter 7: The Journey to the Inn 前往客栈的旅途

The old **jeep**（吉普车） rumbled along the dusty **highway**（公路）. Tom looked at the map. 'We **left**（离开） the **harbour**（港口） an hour ago,' he said. A **helicopter**（直升机） flew low overhead. At a fork, they turned **left**（在左边） toward a small village **inn**（客栈）. Inside, an **iron**（铁） stove glowed warmly, and the owner hung a **lantern**（灯笼） by the door. 'Here is the **key**（钥匙） to your room,' she said. Outside, a **horse**（马） neighed in the stable. An old **hunter**（猎人） at the bar shared stories. 'I used to **hunt**（狩猎） in these woods. At night, the wolves **howl**（嗥叫）.' Tom heard a bird **hatch**（孵化） from its egg in the rafters. He tried to **hide**（藏） his nervousness. 'Put on your **helmet**（头盔） for the mountain trail tomorrow,' his friend said. 'You can **lead**（带领） the way.' This was no ordinary **hotel**（旅馆）. A **kangaroo**（袋鼠）-shaped shadow hopped past. 'This place runs on mystery, not **jet**（喷射流） fuel,' Tom whispered.

旧**吉普车**（jeep）沿着尘土飞扬的**公路**（highway）隆隆行驶。汤姆看着地图。'我们一小时前就**离开**（left）了**港口**（harbour），'他说。一架**直升机**（helicopter）低空飞过。在一个岔路口，他们向**左**（left）转，朝一家小村庄**客栈**（inn）驶去。里面，**铁**（iron）炉温暖地发着光，店主在门边挂了一盏**灯笼**（lantern）。'这是你房间的**钥匙**（key），'她说。外面，一匹**马**（horse）在马厩里嘶鸣。吧台边一位老**猎人**（hunter）分享着故事。'我曾在这片林子里**狩猎**（hunt）。夜里，狼群**嗥叫**（howl）。'汤姆听到一只鸟在房梁上**孵化**（hatch）出来。他试图**藏**（hide）起紧张。'明天走山路要戴上**头盔**（helmet），'朋友说。'你可以**带领**（lead）。'这不是普通的**旅馆**（hotel）。一个**袋鼠**（kangaroo）形状的影子从窗外跳过。'这地方靠神秘运转，而非**喷射流**（jet）燃料，'汤姆低语。

---

## Part 127 — Chapter 8: The Courtroom Verdict 法庭判决

The **judge**（法官） entered, and the courtroom fell silent. A young woman's **identity**（身份） had been stolen. Her **lawyer**（律师） argued that evidence would **indicate**（表明） her innocence. 'The **legal**（合法的） process must be fair,' the judge said. A **guard**（守卫） stood by as the prosecutor claimed **illegal**（非法的） transactions had occurred. 'We **guarantee**（保证） a fair trial,' the judge continued. The woman's **insurance**（保险） company had refused to **insure**（投保） her against fraud. 'This **institution**（机构） exists for **justice**（正义）,' the lawyer declared. 'My client values her **independence**（独立） and is fully **independent**（独立的） in all matters.' The police returned to **inspect**（检查） the evidence. A detective with sharp **intelligence**（智力） found the real criminal, who was carrying a **gun**（枪）. 'I **invite**（邀请） the jury to consider these facts,' the judge said. She sent an **invitation**（邀请） to a key witness to testify. During the **interview**（面试） with the suspect, the truth finally emerged. Justice had won.

**法官**（judge）走进来，法庭安静下来。一名年轻女子的**身份**（identity）被盗用了。她的**律师**（lawyer）辩称证据将**表明**（indicate）她的清白。'**合法的**（legal）程序必须公正，'法官说。一名**守卫**（guard）站在一旁，检察官声称发生了**非法的**（illegal）交易。'我们**保证**（guarantee）审判公正，'法官继续说道。该女子的**保险**（insurance）公司拒绝为她**投保**（insure）防欺诈险。'这个**机构**（institution）为**正义**（justice）而存在，'律师说。'我的当事人珍视她的**独立**（independence），在所有事务上都是**独立的**（independent）。'警察再次**检查**（inspect）证据。一名有敏锐**智力**（intelligence）的侦探找到了真正的罪犯，那人带着一把**枪**（gun）。'我**邀请**（invite）陪审团考虑这些事实，'法官说。她向关键证人发出了作证的**邀请**（invitation）。在对嫌疑人的**面试**（interview）中，真相终于浮出水面。正义赢了。

---

## Part 128 — Chapter 9: The Young Inventor 年轻发明家

Lily wanted to **invent**（发明） something meaningful. Her **invention**（发明） was a water-saving device for farms. A **leader**（领导者） in the **industry**（产业） agreed to **hire**（雇用） her as an intern. 'This **job**（工作） will help **increase**（增加） your family's **income**（收入）,' her mother said. Lily understood the **importance**（重要性） of dedication. She wanted to **impress**（使有印象） her boss and leave a lasting **impression**（印象）. The company planned to **import**（进口） new materials from abroad. A **journalist**（记者） came to cover her story. 'I **intend**（打算） to make clean water accessible to all,' Lily said. 'That is my **intention**（意图）.' Her mentor helped **introduce**（介绍） her to investors. 'Let me give a brief **introduction**（介绍） of the project,' she began. Her story would **influence**（影响） many young scientists. The company promised to **inform**（通知） her of any progress. 'All your **labour**（劳动） will pay off,' her father said with pride.

莉莉想**发明**（invent）一些有意义的东西。她的**发明**（invention）是一个农场节水装置。该**产业**（industry）的一位**领导者**（leader）同意**雇用**（hire）她做实习生。'这份**工作**（job）能帮助**增加**（increase）家里的**收入**（income），'母亲说。莉莉明白奉献的**重要性**（importance）。她想**使有印象**（impress）她的老板，留下持久的**印象**（impression）。公司计划从国外**进口**（import）新材料。一名**记者**（journalist）来报道她的故事。'我**打算**（intend）让清洁水惠及所有人，'莉莉说。'这就是我的**意图**（intention）。'导师帮她将项目**介绍**（introduce）给投资人。'我先做一个简短的**介绍**（introduction），'她开始说。她的故事将**影响**（influence）许多年轻科学家。公司承诺会**通知**（inform）她任何进展。'你所有的**劳动**（labour）都会有回报，'父亲自豪地说。

---

## Part 129 — Chapter 10: The Handy Repair Shop 灵巧的修理铺

Mr. Wang's repair shop could fix anything. His skilled **hand**（手） mended a torn **handbag**（手提包） in minutes. A customer handed him a stained **handkerchief**（手帕） and broken **jewellery**（珠宝）. 'Be **handy**（便利的） with your tools,' he told his apprentice. A young woman brought a **leather**（皮革） **jacket**（夹克） and faded **jeans**（牛仔裤） for repair. Mr. Wang grabbed his **hammer**（锤子）, hung a **hat**（帽子） on a **hook**（钩子）, and said, '**Hang**（悬挂） your coat there.' The work was **hard**（困难的）, and he **hardly**（几乎不） rested. 'Only a **handful**（少数） of orders left,' he said. He cut a strap into **half**（一半）. 'Let me **handle**（处理） this clasp,' he murmured, gripping the door **handle**（把手）. 'Your **help**（帮助） means so much,' the customer said. 'You have been so **helpful**（有帮助的）. I was in a real **hurry**（匆忙） to get everything fixed before my trip!'

王师傅的修理铺什么都能修。他灵巧的**手**（hand）几分钟就修好了一个破损的**手提包**（handbag）。一位顾客递给他一条弄脏的**手帕**（handkerchief）和坏掉的**珠宝**（jewellery）。'工具要用得**便利的**（handy），'他对学徒说。一位年轻女子拿来了**皮革**（leather）**夹克**（jacket）和褪色的**牛仔裤**（jeans）来修。老王拿起**锤子**（hammer），把一顶**帽子**（hat）挂在**钩子**（hook）上，说：'把外套**悬挂**（hang）在那里。'工作很**困难的**（hard），他**几乎不**（hardly）休息。'只剩**少数**（handful）订单了，'他说。他把一条带子切成**一半**（half）。'让我**处理**（handle）这个搭扣，'他嘟囔着，紧握着门的**把手**（handle）。'你的**帮助**（help）太重要了，'顾客说。'你一直很**有帮助的**（helpful）。我真的很**匆忙**（hurry），想在旅行前修好所有东西！'

---

## Part 130 — Chapter 11: The Language Exchange 语言交流会

At the student centre, a **language**（语言） exchange was underway. Tom met a girl who taught him a Chinese **idiom**（成语）. '**Hello**（你好）,' she smiled. '**Hi**（嗨）!' Tom replied. '**How**（怎样） do you say dream in Chinese?' 'Mengxiang,' she said. '**However**（然而）, let's start with basics.' Tom could **hear**（听到） voices in six different languages. The event made the front-page **headline**（大标题）. An **interpreter**（口译者） helped students communicate. Tom tried a **joke**（笑话） in broken Chinese. '**Guess**（猜测） what I just said!' 'These exchanges **happen**（发生） every month,' Lily explained. 'The **idea**（想法） is to build bridges.' Tom did not **hesitate**（犹豫） to try speaking, even when he made mistakes. A nervous student tried to **interrupt**（打断）, but Tom encouraged her. 'I **insist**（坚持） you try too,' he said warmly. 'Never **ignore**（忽视） a chance to learn. That is what **inspire**（鼓舞）s me most.'

在学生中心，一场**语言**（language）交流活动正在进行。汤姆遇到一个女孩，她教了他一个中文**成语**（idiom）。'**你好**（hello），'她微笑着。'**嗨**（hi）！'汤姆回答。'梦想用中文**怎样**（how）说？''梦想，'她说。'**然而**（however），我们先从基础开始吧。'汤姆能**听到**（hear）六种不同语言的声音。这个活动登上了校报的头版**大标题**（headline）。一名**口译者**（interpreter）帮助学生沟通。汤姆用蹩脚的中文开了个**笑话**（joke）。'**猜测**（guess）我刚说了什么！''这些交流每月都**发生**（happen），'莉莉解释道。'**想法**（idea）是建立沟通的桥梁。'汤姆毫不**犹豫**（hesitate）地尝试开口，即使会犯错。一个紧张的学生试图**打断**（interrupt），但汤姆鼓励了她。'我**坚持**（insist）你也试试，'他热情地说。'永远不要**忽视**（ignore）学习的机会。这就是最**鼓舞**（inspire）我的事。'

---

## Part 131 — Chapter 12: The Lost Traveller 迷路的旅人

A traveller reached a village and met a **group**（组） of locals. 'I **have**（有） a problem,' **he**（他） said. 'I **grasp**（把握） the map, but I have a **habit**（习惯） of getting lost.' A **handsome**（英俊的） young man stepped forward. '**He**（他） is the village **head**（头）,' someone whispered. '**His**（他的） name is Jack. **He** calls **himself**（他自己） a guide.' Jack looked at **her**（她的） — no, wait. The traveller was male. Let me fix: A young woman traveller arrived. She looked at the map. 'This path is **hers**（她的） to follow,' Jack said of the woman. 'She can do it **herself**（她自己） if she trusts the road.' The traveller said of Jack: 'I will follow **him**（他） wherever he leads.' 'The village values **harmony**（和谐） above all,' Jack explained. 'We see every stranger as a potential **hero**（英雄）.' They passed a **huge**（巨大的） **holy**（神圣的） temple. 'Wait half an **hour**（小时） here,' Jack said. 'A **hydrogen**（氢） balloon marks the festival grounds. You will find your way.'

一位旅行者到达一个村庄，遇到了一**组**（group）当地人。'我**有**（have）一个问题，'她说。'我**把握**（grasp）了地图，但我有迷路的**习惯**（habit）。'一个**英俊的**（handsome）年轻人走上前来。'**他**（he）是村里的**头**（head），'有人低语。'**他的**（his）名字叫杰克。**他**称**他自己**（himself）为向导。'杰克看着地图说：'这条路是**她的**（hers）——让她**她自己**（herself）来走。'女旅行者看着杰克说：'不管**他**（him）带我去哪，我都跟着。'村庄最看重**和谐**（harmony），'杰克解释道。'我们视每位陌生人为潜在的**英雄**（hero）。'他们经过一座**巨大的**（huge）**神圣的**（holy）寺庙。'在这等半个**小时**（hour），'杰克说。'一个**氢**（hydrogen）气球标记着节日的场地。你会找到路的。'

## Part 132 — Chapter 13: The Science Fair 科学展览会

At the **international**（国际的） science fair, students from every **institute**（学院） gathered. Tom walked **into**（进入） the main hall and took a seat **in**（在……之内） the front row. 'This **is**（是） the moment,' he thought. He watched a presenter **insert**（插入） a chip into a new **instrument**（仪器）. '**It**（它） can analyse water quality in an **instant**（瞬间）,' the presenter said. The device used **irrigation**（灌溉） sensors to help **improve**（改进） farming. 'We must **include**（包括） rural communities,' Tom noted. '**Instead**（反而） of just talking, we need action.' He recalled his **initial**（最初的） prototype, which was only an **inch**（英寸） wide. '**Hold**（把握） onto your dreams,' a professor told him. 'The **immigration**（移民） of ideas across borders drives progress.' Tom felt a sudden **hit**（打击） of inspiration and looked **inside**（内部） the device once more, understanding it fully.

在**国际的**（international）科学展览会上，来自各个**学院**（institute）的学生齐聚一堂。汤姆走**进**（into）主厅，坐**在**（in）前排。'这**是**（is）那个时刻，'他想。他看着一位展示者将芯片**插入**（insert）一台新**仪器**（instrument）。'**它**（it）能在**瞬间**（instant）分析水质，'展示者说。该设备利用**灌溉**（irrigation）传感器来**改进**（improve）农业。'我们必须**包括**（include）农村社区，'汤姆说。'**反而**（instead）空谈，我们需要行动。'他想起自己**最初的**（initial）原型，只有一**英寸**（inch）宽。'**把握**（hold）住你的梦想，'一位教授对他说。'思想的**移民**（immigration）跨越国界推动进步。'汤姆感到一阵灵感的**打击**（hit），再次看向设备**内部**（inside），终于完全理解了它。
