## Part 163 — 14. Into the Wild 深入野外

We tracked a wild **rabbit**（兔子）. The species **race**（种族）here amazed us. We studied **racial**（种族的）diversity in nature. Scientists measured **radiation**（辐射）with a **radio**（无线电）device. Some rocks were **radioactive**（放射性的）—**radium**（镭）had been found nearby. I cleaned the lens with a **rag**（抹布）. We walked along the **rail**（铁轨）of an old **railway**（铁路）. Suddenly, **rain**（雨）began. A **rainbow**（彩虹）appeared. I put on my **raincoat**（雨衣）as **rainfall**（降雨量）rose. It was a **rainy**（下雨的）afternoon. We had to **raise**（抬高）the camera. We took **random**（随机的）samples from the mountain **range**（山脉）. We noted the species **rank**（等级）. The rabbit was **rapid**（迅速的）—too fast to film.

追踪野生**兔子**。这里物种**种族**令人惊叹。研究自然中**种族的**多样性。科学家用**无线电**设备测**辐射**。有些岩石是**放射性的**——附近曾发现**镭**。用**抹布**清洁镜头。沿旧**铁路**的**铁轨**前行。突然下**雨**。**彩虹**出现。随**降雨量**增大穿上了**雨衣**。**下雨的**下午。不得不**抬高**摄像机。从**山脉**取**随机的**样本。记录物种**等级**。兔子极其**迅速的**——太快了抓拍不到。

---

## Part 164 — 15. The Old Radio 老收音机

Grandpa's **rare**（稀罕的）radio had been broken—a **rat**（鼠）chewed the wire. At any **rate**（比率）, I would **rather**（宁可）repair it than give up. A **ray**（光线）lit the workbench. I used a **razor**（剃刀）to strip the cord. I could barely **reach**（够到）the broken part. Thankfully, I had **read**（读过）repair guides—my **reading**（阅读）paid off. I was **ready**（准备好的）. This was a **real**（真正的）test. I didn't **realise**（意识到）how hard it was. I **really**（真的）wanted it to work. The **reason**（理由）was sentimental. A **reasonable**（合理的）person might quit, but I chose to **rebuild**（重建）the circuit. The parts store gave me a **receipt**（收据）. I switched it on to **receive**（接收）a signal. The **receiver**（接收器）hummed—and music filled the room!

祖父**稀罕的**收音机坏了——**鼠**咬断了电线。**无论如何**，**宁可**修好也不放弃。**光线**照在工作台。用**剃刀**剥开电线。几乎**够到**损坏部件。幸好**读过**维修手册——**阅读**派上用场。**准备好的**。这是**真正的**考验。没**意识到**有多难。但**真的**想修好它。**理由**是情感上的。**合理的**人可能会放弃，但选择**重建**电路。零件店给了**收据**。打开来**接收**信号。**接收器**嗡嗡作响——音乐充满房间！

---

## Part 165 — 科技创新展

At the **recent**（最近的） technology expo, I went to the **reception**（接待） desk, where a friendly **receptionist**（接待员） handed me a brochure. I grabbed my voice **recorder**（记录员） to **record**（记录） every detail. A scientist demonstrated a smart **refrigerator**（电冰箱） that could **recognize**（认出） food items and suggest a **recipe**（食谱）. A student prepared to **recite**（背诵） her speech on using **rectangle**（长方形） solar panels to **reduce**（减少） energy waste, and she would **refer**（提交） to her notes. She also explained how to **recycle**（使再循环） old electronics. A robot soccer match was in progress—the **referee**（仲裁人） blew the whistle and a **red**（红的） card appeared. I visited the **recreation**（娱乐） area to **refresh**（使清新） myself. Looking around, I began to **reflect**（反射） on how innovation can **reform**（改革） society. I hope to **recover**（重新获得） from my cold quickly so I can come again.

在最近的科技展上，我走到接待处，一位友好的接待员递给我一本小册子。我拿起录音机记录每一个细节。一位科学家展示了一台智能冰箱，它能识别食物种类并推荐食谱。一名学生准备背诵她的演讲，主题是用长方形太阳能板减少能源浪费，她会参考自己的笔记。她还解释了如何回收旧电子产品。旁边正在进行机器人足球赛——裁判吹哨，一张红牌出现了。我去娱乐区让自己恢复精神。环顾四周，我开始反思创新如何改革社会。希望我能早日从感冒中恢复，再来参观。

---

## Part 166 — 家族团圆饭

At the Spring Festival dinner, every **relative**（亲戚） gathered. Uncle Li had a **regular**（规则的） habit of arriving late—we had long stopped feeling **regret**（遗憾）. He brought a **religion**（宗教） book, sparking discussion. Though some were **religious**（宗教性的） and others not, they showed **regard**（关心） for different beliefs. People shared stories—their deep **relationship**（关系） with one another was the heart of it all. 'Family is about how we **relate**（讲） to each other,' Grandma said, and everyone could **relation**（关系）. **regardless**（不管） of past quarrels, we must stay united—that is our family **regulation**（规则）. We all had to **register**（寄存器） names in the guest book; Grandpa would **reject**（被拒之人） anyone who did not. My little brother tried to **refuse**（拒绝） to eat vegetables, but Mom insisted. It is a **relevant**（有关联的） topic for every gathering. I truly **rely**（信赖） on my grandparents—they are the most **reliable**（可靠的） people I know. After dinner, we **relax**（放松） on the sofa, watching TV and **relay**（驿马）ing messages from absent family members. Some traditions may change, but love will always **remain**（保持）.

年夜饭上，每个亲戚都聚齐了。李叔叔有一个规则性的迟到习惯——我们早已不再感到遗憾。他带来了一本宗教书，引发了讨论。虽然有人虔诚有人不，但大家彼此表示尊敬。大家分享故事——他们彼此之间深厚的关系是聚会的核心。奶奶说：'家庭在于我们如何相互关联，'大家都能叙述自己的故事。不管过去的争吵，我们必须团结——这是我们的家规。我们都要在来宾簿上登记名字，爷爷会拒绝任何不登记的人。弟弟试图拒绝吃蔬菜，但妈妈坚持要他吃。这是每次聚会都相关的话题。我真心依赖我的祖父母——他们是我认识的最可靠的人。晚饭后，我们在沙发上放松，收看并转达缺席家人的消息。有些传统会变，但爱将永远保持。

---

## Part 167 — 校园记者团

As a student **reporter**（记者）, I was asked to write a **report**（报告） on the new library policy. Let me **remark**（评论） on something first: I still **remember**（记得） my first assignment. I had to **research**（研究） old newspapers and **repair**（修理） the interview three times when my recorder failed. I needed to **remove**（移动） outdated files from the archive and borrow equipment we could not **rent**（租金）. The principal's **reply**（答复） to my interview **request**（请求） arrived. The school promised to **replace**（代替） old desks and **repeat**（重复） broken chairs. Our class **representative**（代表） will **represent**（表现） us at the meeting. The new policy will **require**（需要） every student to meet basic fitness **requirement**（需求）s. The student union, like a mini **republic**（共和国）, voted to **rescue**（援救） after-school clubs. My article should **resemble**（相似） a professional piece. I made a **reservation**（保留） for the interview room. Everything was finally in place.

作为学生记者，我被要求写一篇关于新图书馆政策的报告。让我先评论一件事：我还记得我的第一次任务。我不得不研究旧报纸，录音机坏了的时候把采访重复了三遍。我还需要从档案中移除过时的文件，还要租用我们租不起的设备。校长对我采访请求的答复终于来了。学校承诺替换旧课桌并修理破损的椅子。我们的班级代表将代表我们出席会议。新政策将要求每个学生达到基本体能要求标准。学生会像一个小共和国一样投票决定拯救课外社团。我的文章应该像一篇专业作品。我预约了采访室。一切终于就绪。

---

## Part 168 — 历史研究项目

Our history group chose to study the **revolution**（革命） that shaped our nation. We had to **retell**（再讲） the stories of heroes who dared to **resist**（抵抗） tyranny. I volunteered to **reserve**（储备品） the library room, and we promised to **respect**（尊敬） every team member's opinion. Tom wanted to **resign**（辞职） as leader, but after some **rest**（休息）, he agreed to stay. The **result**（结果） of our archival search was amazing: letters from soldiers longing to **return**（回来） home. During a **review**（检讨） session, we found the draft needed **revision**（校订）. Everyone had to **respond**（以） to feedback. The library imposed a **restriction**（限制） on old manuscripts—no one could borrow them. We had to **rewind**（重绕） the microfilm by hand! We even wrote a **rhyme**（韵） to memorize key dates. On the last day, the others chose to **retire**（隐居） to the cafeteria, but I stayed to finish. One book described how **rice**（米） became a staple in a **rich**（富裕的） kingdom, and an ancient **riddle**（谜）—a clever puzzle—that sounded **ridiculous**（荒谬的） yet taught a deep lesson.

我们历史小组选择研究那场塑造了国家的革命。我们必须重新讲述那些敢于抵抗暴政的英雄故事。我自愿预定图书馆房间，我们承诺尊重每位成员的意见。汤姆想辞去组长一职，但稍作休息后，他同意留下了。我们档案搜索的结果令人惊叹：找到了渴望返回家乡的士兵们的信件。在一次回顾会议上，我们发现草稿需要修订。每个人都必须回应反馈。图书馆对旧手稿有限制——谁也不能借走。我们只能用手倒回微缩胶卷！我们甚至写了一首押韵诗来记忆关键日期。最后一天，其他人都退去食堂了，我却留下来完成。一本书描述了水稻如何在一个富饶王国成为主食，还讲了一个古老的谜语——一个听起来荒谬却蕴含深意的巧妙谜题。

---

## Part 169 — 山间村落

Grandpa took me on a bicycle **ride**（骑马） down a narrow mountain **road**（路）. We stopped at a wooden bridge over a rushing **river**（河）. He pointed to the **right**（权利） path: 'That is the **root**（根） of our family.' Huge **rock**（岩石） formations stood like ancient guards. 'There is always **risk**（冒险） in the mountains,' he warned. We passed a **ripe**（成熟的） fruit tree. 'Wait for the fruit to fully **ripen**（使成熟）,' he said. A **rooster**（公鸡） crowed on a fence, and a coil of **rope**（绳） hung from a post. Grandpa spoke about his **role**（角色） in building this community—he once dreamed of designing a **rocket**（火箭）! The sun began to **rise**（上升） over the peak. A **rigid**（坚硬的） old system once governed life here, but progress cannot be stopped. I found an old **ring**（环） in the grass. Back home, the **room**（房间） was filled with the smell of a **roast**（烤肉） dinner. 'Never let anyone **rob**（抢夺） you of your dreams,' Grandpa said. He took a **roll**（卷） of bread and smiled. I would treasure this day forever.

爷爷骑自行车带我在狭窄的山路上骑行。我们在一条湍急河流的木桥上停了下来。他指向右边的小路：'那是我们家族的根。'巨大的岩石像古老的守卫矗立着。'山里总有风险，'他警告道。我们经过一棵成熟的果树。'等果子完全成熟，'他说。一只公鸡在篱笆上啼叫，一卷绳子挂在柱子上。爷爷谈起他在建设这个社区中的角色——他曾梦想设计一枚火箭！太阳开始从山巅升起。一套僵化的旧制度曾统治这里，但进步无法阻挡。我在草丛中发现了一枚旧戒指。回到屋里，房间里弥漫着烤肉晚餐的香气。'永远不要让人夺走你的梦想，'爷爷说。他拿起一卷面包，微笑起来。我将永远珍藏这一天。

---

## Part 170 — 中世纪城堡

The castle stood on a **rough**（粗糙的东西） hilltop. A single **rose**（玫瑰） bush by the gate was the only beauty amid the **ruin**（毁灭）. Inside, wooden beams had begun to **rot**（腐烂）. The **royal**（王室） hall—once glorious—was now empty, and remembering its past felt like a **sacred**（神圣的） duty. A **round**（圆） table sat in the center where knights once gathered. The **ruler**（统治者） of this land had established one **rule**（规则）: protect the innocent. On the floor lay a piece of old **rubber**（橡皮）, and scattered **rubbish**（废物） left by careless tourists made me frown. How **rude**（粗鲁无礼的）! Near the **roundabout**（迂回的） path behind the castle, a field served as a **safety**（安全） zone. Though once a place of **sadness**（悲哀）, the castle is now a **safe**（保险箱） refuge for historians. A **row**（排） of birds broke the silence. I smiled, imagining knights playing **rugby**（橄榄球） on the green—a different era indeed. One day, this place will **run**（跑） again as a museum. A white **sail**（帆） on the horizon signaled the return of a research vessel, bringing new hope.

城堡矗立在粗糙的山顶上。大门旁一丛玫瑰是废墟中唯一的美丽。城堡内，木梁已经开始腐烂。皇家大厅——昔日辉煌——如今空空荡荡，追忆往事仿佛一项神圣的职责。大厅中央摆着一张圆桌，骑士们曾围聚于此。这片土地的统治者曾立下一条规则：保护无辜。地板上有一块旧橡胶，散落的垃圾——粗心游客留下的——让我皱眉。多么粗鲁！城堡后迂回的小路旁，一片田野曾是安全地带。虽曾是悲伤之地，如今城堡却是历史学家安全的避风港。一阵鸟儿的吵闹打破了寂静。我笑了，想象骑士们曾在草地上打橄榄球——那确实是另一个时代。有一天，这里将重新运转为博物馆。地平线上的一面白帆标志着研究船的归来，带来了新的希望。

---

## Part 171 — 厨房大赛

The cooking contest took place at a seaside hotel. A former **sailor**（水手） turned chef prepared a fresh **salad**（色拉） with a secret **salt**（盐） mix—surprisingly **salty**（有盐分的） yet delicious. His helper, a young **salesgirl**（女店员） from a grocery store, managed ticket **sale**（出售）s while dreaming of a better **salary**（薪水）. The **same**（相同的） judges from last year returned. They sat at a table decorated with white **sand**（沙） and ate a quick **sandwich**（三明治） before judging began. A **satellite**（人造卫星） TV crew broadcast the event live. The chef wanted to **satisfy**（使满意） every judge. He placed the **sausage**（香肠） on a delicate **saucer**（茶碟） and whispered, 'I must **save**（救球） the best for last.' His mentor used to **say**（说）, 'Old **saying**（叙述） goes: patience is a cook's best friend.' He used a thermometer to **scan**（审视） the dish, then hid a **scar**（疤痕） on his hand—a reminder of his first kitchen burn. The judges gave a **salute**（行礼） to the chef—a respectful bow of approval. He had finally reached his dream.

烹饪大赛在海滨酒店举行。一位曾当过水手的厨师准备了一份新鲜色拉，配以秘制盐料——出奇地咸却十分美味。他的助手——一位杂货店的年轻女店员——负责售票，同时梦想着更好的薪水。同样的评委们去年也来了。他们坐在铺着白沙装饰的桌旁，评审开始前匆匆吃了一个三明治。卫星电视摄制组直播了这场比赛。厨师想满足每一位评委。他把香肠放在精致的茶碟上，低声说：'我必须把最好的留到最后。'他的导师常说：'有句老话：耐心是厨师最好的朋友。'他用温度计扫描菜品，然后藏起手上的疤痕——那是对第一次厨房烫伤的提醒。评委们向厨师致意——一个充满敬意的认可之礼。他终于实现了梦想。

---

## Part 172 — 艺术学校

The **school**（学校） hallway hummed with activity. A dramatic **scene**（场） drew everyone's attention: Lily's **sculpture**（雕刻） had fallen and shattered. She grabbed **scissors**（剪刀） and glue, determined to fix it. Behind her, a **scenery**（风景） painting showed the calm blue **sea**（海）. Her **schoolmate**（同窗） Jack was **sceptical**（怀疑论的）: 'Can you really repair that?' But Professor Wang, a respected **scholar**（学者）, believed the piece could win a **scholarship**（学问）. A visiting **scientist**（科学家） praised the class for blending **science**（科学） with art—a **scientific**（科学的） method. Lily opened her **schoolbag**（书包） for tools. 'Do not **scold**（好骂街的人） yourself,' Jack said. 'Your last art **score**（得分） was the best.' She used a file to **scratch**（抓痕） off extra glue, then gave a tiny **scream**（尖叫声） when her sleeve got stuck. It was nothing to **scare**（惊吓） about. In the end, the repaired piece was even more beautiful.

教学楼走廊里一片忙碌。一个戏剧性的场景吸引了所有人的注意：莉莉的雕塑摔碎了。她抓起剪刀和胶水，决心修好它。她身后，一幅风景画描绘着平静的蓝色大海。她的同学杰克持怀疑态度：'你真的能修好？'但王教授——一位受人尊敬的学者——相信这件作品能赢得奖学金。一位来访的科学家称赞班级将科学与艺术结合——一种科学的方法。莉莉打开书包找工具。'别责骂自己，'杰克说，'你上次艺术得分是最高的。'她用锉刀刮掉多余的胶水，当袖子被粘住时发出一声小小的尖叫。这没什么可吓到的。最终，修复后的作品更加美丽了。

---

## Part 173 — 海滩发现

A group of students went to the **seaside**（海边的） for a field trip. **seagull**（鸥） cries echoed overhead as they settled on the sand. Amy found a delicate **seashell**（海贝壳） at the water's edge, while strands of **seaweed**（海草） washed ashore nearby. David claimed he knew a **secret**（秘密）—the location of a **seal**（海豹） colony. Everyone started to **seek**（寻求） and **search**（搜寻） the coastline. They discovered a hidden cove and took their **seat**（座）s on the warm rocks. There they were—seals! Someone tried to **seize**（抓住） the moment with a photo. The club **secretary**（秘书） carefully noted the finding. She wrote that this **section**（区域） of the beach needed better **security**（安全） measures. This was only the **second**（第二次） sighting in years. 'Can you **see**（看见） them?' a younger student asked. She could clearly **see**（看见） the seals through binoculars. A gardener nearby showed them how to plant a **seed**（种子） in sandy soil. Tim, who **seldom**（很少） spoke up, said, 'I want to learn.' The teacher helped everyone **select**（选择） the best pictures. 'Sometimes you must lose your **self**（自我） in nature to find yourself,' she said. It may **seem**（似乎） a simple trip—but it was unforgettable.

一群学生去海滨野外考察。海鸥的叫声在上空回荡，他们在沙滩上安顿下来。艾米在水边发现了一枚精致的海贝壳，而不远处海藻被冲上了岸。大卫声称他知道一个秘密——一处海豹栖息地。大家开始寻找并搜寻海岸线。他们发现了一个隐蔽的小海湾，在温暖的岩石上坐下。它们就在那里——海豹！有人试图抓住时机拍照。社团秘书仔细记录了这一发现。她写道这片海滩区域需要更好的安全措施。这是多年来仅有的第二次目击。'你能看到它们吗？'一个年幼的学生问。她用望远镜清楚地看到了海豹。附近一位园丁教他们如何在沙土中播下种子。很少发言的提姆说：'我想学。'老师帮大家选择最好的照片。'有时你须在大自然中放下自我，才能找到自己，'她说。这似乎是一次简单的旅行——却令人难以忘怀。

---

## Part 174 — 模拟法庭

The mock court was in **session**（期间）. The case involved a **serious**（严肃的） accusation of theft. A **senior**（年长者） student wore the judge's robe and read the **sentence**（句子） solemnly. The defendant, accused of trying to **sell**（卖） stolen artwork, sat in the **shadow**（阴影） of the room. The art club insisted the painting was **separate**（独立件） property—belonging to the club, not the individual. A special **seminar**（研究班） had been held on the **separation**（分离） of personal and collective ownership. A witness—who worked as a **servant**（仆人） at the gallery—said the piece was found in a **shabby**（衣衫褴褛的） storage cabinet. The jury sat in a **semicircle**（半圆形）. The mediator, acting as **settler**（移民者）, suggested a fair **settlement**（安顿）. **several**（几个的） pieces of evidence supported the defendant's claim. The case also raised a question about **sex**（性别）—the defendant was a young woman facing bias. A **severe**（严厉的） punishment seemed unlikely. The defendant's sister had helped **sew**（缝纫） the canvas backing. Both sides agreed to **settle**（有背长椅） the dispute outside court. The judge declared: 'Let this case **set**（日落） a standard for future art disputes.' A fair outcome was reached—one that considered all perspectives equally.

模拟法庭正在开庭。案件涉及一项严重的盗窃指控。一位高年级学生穿上法官袍，庄严宣读了判决。被告被指控试图出售偷来的艺术品，坐在房间的阴影中。艺术社团坚持说这幅画是分开的财产——属于社团而非个人。学校曾就个人与集体所有权的分离问题举办过专题研讨会。一位在画廊当服务员的目击者说，那件作品是在一个破旧的储藏柜里发现的。陪审团以半圆形围坐。调解人——作为解决者——提出了一个公平的解决方案。几项证据支持了被告的说法。此案还涉及性别问题——被告是一位面临偏见的年轻女性。严厉的惩罚似乎不太可能。被告的姐姐曾帮忙缝制画布的衬底。双方同意在庭外解决纠纷。法官宣布：'愿此案为未来艺术纠纷树立标准。'达成了一个公平的结果——考虑到了所有视角。

---

## Part 175 — 农场生活

Grandma's gentle **shake**（摇动） woke me at dawn. 'You **shall**（将） help with the **sheep**（羊） today,' **she**（她） said. I walked past the **shallow**（水浅的地方） pond and climbed the hill where **sheep**（羊） grazed. It was a **shame**（羞耻） I had slept so late. I grabbed a **sharp**（半升音调） knife to **sharpen**（使尖锐） the fence posts—my **sharpener**（研磨者） was ready in my pocket. Grandpa, an experienced **shaver**（修面的人）, grinned at my effort. My sister hung a clean **sheet**（床单） on the line. The sun began to **shine**（照耀） brightly, outlining the **shape**（形状） of a **shark**（鲨鱼）—wait, just a big fish leaping in the pond! We all **share**（部分） the work here. The old barn is a **shelter**（庇护所） for animals during storms. A delivery **ship**（船） arrived from town. I wore my favorite **shirt**（衬衫） and sturdy **shoe**（鞋）. A sudden **shock**（震动）—thunder! But the sun soon returned. Life on the farm was simple, but full of meaning.

奶奶轻轻的摇动在黎明唤醒了我。'你今天将帮忙照看羊群，'她说。我走过浅浅的池塘，爬上羊群吃草的山坡。真羞愧我起得这么晚。我拿起一把锋利的刀来削尖篱笆桩——磨刀器就在口袋里准备好了。爷爷——一位有经验的修面人——对着我的努力咧嘴笑了。姐姐在晾衣绳上挂了一张干净的床单。太阳开始明亮地照耀，勾勒出一个鲨鱼的形状——等等，只是池塘里跃起的一条大鱼！我们在这里分享所有的工作。老谷仓是暴风雨时动物们的庇护所。一辆送货船——不，一辆卡车从镇上来了。我穿着最喜欢的衬衫和结实的鞋。一阵突然的震动——雷声！但太阳很快又出来了。农场生活虽然简单，却充满意义。

---

## Part 176 — 城市一日

Lily visited a cozy **shop**（商店） run by an elderly **shopkeeper**（零售商）. She loved **shopping**（买东西） there every Friday after class. Today, she noticed a **shortcoming**（缺点） in her new bag—the strap was fraying, and she regretted buying it so **shortly**（不久） before. She changed into **shorts**（短裤） and walked toward the **shore**（海岸）. On the basketball court, a player made a perfect **shot**（发射）. 'You **should**（应该） join us!' he called, tapping his **shoulder**（肩）. Lily smiled but was too **shy**（惊跳） to respond. She continued along the **side**（旁边） of the road, then turned onto a quiet **sideroad**（旁路） leading to the hills. Dark clouds rolled in. A sudden **shower**（阵雨） began—she hurried to **shut**（关闭） her bag. A free **shuttle**（穿梭） bus stopped nearby and she hopped aboard. A passenger let out a loud **shout**（呼喊） as lightning flashed. Feeling a bit **sick**（病人） from the bumpy ride, she remembered her motion **sickness**（疾病） pills at home. When the rain stopped, she stepped off and took a deep breath. The city had never smelled so fresh.

莉莉去了一家由年迈店主经营的温馨小店。她喜欢每周五放学后去那里购物。今天，她发现新包的一个缺点——带子磨破了，她后悔那么短时间之前买了它。她换上短裤，走向海岸。篮球场上，一个球员投进了完美的一球。'你应该加入我们！'他拍着肩膀喊道。莉莉笑了，但太害羞了，不敢回应。她继续沿着路边走，然后拐上一条通向山丘的安静小路。乌云滚滚而来。一阵突如其来的阵雨开始了——她赶紧关好书包。一辆免费穿梭巴士停在附近，她跳了上去。闪电划过时，一名乘客发出一声大喊。颠簸的旅程让她感到有点难受，她想起了家里晕车药。雨停后，她下了车，深吸一口气。这座城市从未如此清新。

---

## Part 177 — 美术馆音乐会

The gallery concert began as **silk**（丝） curtains parted. The pianist gave a deep **sigh**（叹息） before touching the keys. What a **sight**（景观）: art on every wall, music in the air. It was part concert, part **sightseeing**（观光）. The conductor raised his baton as a **signal**（信号）. The orchestra played—the **significance**（重要性） of this moment was unmistakable. Afterward, fans lined up for an autograph—the pianist's **signature**（签字） was highly valued. One whispered **sincerely**（真诚地）: 'Your music changed everything.' A **silver**（银） necklace sparkled around a singer's neck. She began to **sing**（唱） a tune **similar**（相似的） to a folk melody. A **silly**（愚蠢的） young man—looking rather **silly**（愚蠢的）—proposed on the spot. Everyone cheered! He said, '**since**（自） I met you, I found purpose.' The pianist helped **simplify**（单一化） the proposal with soft music. My **single**（单身的） friend glanced **sideways**（向旁边） at the couple and sighed. 'Even a **sir**（先生） like me hopes for a soulmate.' His **sister**（姐妹） patted his arm. As the night ended, hearts seemed to **sink**（藏垢的场所） with the weight of beauty—but the memories would last forever.

美术馆音乐会随着丝绸帷幕的拉开而开始。钢琴家触碰琴键前发出一声深深的叹息。多么美丽的景象：四面墙上挂满画作，音乐在空气中流淌。这既是音乐会，又是观光。指挥家举起指挥棒作为信号。乐团奏响——此刻的重要性不言而喻。演出结束后，粉丝排起长队——钢琴家的签名极为珍贵。有人真诚地低语：'你的音乐改变了一切。'一条银色的项链在歌手的脖子上闪烁。她开始唱一首与民谣相似的曲调。一个看起来傻傻的年轻人当场求婚。所有人都欢呼起来！他说：'自从遇见你，我才找到意义。'钢琴家弹起柔和的曲子，帮他简化了求婚。我的单身朋友侧目看了看那对情侣，叹了口气。'连我这样的先生也希望能有个灵魂伴侣。'他的姐妹拍了拍他的手臂。夜幕降临，人们的心似乎随着美的重量而下沉——但回忆将永存。

---

## Part 178 — 冬日运动日

The **ski**（滑雪橇） resort buzzed with excitement. People of every **size**（大小） came to enjoy the snow. I found a bench to **sit**（坐） on and put on my boots. The **situation**（情形） was ideal: fresh powder under a clear **sky**（天空）. A little boy kept falling while trying to **skate**（溜冰） on the rink, then switched to his **skateboard**（溜冰板）. A **skilful**（灵巧的） instructor taught proper **skill**（技术） to beginners. His jacket had a torn **sleeve**（袖子）, exposing his **skin**（皮肤） to the cold. A woman in a stylish ski **skirt**（裙子） glided down the **slide**（滑）. In the distance, a **skyscraper**（摩天楼） — no, a mountain peak — pierced the clouds. In the lodge, a **sleepy**（困乏的） child napped by the fire. His father cut a thin **slice**（薄的切片） of cake for him. The guide shared a story: 'This mountain was once worked by **slave**（奴隶） labor during the era of **slavery**（奴隶的身分）.' A **slight**（轻蔑） breeze picked up, but it did not spoil the fun. Even the most **slim**（瘦的） beginners were learning fast.

滑雪场充满了兴奋。各种体型的人都来享受雪地。我找了张长椅坐下穿靴子。情况很理想：新鲜的雪粉，晴朗的天空。一个小男孩在冰场上不断摔倒，试着滑冰，然后转而玩起了滑板。一位熟练的教练教初学者正确的技巧。他的外套袖子破了，皮肤暴露在寒风中。一位穿着时尚滑雪裙的女士从滑坡上优雅滑下。远处，一座摩天大楼——不，是山峰——直穿云霄。小屋里，一个困倦的孩子在炉火旁打盹。他父亲为他切了一薄片蛋糕。导游分享了一个故事：'这座山在奴隶制时代曾被奴隶劳工开采过。'一阵轻微的微风袭来，但没有影响乐趣。即便最苗条的初学者也学得很快。

---

## Part 179 — 午后漫步

I took a **slow**（慢的） afternoon stroll through the park. The path was **smooth**（平滑的） and lined with lavender. A **small**（小的） puppy dashed past, nearly causing me to **slip**（滑） on a wet leaf. I noticed a **smart**（聪明的） boy reading beneath an oak tree and could not help but **smile**（微笑）. After the morning rain, the city **smog**（烟雾） had vanished. No factory **smoke**（烟）, no **smoker**（吸烟者） in sight—just crisp, clean air. A gardener used a stick to guide a tiny **snake**（蛇） safely off the trail. A teen in bright **sneaker**（鬼鬼祟祟做事的人） jogged past, full of energy. Someone nearby let out a loud **sneeze**（喷嚏）—spring pollen was at its peak. A friendly dog trotted over to **sniff**（以鼻吸气） my fingers. In my mind, it was a **snowy**（多雪的） winter day, but in reality, it was just a cool autumn afternoon. I stopped at the fountain to wash my hands with lavender **soap**（肥皂）. The day was **so**（如此） peaceful, **so**（如此） perfect.

我在公园里慢慢散步度过午后时光。小路平滑，两旁种满薰衣草。一条小狗冲过去，差点让我在一片湿叶子上滑倒。我看到一个聪明的男孩在橡树下读书，忍不住露出了微笑。早晨的雨后，城市的烟雾已经消散。看不到工厂的烟，也没有吸烟者——只有清爽干净的空气。一位园丁用棍子小心地把一条小蛇移到路边。一个穿着亮色运动鞋的少年跑过，充满活力。旁边有人大声打了个喷嚏——春天的花粉正浓。一只友好的狗跑过来嗅了嗅我的手指。在我的想象中，这是一个下雪的日子，但实际上只是一个凉爽的秋日下午。我在水池边用薰衣草肥皂洗了手。这一天是如此宁静，如此完美。

---

## Part 180 — The Lost Sob

On a quiet afternoon, Lily sat alone on the **sofa**（沙发）, letting out a soft **sob**（啜泣）. She had no interest in **soccer**（英式足球）or any **social**（社会的）activities lately. Her father was a devoted **socialist**（社会主义者）who believed in **socialism**（社会主义）, always helping others. But today, Lily felt **somehow**（不知何故）empty. One **sock**（短袜）lay on the floor near an empty **socket**（插座）. The **soft**（软的）pillow could not comfort her. Outside, **solar**（太阳的）light warmed the **soil**（土地）, but inside she felt cold. A young **soldier**（士兵）walked past her window, looking **solid**（坚硬的）and determined. She remembered her father saying, "**Somebody**（有人）out there needs you. **Someone**（某人）is waiting for **something**（某物）only you can give." **Sometimes**（有时）, he would add, "You will find your place **somewhere**（在某处）. You just need **some**（一些）courage." Lily wiped her tears and stood up. She finally understood.

一个安静的下午，莉莉独自坐在沙发上，轻声啜泣。她最近对足球或任何社交活动都没有兴趣。她的父亲是一位坚信社会主义的社会主义者，总是帮助他人。但今天，莉莉不知何故感到空虚。一只短袜躺在地板上，靠近一个空插座。柔软的枕头无法安慰她。窗外，太阳的光芒温暖着土地，但她内心感到寒冷。一个年轻的士兵走过她的窗户，看起来坚毅而坚定。她想起父亲的话："有人需要你。有人在等待只有你能给予的某样东西。"有时，他还会补充说："你会在某处找到你的位置。你只需要一些勇气。"莉莉擦干眼泪，站了起来。她终于明白了。

---

## Part 181 — The Son and His Song

The old man's **son**（儿子）had a deep **soul**（灵魂）for music. He could turn **sorrow**（悲伤）into a beautiful **song**（歌）that touched every heart. "**Soon**（不久）you will be famous," people told him. But he had a different **sort**（种类）of dream. The **sound**（声音）of his guitar was never **sour**（酸的）or unpleasant. He decided to travel **south**（南方）, through the **southeast**（东南）plains and the **southwest**（西南）hills, all across the **southern**（南方的）lands. At every stop, he bought a small **souvenir**（纪念品）. He gazed at the vast open **space**（空间）under an endless sky. One night, he dreamed of a **spaceship**（宇宙飞船）flying high above. An old man dug with a **spade**（铲子）near a singing **sparrow**（麻雀）. When he returned home, he was invited to **speak**（演说）at the town hall. Standing before the crowd as the main **speaker**（发言人）, he held an ancient **spear**（矛）— a family heirloom — and told his story. "Music," he said, "is the bridge between all hearts."

老人的儿子有着深邃的音乐灵魂。他能将悲伤转化为触动每颗心的美丽歌曲。"不久你就会出名，"人们告诉他。但他有不同种类的梦想。他吉他的声音从不刺耳难听。他决定向南方旅行，穿过东南平原和西南丘陵，走遍所有南方的土地。每到一站，他都买一件小纪念品。他凝视着无尽天空下广阔的空间。一天夜里，他梦见一艘宇宙飞船高高飞翔。一位老人用铲子在唱歌的麻雀旁挖土。回家后，他被邀请在市政厅演说。作为主要发言人站在人群前，他手持祖传的长矛，讲述了他的故事。"音乐，"他说，"是沟通所有心灵的桥梁。"

---

## Part 182 — A Special Sport Day

It was a **special**（特别的）day at school. The **sport**（运动）competition had everyone excited. Tom, a **specialist**（专家）in running, had a **specific**（特殊的）goal: to break the school **speed**（速度）record. At the starting **spot**（地点）, he felt as if a magic **spell**（符咒）had been cast on him. His heart began to **spin**（旋转）with excitement. "Good **spelling**（拼写）won't help you here," joked his friend. But Tom knew how to **spend**（花费）his energy wisely. His **spirit**（精神）was strong, almost **spiritual**（精神上的）in its focus. He would never **spit**（唾吐）on the track like some runners did. The weather was **splendid**（灿烂的）— a perfect day to **split**（劈开）the air like an arrow. His coach had **spoken**（口头讲的）words of encouragement. Their **sponsor**（赞助者）even provided a lucky **spoon**（匙）— just one **spoonful**（一匙）of honey before the race. The whistle blew, and Tom shot forward like a **spear**（矛）through the wind.

这是学校里特别的一天。运动比赛让每个人都兴奋不已。汤姆是跑步方面的专家，有一个特殊的目标：打破学校的速度纪录。在起跑地点，他感觉仿佛有一道符咒降临在他身上。他的心开始因兴奋而旋转。"好的拼写在这里可帮不了你，"他的朋友开玩笑说。但汤姆知道如何明智地花费自己的精力。他的精神很强大，专注得几乎是精神上的。他从不像一些跑步者那样在跑道上唾吐。天气灿烂——这是像箭一样劈开空气的完美一天。他的教练说了口头鼓励的话。他们的赞助者甚至提供了一把幸运的匙——赛前只吃一匙蜂蜜。哨声响起，汤姆像矛一样穿过风向前冲去。

---

## Part 183 — The Spy in the Square

In the town **square**（广场）, spring had arrived. Water **spray**（水花）from the fountain **spread**（传播）a cool mist. A **squirrel**（松鼠）darted across the grass. But **spring**（春天）brought more than flowers — a suspected **spy**（间谍）was hiding in plain sight. Agent Chen stood by the **stadium**（露天大型运动场）, watching the **staff**（全体人员）arrive. The **stable**（马房）nearby was quiet. He noticed a **stain**（污点）on a man's coat — not **stainless**（无瑕疵的）at all. The man climbed the **stair**（楼梯）to the stands, clutching a document with a strange **stamp**（印章）. Chen took his **stand**（站立）behind a pillar. This was **standard**（标准的）procedure. The **star**（明星）athlete was about to arrive. Chen could only **stare**（凝视）and wait. The **start**（开端）of the event was minutes away. Years ago, Chen had witnessed **starvation**（饥饿）in a war zone — he would never let anyone **starve**（挨饿）or suffer again. That was why he served.

在城镇广场，春天已经到来。喷泉的水花传播着凉爽的雾气。一只松鼠跑过草地。但春天带来的不仅仅是花朵——一名疑似间谍正藏在众目睽睽之下。陈特工站在露天大型运动场旁，看着全体人员到达。附近的马房很安静。他注意到一个男人外套上的污点——一点也不无瑕疵。那人爬上楼梯走向看台，紧握着一份盖有奇怪印章的文件。陈在一根柱子后面站好位置。这是标准的程序。那位明星运动员即将到达。陈只能凝视和等待。活动的开端只有几分钟了。多年前，陈在一个战区目睹过饥饿——他绝不会让任何人再挨饿或受苦。这就是他服役的原因。

---

## Part 184 — Journey to the Northern State

At the train **station**（车站）, Maria read the official **statement**（声明）posted on the wall. The **statistics**（统计资料）showed rising temperatures across every **state**（州）. A marble **statue**（雕像）of a hero stood near the platform, its **status**（地位）unchanged for a century. Maria decided to **stay**（停留）calm and **steady**（稳定的）. She went to a restaurant and ordered **steak**（牛排）. A man tried to **steal**（偷窃）her bag, but she caught him. The kitchen's **steam**（蒸汽）rose as the cook wielded **steel**（钢）knives. Behind the restaurant rose a **steep**（陡峭的）hill. A kind **steward**（乘务员）helped with her luggage, while a **stewardess**（空中小姐）smiled warmly. Maria used her walking **stick**（拐杖）as she boarded. She sat **still**（静止地）, adjusting her **stocking**（长袜）. Her **stomach**（胃）felt tight with nerves. Outside the window, ancient **stone**（石头）walls passed by — reminders of history's weight.

在火车站，玛丽亚读着墙上张贴的官方声明。统计资料显示每个州的气温都在上升。一座英雄的大理石雕像矗立在站台旁，其地位一个世纪以来未曾改变。玛丽亚决定保持冷静和稳定。她去餐厅点了牛排。一个男人试图偷窃她的包，但她抓住了他。厨房的蒸汽升腾，厨师挥舞着钢刀。餐厅后面耸立着一座陡峭的山。一位好心的乘务员帮她搬行李，一位空中小姐温暖地微笑着。玛丽亚用拐杖上了车。她静静地坐着，整理了一下长袜。她的胃因为紧张而收紧。窗外，古老的石头墙壁掠过——是历史重量的见证。

---

## Part 185 — The Stubborn Student

The bus came to a **stop**（停止）at a small town. In the **storage**（仓库）room of an old inn, a boy was reading a **story**（故事）to his **stout**（结实的）grandfather. The **stove**（火炉）burned warmly as the old man spoke of a **straight**（直的）and **straightforward**（率直的）life. "Never cross the **strait**（海峡）of dishonesty," he said. The boy found this **strange**（奇怪的）— his grandfather was once a **stranger**（陌生人）here too. He sipped water through a **straw**（吸管）and ate a **strawberry**（草莓）. A **stream**（小河）flowed past the window onto the **street**（街道）. The boy knew he must find his **strength**（力量）and **strengthen**（加强）his resolve. He tied a **string**（细绳）around his wrist as a reminder to be **strong**（坚强的）. Though **stubborn**（顽固的）, he was a dedicated **student**（学生）who never gave up on his dreams.

公共汽车在一个小镇停了下来。在一家老客栈的仓库里，一个男孩正在给他的结实的祖父读故事。火炉温暖地燃烧着，老人讲述着笔直而率直的人生。"永远不要跨越不诚实的海峡，"他说。男孩觉得这很奇怪——他的祖父曾经也是这里的陌生人。他用吸管喝着水，吃着一颗草莓。一条小溪流过窗外，汇入街道。男孩知道他必须找到自己的力量并加强决心。他在手腕上系了一根细绳，提醒自己要保持坚强。虽然顽固，但他是一个从不放弃梦想的专注学生。

---

## Part 186 — Inside the Studio

Ming worked late at his art **studio**（工作室）. His latest **study**（研究）was about blending Eastern and Western **style**（风格）. But art was **subjective**（主观的）— not everyone would agree. He had to **submit**（提交）his portfolio to the gallery. His friend urged him to **subscribe**（订阅）to an art magazine. "There is no **substitute**（代替物）for real practice," Ming replied. **Success**（成功）required **such**（如此的）dedication. He would **sow**（播种）the seeds of creativity daily, never letting the work **suck**（吸）all his energy. All of a **sudden**（突然）, inspiration struck — he would create a series about those who **suffer**（遭受）and their **suffering**（苦难）. He added a touch of **sugar**（糖）to his tea and smiled. His mentor had given him a key **suggestion**（建议）: always wear a proper **suit**（套装）to meetings. It was **suitable**（适当的）advice for any professional. He packed his **suitcase**（手提箱）and headed out. His **suite**（套房）at the hotel would be his base for the exhibition.

明在他的艺术工作室工作到很晚。他最近的研究是将东西方风格融合。但艺术是主观的——不是每个人都会同意。他必须向画廊提交他的作品集。他的朋友劝他订阅一本艺术杂志。"没有什么能代替真正的实践，"明回答。成功需要如此的投入。有时工作会吸走他所有的精力。突然，灵感来了——他要创作一个关于遭受苦难的人的系列。他往茶里加了一点糖，微笑着。他的导师给了他一个关键的建议：开会时总要穿合适的套装。这对任何专业人士来说都是适当的忠告。他打包好手提箱出发了。酒店的套房将成为他展览期间的大本营。

---

## Part 187 — A Summer Full of Surprise

The **summary**（摘要）of last year's report still haunted him. But this **summer**（夏季）would be different. The **sun**（太阳）rose brilliantly, though his skin got **sunburnt**（晒黑的）quickly. Soft **sunlight**（日光）filtered through the trees. Every day was **sunny**（阳光充足的）, full of warm **sunshine**（阳光）. He felt **super**（特级的）energized and in a **superb**（极好的）mood. His **superior**（上级）had given him time off. At the **supermarket**（超级市场）, he bought ingredients for **supper**（晚餐）. The **supply**（供给）of fresh vegetables was impressive. "I **suppose**（猜想）you are cooking tonight," his wife laughed. The meal was of **supreme**（至高的）quality — he was **sure**（确信）of it. On the **surface**（表面）, everything seemed perfect. But the real **surprise**（惊喜）came when his brother, a **surgeon**（外科医生）, appeared at the door — using his **surplus**（盈余）vacation days to visit.

去年报告的摘要仍然萦绕在他心头。但这个夏季会有所不同。太阳灿烂地升起，尽管他的皮肤很快被晒黑了。柔和的日光源源透过树木。每一天都是阳光充足的，充满了温暖的阳光。他感到精力极其充沛，心情极好。他的上级给了他假期。在超级市场，他买了做晚餐的食材。新鲜蔬菜的供给令人印象深刻。"我猜想你今晚要做饭，"妻子笑着说。这顿饭品质至高——他确信这一点。在表面上看，一切都很完美。但真正的惊喜是，他的哥哥——一名外科医生——用盈余的假期出现在门口。

---

## Part 188 — The Surround of Silence

Dark forests **surround**（包围）the old hospital. The **surrounding**（周围的）silence was broken only by wind. Dr. Lin specialized in **survival**（生存）medicine. Today she had a **suspect**（嫌疑犯）patient with unusual **symptom**（症状）— perhaps a nerve **suspension**（暂停）issue. She needed to **swap**（交换）shifts with a colleague. "I **swear**（发誓）I will find the cause," she said. Her team began to **sweep**（打扫）through the data. A nurse brought **sweet**（糖果）tea as the patient's wrist began to **swell**（肿胀）. Dr. Lin made a **swift**（迅速的）decision and would **suggest**（建议）a new treatment plan. The patient once loved to **swim**（游泳）and **swing**（摇摆）dance. Now she could barely **switch**（开关）on a light. The **sword**（剑）of illness was cruel. But the patient's **sympathy**（同情）for others never faded — she even hummed a **symphony**（交响乐）from memory. The hospital **system**（系统）kicked into action, and hope returned.

黑暗的森林包围着那座老医院。周围的寂静只有风声打破。林医生专攻生存医学。今天她有一个疑似病例，带着不寻常的症状——也许是神经暂停问题。她需要与同事交换班次。"我发誓我会找到原因，"她说。她的团队开始梳理数据。护士端来甜茶，病人的手腕开始肿胀。林医生做出了迅速的决定。这位病人曾热爱游泳和摇摆舞。现在她几乎连灯的开关都打不开。疾病的剑是残酷的。但病人对他人的同情从未消退——她甚至凭记忆哼唱了一首交响乐。医院的系统启动运转，希望重新降临。

---

## Part 189 — The Tailor and His Tale

Old Mr. Zhang ran a traditional **tailor**（裁缝）shop. He approached every task in a **systematic**（有系统的）way. His work **table**（桌子）was covered with fabric, and a **tablet**（平板）showed new designs. A cat with a fluffy **tail**（尾巴）sat nearby. He would **take**（拿）every measurement with care. His favorite **tale**（故事）was about a **tall**（高的）customer who needed a **tank**（坦克）-sized suit. One day, a young man came in. "I need to **tap**（轻敲）into your wisdom," he said. Zhang handed him a measuring **tape**（卷尺）. "This is your first **task**（任务）." They would **talk**（谈话）for hours about the craft. The young man learned to **taste**（品味）the art of tailoring — never producing **tasteless**（没味道的）work, always making **tasty**（可口的）designs. "Even the **tax**（税）collector wears my suits," Zhang laughed. A **taxi**（出租车）honked outside as another **taxpayer**（纳税人）arrived for a fitting.

老张先生经营着一家传统裁缝店。他以有系统的方式处理每项任务。他的工作桌上铺满布料，一台平板显示着新设计。一只毛茸茸尾巴的猫坐在旁边。他会仔细地量取每一个尺寸。他最喜欢的故事是关于一个需要坦克般尺寸西装的高个子顾客。一天，一个年轻人走了进来。"我需要汲取您的智慧，"他说。张递给他一把卷尺。"这是你的第一个任务。"年轻人学会了品味裁剪的艺术——从不制作没味道的作品，总是做出可口的设计。"连税务员都穿我的西装，"张笑道。一辆出租车在外面鸣笛，另一位纳税人前来试衣。

---

## Part 190 — The Teenager and His Teapot

After **tea**（茶）time, a curious **teenager**（少年）named Wei began his science project. His **teacher**（老师）had asked the **team**（团队）to **teach**（教导）younger students about **technology**（技术）. **Teamwork**（协同作业）was essential, but Wei preferred solo experiments. He used a **teapot**（茶壶）to demonstrate steam power. A **tear**（眼泪）of joy rolled down his cheek when it worked. His friends would **tease**（戏弄）him, but he did not mind. The **technical**（技术上的）aspect was challenging, but his **technique**（技巧）improved daily. He picked up the **telephone**（电话）to call his mentor. Through a **telescope**（望远镜）, he observed stars, then watched a documentary on **television**（电视）. He would **tell**（告诉）everyone about his results. The **temperature**（温度）in the lab rose. He visited the **temple**（寺庙）to calm his mind. This was only a **temporary**（暂时的）pause — his dreams were permanent.

茶点时间过后，一个好奇的少年伟开始了他的科学项目。他的老师要求团队教导更小的学生学习技术。协同作业很重要，但伟更喜欢独自实验。他用一把茶壶来演示蒸汽动力。当实验成功时，一滴喜悦的眼泪滚下他的脸颊。朋友们会戏弄他，但他不在意。技术上的方面很有挑战性，但他的技巧每天都在提高。他拿起电话打给他的导师。通过望远镜，他观察星星，然后在电视上看了一部纪录片。他会把结果告诉每个人。实验室的温度升高了。他去了寺庙平静心绪。这只是暂时的停顿——他的梦想是永恒的。

---

## Part 191 — The Tent by the Terminal

They **tend**（倾向于）to camp near the old bus **terminal**（终点站）. This **tendency**（倾向）had become a tradition. After playing **tennis**（网球）, the group felt the **tension**（紧张）melt away. They set up a **tent**（帐篷）under the stars. It was a **tentative**（试验性的）plan — they were not sure about the weather. The **term**（术语）"adventure" took on real meaning. But something at the **terminal**（终点站）began to **terrify**（使恐惧）them at night. Strange noises! They decided to **test**（测试）their courage. Armed with a **text**（课文）on local legends and a **textbook**（教科书）on wildlife, they investigated. Nothing could be worse **than**（比）giving in to fear. "**Thank**（感谢）goodness we came together," whispered one. "I am **thankful**（感谢的）for **that**（那）." **The**（这）sounds turned out to be a stray dog. A simple **theft**（盗窃）of their snacks by a raccoon had caused all the drama. The night at the **theatre**（戏院）of nature was unforgettable.

他们倾向于在旧巴士终点站附近露营。这种倾向已成为一项传统。打完网球后，这群人感到紧张情绪消散了。他们在星空下搭起了帐篷。这是一项试验性的计划——他们不确定天气状况。"冒险"这个术语有了真正的意义。但终点站的某些东西在夜里开始使他们恐惧。奇怪的声音！他们决定测试自己的勇气。带着一本关于当地传说的课文和一本关于野生动物的教科书，他们展开了调查。没有什么比屈服于恐惧更糟糕。"感谢老天我们一起来了，"一个人轻声说。"我为那件事感到感谢。"这声音原来是一只流浪狗。一只浣熊对他们零食的简单盗窃引发了这场风波。在自然戏院里的这一夜令人难忘。

---

## Part 192 — The Thief in Their Midst

**Their**（他们的）library was the heart of **theirs**（他们的）community. Everyone loved **them**（他们）— the librarians. The annual **theme**（主题）was "Mystery and Theory." The librarians dedicated **themselves**（他们自己）to the event. **Then**（然后）, disaster struck. A **theoretical**（理论上的）physics book vanished. The **theory**（理论）section was in chaos. "**There**（在那里）must be an explanation," said the head librarian. "**Therefore**（因此）, we will investigate." She poured tea from her **thermos**（热水瓶）and studied **these**（这些）clues. **They**（他们）found a **thick**（厚的）envelope left behind. A **thin**（薄的）person could have slipped through the window. But the most important **thing**（事情）was to **think**（思考）logically. Deep **thinking**（思考）revealed the truth: the book had simply been misplaced. There was no **thief**（小偷）at all — just careless shelving.

他们的图书馆是他们社区的核心。每个人都喜欢他们——那些图书管理员。年度主题是"神秘与理论"。图书管理员们将自己完全投入到活动中。然后，灾难降临了。一本理论上的物理书消失了。理论区域一片混乱。"在那里一定有个解释，"馆长说。"因此，我们要进行调查。"她从热水瓶里倒了杯茶，研究起这些线索。他们发现了一个被留下的厚信封。一个瘦的人可能从窗户溜了进来。但最重要的事情是进行逻辑思考。深入思考揭示了真相：那本书只是被放错了地方。那个"小偷"不过是粗心的上架而已。

---

## Part 193 — The Thriller at Dawn

**Thirst**（口渴）woke him at dawn. **This**（这）was the day. He had been **thorough**（彻底的）in his preparation. **Those**（那些）warnings from the guide echoed in his mind. **Though**（虽然）fear crept in, a single **thought**（想法）kept him going: find the treasure. He followed a **thread**（线）of clues that read like a **thriller**（惊悚小说）. His **throat**（咽喉）was dry as he crawled **through**（穿越）the narrow cave **throughout**（自始至终）the morning. He had to **throw**（投）a rope to cross a gap. **Thus**（如此）, he reached the hidden chamber. The **tick**（滴答声）of his watch was the only sound. He found an old **ticket**（票）and a **tidy**（整齐的）bundle of maps. Using a **tie**（领带）as a bandage, he pressed on. A carved **tiger**（老虎）guarded the final door. The passage was **tight**（紧的）, but he squeezed through to discover a breathtaking underground lake.

口渴在黎明时唤醒了他。这就是那一天。他做了彻底的准备。那些向导的警告在他脑海中回荡。虽然恐惧悄然袭来，但一个想法支撑着他：找到宝藏。他沿着一条线索前进，读起来就像一部惊悚小说。他的喉咙发干，整个上午都在穿越狭窄的洞穴。他不得不投掷一根绳子来越过一道沟壑。如此，他到达了隐藏的密室。手表的滴答声是唯一的声音。他找到了一张旧票和一捆整齐的地图。用一条领带当绷带，他继续前进。一只雕刻的老虎守护着最后一扇门。通道是紧的，但他挤了过去，发现了一个令人叹为观止的地下湖。

---

## Part 194 — The Tomb of Time

From dawn **till**（直到）dusk, the archaeologists worked. **Time**（时间）was running out. The **timetable**（时间表）was strict. They found a **tin**（锡）box at the **tip**（尖端）of a buried pillar. One researcher, her **tire**（精力）of energy running low, felt deeply **tired**（疲累的）. It had been a **tiresome**（累人的）dig. But then — a fragile **tissue**（薄织物）wrapped object appeared, bearing a royal **title**（头衔）. They raised a **toast**（干杯）**to**（向）their discovery. The faint scent of ancient **tobacco**（烟草）lingered. "**Today**（今天）we made history!" they cheered **together**（一起）. After a quick **toilet**（厕所）break, they continued. One must **tolerate**（容忍）hardship in archaeology. A **tomato**（番茄）sandwich was a feast. Finally, they entered the inner **tomb**（坟墓）— a silent chamber that had waited three thousand years for this moment.

从黎明直到黄昏，考古学家们一直在工作。时间快不够了。时间表很严格。他们在一根埋藏柱子尖端发现了一个锡盒。一位研究员精力耗尽，感到极度疲累。这次挖掘真是累人的。但接着——一件包裹在脆弱薄织物中的物品出现了，上面刻有皇家头衔。他们为发现干杯。古老烟草的淡淡气味萦绕其中。"今天我们创造了历史！"他们一起欢呼。快速上完厕所后，他们继续工作。在考古学中必须容忍艰难。一个番茄三明治就是盛宴。最终，他们进入了内部的坟墓——一个等待了三千年这一时刻的寂静墓室。

---

## Part 195 — Morning Rush

**Tomorrow**（明天） is the big day — the English speech contest! **Tonight**（今晚）, however, I stayed up **too**（太） late practicing. My **tongue**（舌头） feels tangled from all the pronunciation drills. When I **wake**（醒来） up, my bag weighs a **ton**（吨） with books. I grab my **toothbrush**（牙刷） and squeeze out **toothpaste**（牙膏）. My **tooth**（牙齿） hurts — a sudden **toothache**（牙痛）! I use a translation app as a **tool**（工具） to review key phrases. At the **top**（顶部） of my notes, I wrote the **topic**（话题）: "The Future of **Tourism**（旅游业）." A ceramic **tortoise**（乌龟） sits on my desk — a gift from my grandfather, reminding me to be steady. The **total**（总的） preparation time was 30 hours. I **touch**（触摸） the little tortoise for luck. The competition will be **tough**（艰苦的）, but I am ready. A fellow **tourist**（游客） I met last summer sent me a good-luck message. This **tournament**（比赛） is my chance to shine. I walk **toward**（朝着） the door with confidence.

明天就是大日子——英语演讲比赛！然而今晚我练习到太晚。由于所有那些发音训练，我的舌头感觉打结了。当我醒来时，我的包重得像一吨，装满了书。我抓起牙刷，挤了些牙膏。我的牙疼——突然牙痛了！我用一个翻译应用作为工具复习关键短语。在我笔记的顶部，我写下了话题：「旅游业的未来」。一只陶瓷乌龟坐在我的书桌上——爷爷送的礼物，提醒我要沉稳。总的准备时间是三十个小时。我摸了摸那只小乌龟祈求好运。比赛将是艰苦的，但我准备好了。去年夏天遇到的一位游客给我发了祝福信息。这次比赛是我发光的机会。我自信地朝着门口走去。

---

## Part 196 — A Trip to Town

I dry my face with a **towel**（毛巾） and head out. The bus takes me past an ancient **tower**（塔） into **town**（城镇）. A child on the bus plays with a stuffed **toy**（玩具）. Outside, a **tractor**（拖拉机） rumbles by. My father works in the **trade**（贸易） business — today I am helping at his booth. The market is a celebration of **tradition**（传统）. But the **traffic**（交通） is terrible! I switch from the **train**（火车） to a **tram**（有轨电车）. Technology can **transform**（改变） a city, but some chaos remains. I help a foreigner **translate**（翻译） a menu. The **translation**（翻译） is tricky — food names do not always have direct equivalents. A professional **translator**（翻译者） nearby offers help. Public **transport**（运输） here is always crowded. I watch out for a **trap**（陷阱） — a broken step on the platform. I love to **travel**（旅行）, and every journey teaches me something. A fellow **traveller**（旅行者） shares directions. Finally I arrive — the market is a **treasure**（宝藏） of crafts and colors. My father smiles and offers to **treat**（款待） me to lunch later.

我用毛巾擦干脸，然后出门。公交车带我经过一座古塔进入城镇。车上的一个孩子在玩一只毛绒玩具。外面，一辆拖拉机轰隆隆开过。我父亲做贸易生意——今天我在他的摊位帮忙。市场是传统的庆典。但交通太糟糕了！我从火车换乘了有轨电车。科技能改变一座城市，但一些混乱依然存在。我帮一个外国人翻译了一份菜单。翻译有点棘手——菜名不是总有直接对应的说法。附近一位专业翻译者提供了帮助。这里的公共运输总是很拥挤。我小心提防着陷阱——站台上的一级破损台阶。我热爱旅行，每一次旅程都教会我一些东西。一位同行的旅行者给我指了路。终于到了——这个市场是手工艺品和色彩的宝藏。爸爸笑了，说待会儿请我吃饭。

---

## Part 197 — The Forest Trial

After lunch, Dad tells me a story from his youth. He once got lost in a forest and needed medical **treatment**（治疗） for a sprained ankle. He leaned against a **tree**（树）, feeling his body **tremble**（颤抖） from cold and fear. There was a new **trend**（趋势） at the time — survival camping. But this was no game. It was a real **trial**（考验）. He marked trees by carving a **triangle**（三角形） to avoid walking in circles. A stranger tried to play a **trick**（诡计） on him by giving wrong directions. But Dad stayed calm. His scouting **trip**（旅行） turned into a lesson. A passing **trolleybus**（无轨电车） — of all things in a forest! — had broken down. A **troop**（一群） of soldiers on an exercise found him. The real **trouble**（麻烦） began when a **troublesome**（讨厌的） officer questioned his story. Dad's **trousers**（裤子） were torn and dirty. A **truck**（卡车） finally brought him home. The story is **true**（真实的） — I have seen the scar. He was **truly**（真正地） lucky. He carved his name on a **trunk**（树干） as a marker. I **trust**（信任） my father completely. His story taught me that the **truth**（真相） always comes out.

午饭后，爸爸给我讲了他年轻时的一个故事。他曾在森林里迷路，因为扭伤脚踝需要治疗。他靠在一棵树上，感觉身体因寒冷和恐惧而颤抖。当时有一个新的趋势——生存露营。但这可不是游戏。那是一次真正的考验。他在树上刻下三角形标记以免绕圈。一个陌生人试图对他玩诡计，故意指错方向。但爸爸保持了冷静。他的侦察旅行变成了一堂课。一辆路过的无轨电车——在森林里见到这个可真稀奇！——抛锚了。一群正在演习的士兵发现了他。真正的麻烦始于一个讨厌的军官质疑他的说法。爸爸的裤子又破又脏。一辆卡车最终把他带回了家。这个故事是真实的——我见过那道伤疤。他真正地很幸运。他在一棵树干上刻下自己的名字作为标记。我完全信任我的父亲。他的故事教会我，真相总会水落石出。

---

## Part 198 — Musical Twins

My neighbors are **twin**（双胞胎） sisters — Luna and Lena. They both **try**（尝试） to learn music, but in very different ways. Luna plays the **tube**（管乐器） in the school band. Lena prefers to hum a sweet **tune**（旋律） while cooking **turkey**（火鸡） for family dinners. They take **turn**（轮流） practicing — one in the morning, one at night. At the **turning**（转弯处） of the hallway, they hung music notes on the wall. Their styles have a creative **twist**（扭转） — Luna likes classical, Lena loves jazz. They are the same **type**（类型） of passionate, yet so different. Their grandmother used an old **typewriter**（打字机） to write song lyrics. Her style was **typical**（典型的） of 1960s folk music — simple and heartfelt. She worked as a **typist**（打字员） at a music publisher. The twins fixed her **tyre**（轮胎） so she could drive to concerts. Their small apartment is not **ugly**（丑陋的） — every corner is filled with instruments. On rainy days they share an **umbrella**（雨伞） and hum together. I am **unable**（不能的） to tell their voices apart! The pressure of competitions is sometimes **unbearable**（无法忍受的）, but they support each other. Their harmony is **unbelievable**（难以置信的）. Their future in music remains **uncertain**（不确定的）, but their passion never fades. Their **uncle**（叔叔） promised to fund their first album.

我的邻居是一对双胞胎姐妹——露娜和莉娜。她们都尝试学习音乐，但方式大不相同。露娜在学校乐队演奏管乐器。莉娜更喜欢在做家庭晚餐的火鸡时哼唱甜美的旋律。她们轮流练习——一个早上，一个晚上。在走廊的转弯处，她们在墙上贴了音符。她们的音乐风格有一种创造性的扭转——露娜喜欢古典，莉娜热爱爵士。她们是同一类型的热情，却又如此不同。她们的祖母曾用一台老式打字机写歌词。她的风格是典型的六十年代民谣——简单而真挚。她曾在一家音乐出版社做打字员。双胞胎修好了她的轮胎，这样她就能开车去听音乐会了。她们的小公寓并不丑陋——每个角落都摆满了乐器。下雨天她们共撑一把雨伞，一起哼唱。我无法分辨她们的声音！比赛的压力有时无法忍受，但她们互相支持。她们的和谐令人难以置信。她们在音乐上的未来还不确定，但热情永不消退。她们的叔叔承诺资助她们的第一张专辑。

---

## Part 199 — The Union Underground

Beneath the city, I discover an **underground**（地下的） world. It is cold here, so I wear thick **underwear**（内衣）. A faded poster hangs on the wall — someone tried to **underline**（在下面划线） the key demands. It tells the story of the **unemployment**（失业） crisis of the 1990s. Workers faced **unfair**（不公平的） layoffs. Many felt **unfit**（不胜任的） for new jobs. It was an **unfortunate**（不幸的） chapter. But the workers formed a **union**（工会） — a **unique**（独特的） organization that became a powerful **unit**（单位） of resistance. Their goal was to **unite**（团结） the people. The government refused to negotiate **unless**（除非） the protests stopped. But the workers stood firm, **unlike**（不像） previous generations who stayed silent. The **unrest**（动荡） lasted for months. They refused to give up **until**（直到） their demands were met. Even those **unwilling**（不愿意的） to strike at first eventually joined. Their spirit was **unconditional**（无条件的） — no fear, no retreat. Walking through these tunnels, I feel almost **unconscious**（无意识的） connection to their struggle. I run my fingers **under**（在...下面） the old brick arches. History breathes here.

在城市之下，我发现了一个地下的世界。这里很冷，所以我穿了厚内衣。一张褪色的海报挂在墙上——有人试图在下面划线标出关键要求。它讲述了九十年代失业危机的故事。工人们面临不公平的裁员。许多人觉得自己不胜任新的工作。那是不幸的一章。但工人们组建了一个工会——一个独特的组织，成为了一个强大的抵抗单位。他们的目标是团结人民。政府拒绝谈判，除非抗议停止。但工人们坚定地站着，不像之前那些保持沉默的世代。动荡持续了数月。他们拒绝放弃，直到要求得到满足。即使是起初不愿意罢工的人也最终加入了。他们的精神是无条件的——没有恐惧，没有撤退。穿过这些隧道，我感到与他们的斗争之间一种几乎无意识的连接。我用手指抚过古老的砖拱门下面。历史在这里呼吸。

---

## Part 200 — Urban Rising

My cousin lives in an **urban**（城市的） apartment on the **upper**（上面的） floor of a skyscraper. She works late — sometimes I **urge**（催促） her to rest. But she says every project is **urgent**（紧急的） in this city. She promised to send me an **update**（更新） about her new job. I look **upward**（向上地） at the towering building and feel dizzy. She works as a **user**（用户） interface designer. Her old laptop is **used**（用过的） and slow, but she refuses to replace it — she says nothing is **useless**（无用的） if you are creative. She once felt like a **vacant**（空虚的） soul, lost in the crowd. But design gave her purpose. Her **usual**（平常的） workday lasts twelve hours. She stays **up**（醒着） late sketching ideas. I often **wonder**（想知道） how she manages it all. Last month she finally felt **uncomfortable**（不舒服的） enough to ask for help, and **us**（我们） friends gladly supported her. Now she makes good **use**（使用） of every resource. But she has grown. I was **upset**（心烦的） when she missed family gatherings, but now I understand. She rushes **upstairs**（向楼上） to her office each morning. **Upon**（一...就...） arriving, she starts designing. Her life is not easy, but she is building something meaningful — one pixel at a time.

我表姐住在一栋摩天大楼上层的一套城市公寓里。她工作到很晚——有时我催促她休息。但她说在这个城市每个项目都很紧急。她答应给我发关于她新工作的更新。我向上望着那座高耸的建筑，感到头晕。她是一名用户界面设计师。她的旧笔记本电脑是用过的，很慢，但她拒绝更换——她说只要你有创造力，没有什么是无用的。她曾感觉自己像一个空虚的灵魂，迷失在人群中。但设计给了她目标。她平常的工作日长达十二个小时。她熬夜到很晚勾勒创意。直到上个月她还不愿意寻求帮助。但她成长了。当她错过家庭聚会时我曾心烦，但现在我理解了。每天早上她冲上楼去办公室。一到达就开始设计。她的生活并不容易，但她正在一像素一像素地构建有意义的东西。

---

## Part 201 — The Valley of Truth

We travel to a remote **valley**（山谷） to investigate an old case. The claim sounds **vague**（模糊的） — a hidden artifact of great **value**（价值）. Some say the search is **vain**（徒劳的）. But we have a **valid**（有效的） permit. The landscape is **vast**（广阔的） — mountains stretch to the horizon. We discover a **variety**（各种各样） of ancient tools. **Various**（各种的） experts join the excavation. A **vase**（花瓶） is unearthed — stunning and intact. Nearby, workers grow **vegetable**（蔬菜） crops. An old scholar carries a rare **version**（版本） of a historical text. The cliff face is nearly **vertical**（垂直的） — climbing it is **very**（非常） dangerous. I wear a protective **vest**（背心） and helmet. We communicate **via**（通过） radio. This is no place for **vice**（恶习） or carelessness. The first **victim**（遇难者） of this mountain was a treasure hunter who ignored warnings. We record everything on **video**（录像）. At last, we find it: a stone tablet proving the legend was true. Its discovery is **valuable**（有价值的） to history beyond measure.

我们来到一个偏远的山谷调查一桩旧案。那个说法听起来很模糊——一件有巨大价值的隐藏文物。有人说这次搜寻是徒劳的。但我们有一份有效的许可。这片土地广阔无垠——山脉延伸至天际。我们发现了各种各样的古代工具。各种专家加入了挖掘。一个花瓶被挖了出来——精美而完好。附近，农民们在种蔬菜。一位老学者带着一份稀有版本的历史文献。崖壁几乎是垂直的——爬上去非常危险。我穿着防护背心和头盔。我们通过无线电沟通。这里容不得半点恶习或疏忽。这座山的第一个遇难者就是一个无视警告的寻宝者。我们用录像记录一切。终于，我们找到了：一块石碑，证明传说是真实的。它对历史的价值是不可估量的。

---

## Part 202 — The Virus Outbreak

A dangerous **virus**（病毒） spreads through the region. The situation is **vital**（至关重要的） — every second counts. The hospital opens a new **ward**（病房）. I use a **videophone**（可视电话） to check on my grandparents in their **village**（村庄）. A **villager**（村民） says they are running low on supplies. A kind neighbor shares homemade **vinegar**（醋） for disinfecting. No one should **violate**（违反） quarantine rules. The **violence**（猛烈） of this outbreak is shocking. But hope remains. A **violinist**（小提琴家） plays the **violin**（小提琴） from her balcony every evening — the whole street listens. Her **virtue**（美德） inspires us all. I apply for a **visa**（签证） to join an international relief team. A **visit**（访问） from a **visitor**（访客） is not allowed now, but love knows no barriers. A **visual**（视觉的） artist paints murals on hospital walls to lift spirits. Scientists say the nearby **volcano**（火山） is unrelated to the crisis. Kids play **volleyball**（排球） in their yards, safely distanced. A **voluntary**（志愿的） group organizes food delivery. People **vote**（投票） on community safety measures. This is our **voyage**（航行） through darkness — together we sail through.

一种危险的病毒在该地区蔓延。局势至关重要——每一秒都很关键。医院开设了新病房。我用可视电话查看在村庄里的祖父母。一位村民说他们的物资快用完了。一位好心的邻居分享了自制的醋用来消毒。任何人都不应违反隔离规定。这次疫情的猛烈令人震惊。但希望依然存在。一位小提琴家每晚在阳台上演奏小提琴——整条街都在倾听。她的美德激励了我们所有人。我申请了签证去加入国际救援队。现在访客的访问是不允许的，但爱没有障碍。一位视觉艺术家在医院墙上画壁画来鼓舞士气。科学家说附近的火山与危机无关。孩子们在院子里打排球，保持安全距离。一个志愿团体组织食品配送。人们对社区安全措施进行投票。这是我们穿越黑暗的航行——我们一起驶过。

---

## Part 203 — The Restaurant

My aunt runs a small restaurant. Her dog will **wag**（摇摆） its tail to greet every customer. She pays fair **wage**（工资） to her staff. A young cook ties an apron around his **waist**（腰部） and gets to work. Customers **wait**（等待） patiently — the food is worth it. A **waiter**（服务员） balances trays skillfully. A **waitress**（女服务员） smiles and takes orders. I **weigh**（称重） the ingredients carefully before cooking. I **walk**（步行） to the market to buy fresh **walnut**（核桃） and herbs. Sometimes I **wander**（漫步） through the old town looking for inspiration. I **want**（想要） to learn every recipe. My aunt lived through **war**（战争） as a child — she gives every customer a warm **welcome**（欢迎） at the door. The restaurant has a clean **washroom**（洗手间） for customers. A small **warehouse**（仓库） next door holds extra supplies. The kitchen is always **warm**（温暖的）, filled with the **warmth**（温暖） of cooking and laughter. Auntie does not **warn**（警告） — she teaches with patience. After closing, we **wash**（洗） every dish by hand. Nothing goes to **waste**（浪费） here.

我姑姑经营着一家小餐馆。她的狗摇摆着尾巴迎接每位顾客。她给员工发公平的工资。一位年轻的厨师在腰间系上围裙开始工作。顾客耐心等待——美食值得等待。一位服务员熟练地托着托盘。一位女服务员微笑着记录点单。我周末早早醒来去帮忙。我步行去市场买新鲜的核桃和香草。有时我在老城区漫步寻找灵感。我想要学会每一道菜谱。姑姑小时候经历过战争——她知道每一粒粮食的份量。餐厅有一个舒适的后间用于存放食材。隔壁的一个小仓库存放额外的物资。厨房总是温暖的，充满了烹饪和笑声的温暖。姑姑从不警告——她用耐心来教导。打烊后，我们手洗每一个碗盘。这里没有任何东西被浪费。

---

## Part 204 — The Watcher by the Water

Every evening, an old man sits by the lake. He wears an old **watch**（手表） — a gift from his late wife. The **water**（湖水） reflects the sunset like a mirror. He slices a **watermelon**（西瓜） and shares it with children. A tiny **wave**（波浪） laps at the shore. Candles made of **wax**（蜡） float on the surface during festivals. He knows every **way**（路） around this lake. He often says: **we**（我们） are all connected — like a **web**（网） of life. His legs are **weak**（虚弱的） now, but his spirit is strong. He never sees age as a **weakness**（弱点）. He shares the **wealth**（财富） of his wisdom freely. Though not **wealthy**（富有的） in money, he is rich in experience. He chooses to **wear**（穿着） simple clothes. Whatever the **weather**（天气）, he comes — rain or shine. His own **wedding**（婚礼） happened by this very lake, fifty years ago. He still pulls a **weed**（杂草） from the path now and then. Every **week**（周） he writes a letter to his daughter abroad. On a **weekday**（工作日） the lake is quiet; on a **weekend**（周末） families fill the park. He reads the **weekly**（每周的） newspaper on a bench. Sometimes he will **weep**（哭泣） quietly — but they are tears of gratitude.

每天傍晚，一位老人坐在湖边。他戴着一块旧手表——已故妻子送的礼物。湖水像镜子一样映照着日落。他切了一个西瓜和孩子们分享。细小的波浪轻拍着湖岸。节日时蜡烛做的蜡花漂浮在水面上。他知道湖边每一条路。他常说：我们都是一张生命之网，彼此相连。他的腿现在虚弱了，但精神依然坚强。他从不把年龄看作弱点。他慷慨地分享智慧的财富。虽然不富有金钱，他却富有经验。他选择穿着简单的衣服。无论天气如何他都来——风雨无阻。他自己的婚礼就在这个湖边举行，五十年前。他偶尔还会从小路上拔掉一棵杂草。每周他都给海外的女儿写一封信。工作日时湖面安静，周末时公园里充满了家庭。他在长椅上看每周的报纸。有时他静静地哭泣——但那是感恩的泪水。

---

## Part 205 — The Western Express

I board a train heading **west**（向西）. My destination is a **western**（西部的） province known for its canyons. The train rolls **westward**（向西） through changing landscapes. Rain makes the tracks **wet**（湿的） and glistening. Through the window I imagine a **whale**（鲸鱼） swimming through clouds — the sky is that vast. I do not know **what**（什么） awaits me, but I am ready for **whatever**（无论什么） comes. Fields of **wheat**（小麦） stretch to the horizon. I grip the steering **wheel**（方向盘） of a rented car after the train ride. **When**（当...时） the sun sets, the canyon glows red. **Whenever**（每当） I travel, I feel most alive. I stop and ask a local **where**（在哪里） the best viewpoint is. He smiles warmly: go **wherever**（无论哪里） your heart leads. The canyon opens before me — a breathtaking welcome to the frontier.

我登上一列朝西的火车。目的地是一个以峡谷闻名的西部省份。火车向西穿行于变化的风景中。雨水使铁轨变得湿亮。透过车窗，我幻想一头鲸鱼在云中游动——天空就是那样辽阔。我不知道什么在等待着我，但我准备好迎接无论什么。小麦田一直延伸到天际。下了火车后我握着一辆租来的车的方向盘。当太阳落下时，峡谷泛出红光。每当旅行时，我感觉最鲜活。我停下来问一个当地人哪里是最好的观景点。他温暖地笑着：无论哪里，心之所向便是。峡谷在我面前展开——向边疆致以令人屏息的欢迎。

---

## Part 206 — The Widow's Garden

A **widespread**（广泛流传的） story in our town tells of a **widow**（寡妇） whose garden was magical. She was a devoted **wife**（妻子） whose husband passed away too soon. She tended the most beautiful garden in the county. **Wild**（野生的） flowers grew alongside cultivated roses. Local **wildlife**（野生动物） — rabbits, birds, even deer — found sanctuary there. She was always **willing**（乐意的） to share cuttings with neighbors. Her garden would **win**（赢得） every local competition. On **windy**（有风的） days, petals danced through the air. She made dandelion **wine**（酒） that warmed your soul. A bird with a broken **wing**（翅膀） once healed in her care. She was a **winner**（获胜者） not just of prizes, but of hearts. Every **winter**（冬天） she covered her roses with straw. She would **wipe**（擦拭） each leaf clean after a dust storm. A **wire**（铁丝） fence kept deer from eating the young shoots. The old **wise**（智慧的） gardener taught me everything I know. She worked **with**（与...一起） nature, never against it. Banks could not **withdraw**（收回） her land — it was hers forever. Peace grew **within**（在...之内） those garden walls. **Without**（没有） her, our town would be less colorful. Every gardener is a **witness**（见证者） to the miracle of life. A lone **wolf**（狼） was once spotted at the garden's edge — even the wild respected her. She was a **woman**（女人） of the soil, and the soil loved her back.

我们镇上有一个广泛流传的故事，讲的是一位寡妇，她的花园充满魔力。她是妻子——是她失去了丈夫。她照料着全县最美丽的花园。野生的花朵与栽培的玫瑰共生。当地的野生动物——兔子、鸟儿、甚至是鹿——在那里找到了庇护所。她总是乐意与邻居分享插枝。她的花园赢得每一次当地比赛。在有风的日子里，花瓣在空中飞舞。她酿的蒲公英酒能温暖你的灵魂。一只折断翅膀的鸟儿曾在她的照料下痊愈。她不仅是获奖的获胜者，更是人心的获胜者。每年冬天她用稻草覆盖玫瑰。沙尘暴后她会擦拭每一片叶子。一道铁丝围栏防止鹿吃掉嫩芽。这位年迈的智慧的园丁教会了我一切。她与自然一起工作，从不与之对抗。银行无法收回她的土地——那永远属于她。安宁在花园墙之内生长。没有她，我们的小镇会少了很多色彩。每个园丁都是生命奇迹的见证者。曾有一只狼出现在花园边缘——即便荒野也敬重她。她是一个土壤的女人，而土壤也爱她。

---

## Part 207 — The Woodworker

My grandfather works with **wood**（木料）. His hands know every grain. He shears **wool**（羊毛） from his own sheep and knits **woollen**（羊毛的） scarves. Every **word**（话） he speaks carries the weight of experience. His **work**（工作） is his meditation. As a young **worker**（工人）, he built furniture for the whole village. He says the **world**（世界） is a workshop, and we are all apprentices. His creations are **worldwide**（全世界的） now — shipped to galleries abroad. Even a tiny **worm**（蠕虫） in the timber fascinates him. His tools are **worn**（磨损的） but perfectly maintained. Each piece is **worth**（值...的） more than its price. A **worthwhile**（值得做的） life is built with patience, he says. He is a man **worthy**（值得的） of deep respect. I asked if he **would**（愿意） teach me. He handed me a chisel. A small **wound**（伤口） on his thumb tells of decades of labor. He taught me to **wrestle**（搏斗） with difficult grain patterns. Every **wrinkle**（皱纹） on his face is a story. His **wrist**（手腕） still moves with precision. He cannot **write**（写） much — his schooling was brief. But his hands speak a language all their own. In the **yard**（院子）, his latest chair catches the morning light. This **year**（年） marks his sixtieth year of craftsmanship. He taught me that the best things take time.

我的祖父与木料打交道。他的双手熟知每一道纹理。他从自己的羊身上剪下羊毛，织成羊毛的围巾。他说的每一句话都带着经验的厚重。他的工作是他的冥想。做一个年轻的工人时，他为全村做家具。他说世界就是一个工坊，我们都是学徒。他的作品现在是全世界的——被运往海外的画廊。即使木料里的一条小蠕虫也令他着迷。他的工具是磨损的，但保养得很好。每一件作品都值得比售价更高的价值。他说，值得做的人生是用耐心建造的。他是一个值得深深尊敬的人。我问他是否愿意教我。他递给我一把凿子。他拇指上的一道小伤口见证了几十年的劳作。他教我如何与难处理的木纹搏斗。他脸上的每一条皱纹都是一个故事。他的手腕依然精准地移动。他不会写太多字——他没上过多少学。但他的双手说着一种属于它们自己的语言。在院子里，他最新做的椅子沐浴着晨光。今年标志着他手艺生涯的第六十个年头。他教会我，最好的东西需要时间。

---

## Part 208 — Young at Heart

I **yell**（喊叫） with excitement — my exam results are out! A bright **yellow**（黄色的） envelope arrives. I tear it open and scream **yes**（太好了）! I got into my dream university. **Yesterday**（昨天） I was anxious. **Yet**（然而） today everything has changed. I celebrate with a cup of cold **yoghurt**（酸奶）. My mother hugs me and says: **you**（你） did it! I am still **young**（年轻的）, but I feel ready for the world. She reminds me to believe in **your**（你的） own strength — the future is **yours**（你的）. Take care of **yourself**（你自己） out there. The cake she baked is absolutely **yummy**（美味的）. I visit the **zoo**（动物园） with friends to celebrate. We see a **zebra**（斑马） and laugh at its stripes. The temperature drops to nearly **zero**（零度） by evening. I pull up the **zip**（拉链） of my jacket — the **zipper**（拉链头） is cold against my chin. We enter a special exhibition **zone**（区域） where butterflies fly freely. I use my phone to **zoom**（放大） in and capture every detail. This is just the beginning. The world is wide and waiting.

我兴奋地喊叫——考试结果出来了！一个亮黄色的信封到了。我撕开信封，大喊太好了！我考上了梦想中的大学。昨天我还在焦虑。然而今天一切都变了。我喝了一杯冰酸奶来庆祝。妈妈拥抱我，说：你做到了！我还年轻，但我感觉准备好面对世界了。她提醒我相信你自己的实力——未来是你的。在外面要照顾好你自己。她烤的蛋糕绝对美味。我和朋友们去动物园庆祝。我们看到一只斑马，笑它的条纹真有趣。傍晚气温降到接近零度。我拉上外套的拉链——拉链头冰冷地贴在下巴上。我们进入一个特殊的展览区域，那里蝴蝶自由飞舞。我用手机放大拍摄每一个细节。这仅仅是开始。世界宽广，等待着我们。

---

## Part 209 — The Choice

I sit in the library, trying to decide **whether**（是否） to study abroad. I do not know **which**（哪一个） university to choose — **whichever**（无论哪一个） I pick will change my life. I sit for a **while**（一会儿）, lost in thought. A librarian's **whisper**（低语） breaks the silence. A **white**（白色的） cat wanders past the shelves. I wonder **who**（谁） else has sat here facing the same choice. The **whole**（整个） decision feels overwhelming. I ask myself: to **whom**（谁） do I owe this decision — myself, or my family? **Whose**（谁的） dream am I really chasing? And **why**（为什么） does growing up feel so hard? The world ahead is **wide**（广阔的） with possibility. I also think about social **welfare**（福利） — maybe I could study public policy and help others. The future is uncertain, but it is mine to shape.

我坐在图书馆里，试图决定是否出国留学。我不知道该选哪一所大学——无论哪一所都会改变我的人生。我坐了一会儿，陷入沉思。一位图书管理员的低语打破了寂静。一只白色的猫从书架旁走过。我想知道谁也曾坐在这里，面对同样的选择。整个决定感觉无比沉重。我问自己：这个选择是为了谁——我自己，还是家人？我到底在追逐谁的梦想？为什么长大感觉如此艰难？前方的世界广阔而充满可能。我也想到了社会福利——也许我可以学公共政策来帮助他人。未来是不确定的，但它由我来塑造。

---
